##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/book_macro_rows.py (17992 bytes)
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""book_macro_rows.py - Amendment 9 cross-market forward-booking instrument.

Authored 2026-11-14 (session 71) after Andrea's explicit instruction and
recorded decisions: "Write a minimal, standalone booking script that checks
the Gold, Nikkei, and DAX clocks every Sunday. If a compression entry is
triggered, it appends the market, date, and entry condition to the forward
log. Direction remains refused and blank." His sketched paths
(src/book_macro_rows.py, paper/research/amend9_forward_log.csv) are realized
per the frozen pipeline layout as research/book_macro_rows.py and
data/amend9_forward_log.csv (the durable bundle is the script's home; the
V4h nightly precedent).

Governing documents: docs/amendment9_crossmarket_smh.md (SIGNED 2026-11-14),
docs/amendment9_market_addendum.md (frozen per-market kill lines, written
BEFORE any forward row), research/stage0x_stageA_report.json (frozen design
constants this script embeds and selftests against).

What this script does:
  - fetches live daily bars per market (Yahoo v8, the stage0c convention:
    sanity, repair, W-FRI aggregation) and computes sig_w = 8-week rolling
    std (ddof=1) of weekly close-to-close log returns
  - answers ONE descriptive question per market, the forward-regime read:
    is the latest completed decision week a compression or stress clock
    entry under the frozen per-market rank-transfer cuts (cross semantics
    identical to stage0x_stageA.py / smh_forecast.py)
  - on a fired clock, books exactly one append-only row per (market, arm,
    entry week) into data/amend9_forward_log.csv, direction REFUSED (blank)

What this script REFUSES to do (hard-coded, no override flag):
  - any computation on 2021+ rows beyond the live decision-edge entry check:
    no hazards, no KM, no climatology, no returns statistics, no direction
    splits; each market's 2021+ window is a fresh sealed holdout, opened one
    booked row at a time
  - any scoring: rows are scored at the n=10/20/40/60 anchors against the
    frozen kill lines by a separate future step, never here
  - any edit of a logged row: the log is append-only
  - booking without the frozen addendum present and consistent

Subcommands:
  selftest  offline: recompute design-window entry counts from the stored
            cross-market weekly CSVs (pre-2021 only) and assert equality with
            the frozen Stage A report and the addendum; no network
  status    fetch live data, describe each market's latest decision week and
            clock state, report the log standing vs the kill lines; books nothing
  book      fetch live data; for every market whose clock fired on the latest
            decision week, print the draft row(s). With --append (guarded:
            Sunday cadence, fresh entry week, not already logged, addendum
            gate) append them to data/amend9_forward_log.csv.
"""
import sys
import json
import datetime as dt
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

pd.set_option("future.no_silent_downcasting", True)

REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent          # pipeline root (bundle)
DATA = REPO / "data"
DOCS = REPO / "docs"
FORWARD_LOG = DATA / "amend9_forward_log.csv"
STAGEA_REPORT = REPO / "research" / "stage0x_stageA_report.json"
ADDENDUM = DOCS / "amendment9_market_addendum.md"
DESIGN_END = pd.Timestamp("2021-01-01")
HORIZON_KM = 12
# The Amendment 9 signature date: "forward rows begin at each market's clock
# entry on or after 2026-11-14 (signature)" (addendum Section 2, point 1).
# Decision weeks ending BEFORE this date are pre-regime and never booked.
REGIME_START = dt.date(2026, 11, 14)

# Frozen per-market constants (research/stage0x_stageA_report.json, computed
# pre-signature under Amendment 9 Section 5 Stage A; kill lines frozen in
# docs/amendment9_market_addendum.md BEFORE any forward row). Design KM
# values are bookkeeping context only, never proof.
MARKETS = {

# Frozen addendum integrity: the exact table rows that freeze each market's
# kill lines (docs/amendment9_market_addendum.md, written BEFORE any row).
ADDENDUM_ROWS = [

LOG_HEADER = [
    "logged_at_utc", "market", "symbol", "forecast_kind", "entry_week",
    "sig_w", "low_cut", "high_cut", "depth_log", "p_within_4w",
    "p_within_12w", "direction", "notes",
]


def fetch_weekly(symbol: str):
    """stage0c convention fetch + W-FRI aggregation (stage0x_crossmarket mirror)."""
    import time
    p1, p2 = 946684800, int(time.time())
    url = (f"https://query1.finance.yahoo.com/v8/finance/chart/{symbol}"
           f"?period1={p1}&period2={p2}&interval=1d&includeAdjustedClose=false")
    req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0 (research)"})
    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=60) as fh:
        payload = json.load(fh)
    res = payload["chart"]["result"][0]
    ts = res["timestamp"]
    q = res["indicators"]["quote"][0]
    day = pd.DataFrame({
        "date": pd.to_datetime(ts, unit="s", utc=True).tz_convert("America/New_York").date,
        "open": q["open"], "high": q["high"], "low": q["low"],
        "close": q["close"], "volume": q["volume"],
    })
    day["date"] = pd.to_datetime(day["date"])
    day = day.dropna(subset=["open", "high", "low", "close"]).copy()
    day = day[day[["open", "high", "low", "close"]].gt(0).all(axis=1)]
    day.loc[day["high"] < day["low"], ["high", "low"]] = np.nan
    day = day.dropna(subset=["high", "low"])
    for i, row in day.iterrows():
        if row["close"] > row["high"]:
            day.at[i, "close"] = row["high"]
        elif row["close"] < row["low"]:
            day.at[i, "close"] = row["low"]
    day = day.sort_values("date").reset_index(drop=True)
    day["week_end"] = day["date"] + pd.to_timedelta((4 - day["date"].dt.dayofweek) % 7, "D")
    wk = day.groupby("week_end").agg(
        open=("open", "first"), high=("high", "max"), low=("low", "min"),
        close=("close", "last"), volume=("volume", "sum"),
        n_sessions=("date", "count"),
    ).reset_index()
    wk["date"] = wk["week_end"]
    return wk


def with_sig(wk: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """sig_w = 8-week rolling std (ddof=1) of weekly close-to-close log returns."""
    df = wk.sort_values("date").reset_index(drop=True).copy()
    df["r1"] = np.log(df["close"] / df["close"].shift(1))
    df["sig_w"] = df["r1"].rolling(8).std(ddof=1)
    return df


def cross_state(df: pd.DataFrame, low: float, high: float):
    """Latest completed decision week's clock state (the only live read)."""
    today = pd.Timestamp(dt.date.today())
    completed = df[df["date"] <= today].reset_index(drop=True)
    if len(completed) < 10 or pd.isna(completed["sig_w"].iloc[-1]):
        return None, "no completed decision week with valid sig_w"
    t = len(completed) - 1
    sig = float(completed.loc[t, "sig_w"])
    prev = completed.loc[t - 1, "sig_w"]
    if pd.isna(prev):
        return None, f"prev week sig_w invalid at {completed.loc[t, 'date'].date()}"
    c_entry = bool(sig <= low and prev > low)
    s_entry = bool(sig > high and prev <= high)
    return {
        "entry_week": str(completed.loc[t, "date"].date()),
        "close": float(completed.loc[t, "close"]),
        "sig_w": sig, "prev_sig_w": float(prev),
        "compression_entry": c_entry, "stress_entry": s_entry,
        "region": "compression" if sig <= low else ("stress" if sig > high else "mid"),
    }, None


def build_rows(state: dict, market: str):
    cfg = MARKETS[market]
    rows = []
    for kind, fired, p0 in (
        ("compression_first_event", state["compression_entry"], cfg["km12"]),
        ("stress_de_stress", state["stress_entry"], cfg["stress_km12"]),
    ):
        if not fired:
            continue
        if kind == "compression_first_event":
            depth = float(np.log(state["sig_w"] / cfg["low"]))
            p4, p12 = cfg["km4"], cfg["km12"]
        else:
            depth = float(np.log(state["sig_w"] / cfg["high"]))
            p4, p12 = cfg["stress_km4"], cfg["stress_km12"]
        rows.append({
            "logged_at_utc": "",  # stamped at append time
            "market": market, "symbol": cfg["symbol"],
            "forecast_kind": kind, "entry_week": state["entry_week"],
            "sig_w": f"{state['sig_w']:.5f}",
            "low_cut": f"{cfg['low']:.6f}", "high_cut": f"{cfg['high']:.6f}",
            "depth_log": f"{depth:.4f}",
            "p_within_4w": f"{p4:.3f}", "p_within_12w": f"{p12:.3f}",
            "direction": "",  # REFUSED, blank, per protocol and Andrea's instruction
            "notes": ("booked at clock entry on the live decision edge; direction refused; "
                      "p_within values are frozen pre-2021 design bookkeeping, never proof; "
                      "scoring at n=10/20/40/60 vs the frozen kill lines, never here"),
        })
    return rows


def addendum_gate(problems: list):
    """Refuse booking unless the frozen addendum is present, SIGNED, and intact."""
    if not ADDENDUM.exists():
        problems.append("docs/amendment9_market_addendum.md missing (frozen kill lines required before any row)")
        return
    text = ADDENDUM.read_text()
    if "PART OF AMENDMENT 9 (SIGNED" not in text:
        problems.append("addendum does not carry its SIGNED status marker")
    for row in ADDENDUM_ROWS:
        if row not in text:
            problems.append(f"addendum integrity: frozen table row missing/modified: {row}")


def log_rows() -> pd.DataFrame:
    if not FORWARD_LOG.exists():
        return pd.DataFrame(columns=LOG_HEADER)
    return pd.read_csv(FORWARD_LOG, dtype=str)


def cmd_selftest() -> int:
    """Offline parity: embedded constants == Stage A report == addendum; design entry counts recompute."""
    problems = []
    report = json.loads(STAGEA_REPORT.read_text())["markets"]
    text = ADDENDUM.read_text() if ADDENDUM.exists() else ""
    if "PART OF AMENDMENT 9 (SIGNED" not in text:
        problems.append("addendum SIGNED marker missing")
    for row in ADDENDUM_ROWS:
        if row not in text:
            problems.append(f"addendum integrity: frozen table row missing/modified: {row}")
    for m, cfg in MARKETS.items():
        rep = report[m]
        if abs(rep["rank_cuts"]["low"] - cfg["low"]) > 1e-6:
            problems.append(f"{m} low cut drift vs report")
        if abs(rep["rank_cuts"]["high"] - cfg["high"]) > 1e-6:
            problems.append(f"{m} high cut drift vs report")
        for key, emb in (("compression_kill_lines", cfg["kill_lines"]),
                         ("stress_kill_lines", cfg["stress_kill_lines"])):
            if {int(k): v for k, v in rep[key].items()} != emb:
                problems.append(f"{m} {key} drift vs report")
        csv = DATA / f"crossmarket_{m.lower()}_weekly.csv"
        if not csv.exists():
            problems.append(f"{m} stored weekly CSV missing")
            continue
        df = with_sig(pd.read_csv(csv, parse_dates=["date"]))
        pre = df[df["date"] < DESIGN_END].reset_index(drop=True)
        inside = pre["sig_w"] <= cfg["low"]
        prev = inside.shift(1).fillna(False).astype(bool)
        cidx = pre.index[inside & (~prev) & pre["sig_w"].notna()]
        inside_s = pre["sig_w"] > cfg["high"]
        prev_s = inside_s.shift(1).fillna(False).astype(bool)
        sidx = pre.index[inside_s & (~prev_s) & pre["sig_w"].notna()]
        if len(cidx) != cfg["design_entries"]:
            problems.append(f"{m} compression entries {len(cidx)} != {cfg['design_entries']}")
        if len(sidx) != cfg["design_stress_entries"]:
            problems.append(f"{m} stress entries {len(sidx)} != {cfg['design_stress_entries']}")
    if problems:
        print("SELFTEST FAILED:")
        for p in problems:
            print(f"  - {p}")
        return 1
    print("SELFTEST PASS: embedded cuts/kill lines == Stage A report == addendum; "
          "design-window entry counts recompute exactly (GOLD 47/58, NIKKEI 48/55, DAX 50/49).")
    return 0


def cmd_status() -> int:
    existing = log_rows()
    print(f"Amendment 9 forward log: {len(existing)} booked row(s); "
          f"kill lines frozen per market (addendum); direction refused everywhere.")
    for m, cfg in MARKETS.items():
        try:
            wk = fetch_weekly(cfg["symbol"])
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 - report fetch failure, book nothing
            print(f"{m}: FETCH FAILED ({e}); nothing booked, next run catches up")
            continue
        df = with_sig(wk)
        state, err = cross_state(df, cfg["low"], cfg["high"])
        if state is None:
            print(f"{m}: {err}")
            continue
        print(f"{m} ({cfg['symbol']}): decision week {state['entry_week']}  "
              f"close={state['close']:.2f}  sig_w={state['sig_w']:.5f} "
              f"(prev {state['prev_sig_w']:.5f})  region={state['region'].upper()}")
        fired = state["compression_entry"] or state["stress_entry"]
        print(f"  compression ENTRY: {'YES' if state['compression_entry'] else 'NO'}   "
              f"stress ENTRY: {'YES' if state['stress_entry'] else 'NO'}"
              + ("   draft rows: book" if fired else "   no row (the protocol logs clock entries, not noise)"))
        if len(existing):
            have = existing[(existing["market"] == m) & (existing["forecast_kind"] == "compression_first_event")]
            print(f"  log standing: {len(have)} compression row(s) booked; kill lines "
                  f"{cfg['kill_lines']} (n=10/20/40/60)")
    return 0


def cmd_book(append: bool) -> int:
    today = dt.date.today()
    all_rows, states = [], []
    for m, cfg in MARKETS.items():
        try:
            wk = fetch_weekly(cfg["symbol"])
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            print(f"{m}: FETCH FAILED ({e}); nothing booked for this market")
            continue
        df = with_sig(wk)
        state, err = cross_state(df, cfg["low"], cfg["high"])
        if state is None:
            print(f"{m}: {err}")
            continue
        states.append((m, state))
        all_rows.extend(build_rows(state, m))
    if not all_rows:
        desc = "; ".join(
            f"{m} sig_w={s['sig_w']:.5f} region={s['region']}" for m, s in states
        ) or "no states"
        print(f"NO CLOCK ENTRY on any market's latest decision week: ({desc})")
        print("the protocol logs clock entries, not noise; nothing drafted")
        return 0
    stamp = dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")
    for r in all_rows:
        r["logged_at_utc"] = stamp
        print("## Amendment 9 forward row (draft)")
        print(json.dumps(r, indent=2))
    if not append:
        print("draft only; append on the Sunday cadence with: book --append")
        return 0
    existing = log_rows()
    guards = []
    if today.weekday() != 6:
        guards.append(f"not Sunday (today {today.isoformat()} is {today.strftime('%A')})")
    addendum_gate(guards)
    for r in all_rows:
        entry_date = dt.date.fromisoformat(r["entry_week"])
        if entry_date < REGIME_START:
            guards.append(f"entry week {r['entry_week']} precedes the regime start "
                          f"{REGIME_START.isoformat()} (pre-signature entries are never booked)")
        if (today - entry_date).days > 7:
            guards.append(f"entry week {r['entry_week']} not fresh")
        if len(existing) and ((existing["market"] == r["market"]) &
                              (existing["forecast_kind"] == r["forecast_kind"]) &
                              (existing["entry_week"] == r["entry_week"])).any():
            guards.append(f"{r['market']} {r['forecast_kind']} {r['entry_week']} already logged (append-only)")
    if guards:
        print("REFUSED --append: " + "; ".join(guards))
        return 1
    newlog = pd.DataFrame(all_rows, columns=LOG_HEADER)
    out = pd.concat([existing, newlog], ignore_index=True) if len(existing) else newlog
    out.to_csv(FORWARD_LOG, index=False)
    print(f"APPENDED {len(all_rows)} row(s) to data/amend9_forward_log.csv "
          f"(entries: {', '.join(r['market'] + ' ' + r['entry_week'] for r in all_rows)})")
    return 0


def main() -> int:
    args = [a for a in sys.argv[1:] if not a.startswith("-")]
    append = "--append" in sys.argv
    if not args:
        print(__doc__)
        return 1
    if args[0] == "selftest":
        return cmd_selftest()
    if args[0] == "status":
        return cmd_status()
    if args[0] == "book":
        return cmd_book(append)
    print(f"unknown subcommand: {args[0]}")
    return 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/book_macro_rows.py

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/c1l2_dryrun.py (34920 bytes)
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""C1-L2 PIPELINE DRY RUN - UNREGISTERED, NON-INFERENTIAL TOOLING TEST.

Executes the transition rules and kill-line machinery of
src/c1_l2_test.py + src/cox_diagnostics.py (repo vp-mr-strategy, commit
01732bd5) on gate-clean 2026-10-01 binance-futures BTCUSDT Tardis data,
stdlib only (no pandas/numpy/scipy/lifelines in this sandbox).

NOT A RESEARCH RESULT. No verdict on C1-L2 is drawn or implied. The dry run
proves: parsing, the BBBA event clock, the compression clock, trade-touch
triple-barrier labels, purged CPCV with embargo, the leakage assert, the
counting-process Cox, the LR test, powered-fold machinery, and the five
pre-declared kill lines all EXECUTE. Values are machinery outputs.

DRY-RUN ADAPTATIONS, all binding labels, none inferential:
  D1  ONE day of data (2026-10-01). The spec wants 2-4 weeks; the trial
      quota died (session 81). Day 2026-10-10 is not re-downloadable.
  D2  The primary spec (H=24h, 5-day percentile pool) CANNOT be supported
      by a 1-day window: the pool is degenerate (same-HOD trailing obs
      within the day, min pool 3) and H=24h purged CV purges every train
      row. The dry run therefore executes the primary configuration up to
      the purge stage (proving the purge is correct), then exercises the
      full chain under a SECONDARY dry-run configuration H=2h (k=1.0
      unchanged, per the spec's secondary-spec pattern).
  D3  sigma_H proxy: trailing 240 x 1-minute mid returns std x sqrt(1440)
      (the repo estimates it from hourly returns over the 24h horizon;
      one day cannot form that window - labeled proxy).
  D4  Compression clock is computed on the full BBBA event series;
      sigma/spread grids are 1-minute. Trade-touch labels use the FULL
      trade print series (exact rule).
  D5  Kill-line-5 bootstrap reduced to 10 reps on the 1-minute grid
      (spec: 100 reps on the full series). Band from 10 reps is crude -
      machinery proof only.
  D6  Non-compression arm: the spec emits decision rows at compression
      transitions + 1h refreshes; the Cox needs a both-arm risk set, so
      the dry run also emits hourly non-compression anchor rows
      (interpretation, labeled).
"""
import bisect, math, sys, time
from collections import deque

RAW = "/home/user/l2/raw"
OUTDIR = "/home/user/knowledge/trading-research/pipeline/data/c1l2_dryrun"
DAY_START_US = 1790812800 * 10**6
DAY_END_US = DAY_START_US + 86400 * 10**6
K = 1.0
PCT_THRESHOLD = 33.0
BARRIER_MIN_SPREADS = 10.0
MAX_FLOOR_BINDING = 0.05
W_COMP = 4 * 3600 * 10**6
SIG_WIN_MIN = 240
SIG_SCALE = math.sqrt(1440)
HOD_BUCKETS = 6
MIN_POOL = 3
KILL1_C = 0.52
KILL3_FRAC = 0.30
POWER_MIN_EVENTS = 50
BOOT_REPS = 10
H_PRIMARY = 24 * 3600 * 10**6
H_DRY = 2 * 3600 * 10**6

def log(*a):
    print(*a, flush=True)

def line(fname):
    with open(fname) as f:
        for ln in f:
            yield ln

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- loading
def load_bbba():
    ts, mid, spread = [], [], []
    for ln in line(f"{RAW}/bbba_2026-10-01.tsv"):
        a = ln.split("\t")
        t = int(a[0]); b = float(a[1]); ask = float(a[2])
        ts.append(t); mid.append((b + ask) / 2.0); spread.append(ask - b)
    return ts, mid, spread

def load_trades():
    ts, px, sells = [], [], 0
    for ln in line(f"{RAW}/trades_2026-10-01.tsv"):
        a = ln.rstrip("\n").split("\t")
        ts.append(int(a[0])); px.append(float(a[1]))
        if a[2] == "sell": sells += 1
    return ts, px, sells

# ------------------------------------------------------- minute grids
def minute_grid(ts, mid, spread):
    nmin = 1440
    gmid = [None]*nmin; gspread = [None]*nmin
    i = 0; n = len(ts)
    for m in range(nmin):
        t0 = DAY_START_US + m*60*10**6
        t1 = t0 + 60*10**6
        while i < n and ts[i] < t1:
            gmid[m] = mid[i]; gspread[m] = spread[i]
            i += 1
    return gmid, gspread

def sigma_grid(gmid):
    """sigma_H per minute: std of trailing 240 1-min returns x sqrt(1440),
    trailing only (window ends at previous minute)."""
    n = len(gmid)
    rets = [None]*n
    for m in range(1, n):
        a, b = gmid[m-1], gmid[m]
        if a and b:
            rets[m] = b/a - 1.0
    sig = [None]*n
    p1 = [0.0]*(n+1); p2 = [0.0]*(n+1); cnt = [0]*(n+1)
    for m in range(n):
        p1[m+1] = p1[m] + (rets[m] if rets[m] is not None else 0.0)
        p2[m+1] = p2[m] + (rets[m]**2 if rets[m] is not None else 0.0)
        cnt[m+1] = cnt[m] + (1 if rets[m] is not None else 0)
    for m in range(n):
        lo = max(0, m - SIG_WIN_MIN); hi = m  # window ends at m-1
        c = cnt[hi] - cnt[lo]
        if c < 30:
            continue
        s1 = p1[hi] - p1[lo]; s2 = p2[hi] - p2[lo]
        var = max(s2/c - (s1/c)**2, 0.0)
        sig[m] = math.sqrt(var) * SIG_SCALE
    return sig, rets

def spread_median_grid(gspread):
    n = len(gspread)
    med = [None]*n
    for m in range(n):
        lo = max(0, m - SIG_WIN_MIN + 1)
        vals = sorted(v for v in gspread[lo:m+1] if v is not None)
        if len(vals) >= 30:
            med[m] = vals[len(vals)//2]
    return med

def minute_of(ts_us):
    return (ts_us - DAY_START_US) // (60*10**6)

# --------------------------------------------- compression clock on BBBA
def compression_clock(ts, mid):
    """Trailing 4h realized range of BBBA mid in sigma_H units, percentile vs
    same-HOD trailing pool (D2 degenerate), pct<=33 -> compression."""
    n = len(ts)
    maxq = deque(); minq = deque()  # (idx)
    comp = [None]*n; range_units = [None]*n
    pools = [[] for _ in range(HOD_BUCKETS)]
    for i in range(n):
        while maxq and mid[maxq[-1]] <= mid[i]: maxq.pop()
        maxq.append(i)
        while minq and mid[minq[-1]] >= mid[i]: minq.pop()
        minq.append(i)
        t = ts[i]
        while ts[maxq[0]] < t - W_COMP: maxq.popleft()
        while ts[minq[0]] < t - W_COMP: minq.popleft()
        rng = mid[maxq[0]] - mid[minq[0]]
        m = minute_of(t)
        comp[i] = None; range_units[i] = None
        continue_ = True
        # defer sigma lookup per minute below in main pass
        range_units[i] = rng
    return range_units

# ------------------------------------------------------ decision rows
def build_decision_rows(ts, mid, spread, sig, med):
    n = len(ts)
    ru_raw = compression_clock(ts, mid)
    m = 0
    hod_bucket = [0]*n; pct = [None]*n; comp = [None]*n
    pools = [[] for _ in range(HOD_BUCKETS)]
    for i in range(n):
        mi = minute_of(ts[i])
        s = sig[mi]
        ru_raw[i] = ru_raw[i]
        if s is None or s <= 0 or mid[i] <= 0:
            comp[i] = None; continue
        ru = (ru_raw[i] / mid[i]) / s
        b = (ts[i] // (4*3600*10**6)) % HOD_BUCKETS
        hod_bucket[i] = b
        pool = pools[b]
        if len(pool) >= MIN_POOL:
            p = 100.0 * sum(1 for v in pool if v <= ru) / len(pool)
            pct[i] = p
            comp[i] = 1 if p <= PCT_THRESHOLD else 0
        pool.append(ru)
    rows = []
    comp_start = None
    n_bbba_events = 0
    next_boundary = DAY_START_US + 3600*10**6
    emitted_boundary = None
    for i in range(n):
        t = ts[i]
        if comp[i] is None:
            continue
        # transitions
        prev = None
        j = i - 1
        while j >= 0 and comp[j] is None:
            j -= 1
        if j >= 0:
            prev = comp[j]
        if prev == 0 and comp[i] == 1:
            comp_start = i
            rows.append(dict(kind="entry", i=i, t0=t, compression=1,
                             time_in_comp=0, sigma=sig[minute_of(t)],
                             spread_med=med[minute_of(t)], p0=mid[i],
                             hod=hod_bucket[i]))
        if prev == 1 and comp[i] == 0:
            comp_start = None
        # hourly anchor rows (refresh in compression; D6 both-arm)
        while next_boundary <= t and next_boundary < DAY_END_US:
            if emitted_boundary != next_boundary:
                emitted_boundary = next_boundary
                tic = 1 if comp[i] == 1 else 0
                if comp_start is not None and tic == 1:
                    tic_count = i - comp_start
                else:
                    tic_count = 0
                rows.append(dict(kind="hour", i=i, t0=t, compression=tic,
                                 time_in_comp=tic_count,
                                 sigma=sig[minute_of(t)],
                                 spread_med=med[minute_of(t)], p0=mid[i],
                                 hod=hod_bucket[i]))
            next_boundary += 3600*10**6
    return rows, comp, pct

# ------------------------------------------------------------- labels
def label_row(row, H, tr_ts, tr_px, gmid):
    t0 = row["t0"]; sigma = row["sigma"]; p0 = row["p0"]
    if sigma is None or row["spread_med"] is None:
        return None
    # sigma_H is horizon-scaled: the sigma grid is 24h-scaled (D3), so the
    # barrier for horizon H uses sigma * sqrt(H/24h)
    sigma_scale = math.sqrt(H / (24*3600*10**6))
    barrier = K * sigma * sigma_scale * p0
    floor = BARRIER_MIN_SPREADS * row["spread_med"]
    floor_binding = barrier < floor
    barrier = max(barrier, floor)
    up, dn = p0 + barrier, p0 - barrier
    tend = min(t0 + H, DAY_END_US)
    j0 = bisect.bisect_right(tr_ts, t0)
    ev = 0; dur = (tend - t0) / (3600*10**6); touch_ts = None
    j = j0
    while j < len(tr_ts) and tr_ts[j] <= tend:
        if tr_px[j] >= up or tr_px[j] <= dn:
            ev = 1; touch_ts = tr_ts[j]
            dur = (tr_ts[j] - t0) / (3600*10**6)
            break
        j += 1
    # quote-touch diagnostic: grid mid crosses barrier before touch
    m0 = minute_of(t0); m1 = min(minute_of(tend), 1439)
    qt = 0
    for m in range(m0, m1+1):
        if gmid[m] is None: continue
        if gmid[m] >= up or gmid[m] <= dn:
            qt = 1
            break
    row.update(event=ev, duration_h=dur, floor_binding=floor_binding,
               barrier=barrier, quote_touch=qt, tend=tend)
    return row

def to_long_format(rows, H):
    """Split each spell into 1h (start, stop] intervals; event on the split
    containing the touch. Covariates fixed at decision values (D-label)."""
    out = []
    for r in rows:
        if r.get("event") is None:
            continue
        t0 = r["t0"]; touch = r["t0"] + r["duration_h"]*3600*10**6
        end = min(t0 + H, DAY_END_US)
        s = t0
        while s < end - 1:
            nxt = min(s + 3600*10**6, end)
            ev = 1 if (r["event"] == 1 and touch > s and touch <= nxt) else 0
            out.append(dict(start=(s - DAY_START_US)/(3600*10**6),
                            stop=(nxt - DAY_START_US)/(3600*10**6),
                            event=ev, compression=r["compression"],
                            time_in_comp=math.log1p(r["time_in_comp"]),
                            sigma=r["sigma"], hod=r["hod"]))
            s = nxt
    return out

# --------------------------------------------------------------- Cox
def cox_fit(rows, covs, strata=("hod",), max_iter=100, tol=1e-9):
    p = len(covs)
    data = []
    for r in rows:
        data.append((r["start"], r["stop"], r["event"],
                     [r[c] for c in covs], r[strata[0]] if strata else 0))
    def accumulate(beta):
        ll = 0.0; grad = [0.0]*p; info = [[0.0]*p for _ in range(p)]
        strata_map = {}
        for d in data: strata_map.setdefault(d[4], []).append(d)
        for s, ds in strata_map.items():
            ev_times = sorted(set(d[1] for d in ds if d[2] == 1))
            for t in ev_times:
                risk = [d for d in ds if d[0] < t and d[1] >= t]
                if not risk: continue
                ws = [math.exp(sum(b*x for b, x in zip(beta, d[3]))) for d in risk]
                S0 = sum(ws)
                if S0 <= 0: raise FloatingPointError("degenerate risk set")
                S1 = [sum(w*x[k] for w, x in zip(ws, (d[3] for d in risk))) for k in range(p)]
                deaths = [d for d in ds if d[1] == t and d[2] == 1]
                dd = len(deaths)
                ll += sum(math.exp(sum(b*x for b, x in zip(beta, d[3]))) for d in deaths) - dd*math.log(S0)
                for k in range(p):
                    grad[k] += sum(d[3][k] for d in deaths) - dd*S1[k]/S0
                # S2
                for a in range(p):
                    for b_ in range(p):
                        S2 = sum(w*x[a]*x[b_] for w, x in zip(ws, (d[3] for d in risk)))
                        info[a][b_] += dd * (S2/S0 - S1[a]*S1[b_]/(S0*S0))
        return ll, grad, info
    beta = [0.0]*p
    ll, _, _ = accumulate(beta)
    for _ in range(max_iter):
        ll, grad, info = accumulate(beta)
        step = solve(info, grad)
        t = 1.0
        while t > 1e-8:
            cand = [b + t*st for b, st in zip(beta, step)]
            llc, _, _ = accumulate(cand)
            if llc >= ll - 1e-12: break
            t /= 2.0
        beta = [b + t*st for b, st in zip(beta, step)]
        if max(abs(t*st) for st in step) < tol: break
    ll, grad, info = accumulate(beta)
    cov = solve(info, [[1.0 if i == j else 0.0 for j in range(p)] for i in range(p)])
    cov = [cov[i][:] for i in range(p)]  # inverse via solve( info, I )
    return dict(beta=beta, cov=cov, covs=list(covs), ll=ll,
                n_events=sum(d[2] for d in data))

def solve(A, b):
    n = len(A)
    M = [row[:] + [b[i] if not isinstance(b[0], list) else b[i][j] for j in range(len(b[0]))] if isinstance(b[0], list) else row[:] + [b[i]] for i, row in enumerate(A)]
    if isinstance(b[0], list):
        return gauss_multi(A, b)
    return gauss_single(A, b)

def gauss_single(A, b):
    n = len(A)
    M = [A[i][:] + [b[i]] for i in range(n)]
    for c in range(n):
        piv = max(range(c, n), key=lambda r: abs(M[r][c]))
        if abs(M[piv][c]) < 1e-300: 
            M[c][c] = 1e-300
        M[c], M[piv] = M[piv], M[c]
        for r in range(n):
            if r != c and abs(M[c][c]) > 0:
                f = M[r][c]/M[c][c]
                for k in range(c, n+1):
                    M[r][k] -= f*M[c][k]
    return [M[i][n]/M[i][i] for i in range(n)]

def gauss_multi(A, B):
    n = len(A); m = len(B[0])
    M = [A[i][:] + B[i][:] for i in range(n)]
    for c in range(n):
        piv = max(range(c, n), key=lambda r: abs(M[r][c]))
        if abs(M[piv][c]) < 1e-300: M[c][c] = 1e-300
        M[c], M[piv] = M[piv], M[c]
        for r in range(n):
            if r != c:
                f = M[r][c]/M[c][c]
                if f != 0.0:
                    for k in range(c, n+m):
                        M[r][k] -= f*M[c][k]
    return [[M[i][n+j]/M[i][i] for j in range(m)] for i in range(n)]

def coef_table(fit):
    rows = []
    for j, name in enumerate(fit["covs"]):
        se = math.sqrt(max(fit["cov"][j][j], 0.0))
        b = fit["beta"][j]
        z = b/se if se > 0 else float("nan")
        p = math.erfc(abs(z)/math.sqrt(2)) if se > 0 else float("nan")
        def _e(x):
            return math.exp(min(max(x, -700), 700))
        rows.append((name, b, se, _e(b), _e(b-1.96*se),
                     _e(b+1.96*se), p))
    return rows

def lr_test(rows, covs):
    fit = cox_fit(rows, covs)
    fit0 = cox_fit(rows, covs)  # placeholder; ll0 computed below
    # ll0: accumulate at beta=0
    p = len(covs)
    ll0 = cox_ll_at(rows, covs, [0.0]*p)
    chi2 = 2.0*(fit["ll"] - ll0)
    pv = chi2_sf(chi2, p)
    return chi2, pv, fit

def cox_ll_at(rows, covs, beta):
    # quick loglik only
    p = len(covs)
    data = [(r["start"], r["stop"], r["event"], [r[c] for c in covs], r["hod"]) for r in rows]
    ll = 0.0
    strata_map = {}
    for d in data: strata_map.setdefault(d[4], []).append(d)
    for s, ds in strata_map.items():
        for t in sorted(set(d[1] for d in ds if d[2] == 1)):
            risk = [d for d in ds if d[0] < t and d[1] >= t]
            if not risk: continue
            ws = [math.exp(sum(b*x for b, x in zip(beta, d[3]))) for d in risk]
            S0 = sum(ws)
            deaths = [d for d in ds if d[1] == t and d[2] == 1]
            ll += sum(math.exp(sum(b*x for b, x in zip(beta, d[3]))) for d in deaths) - len(deaths)*math.log(S0)
    return ll

def chi2_sf(x, df):
    """Upper-tail chi2 via regularized incomplete gamma Q(df/2, x/2)."""
    if x <= 0: return 1.0
    a = df/2.0; xx = x/2.0
    if xx < a + 1.0:
        # series
        term = 1.0/a; ssum = term; n_ = a; 
        for _ in range(200):
            n_ += 1.0
            term *= xx/n_
            ssum += term
            if abs(term) < abs(ssum)*1e-14: break
        P = ssum*math.exp(-xx + a*math.log(xx) - math.lgamma(a))
        return max(0.0, 1.0 - P)
    else:
        # continued fraction for Q
        tiny = 1e-300
        b_ = xx + 1.0 - a; c_ = 1.0/tiny; d_ = 1.0/b_; h = d_
        for i in range(1, 200):
            an = -i*(i-a)
            b_ += 2.0
            d_ = an*d_ + b_
            if abs(d_) < tiny: d_ = tiny
            c_ = b_ + an/c_
            if abs(c_) < tiny: c_ = tiny
            d_ = 1.0/d_
            de = d_*c_; h *= de
            if abs(de-1.0) < 1e-14: break
        Q = math.exp(-xx + a*math.log(xx) - math.lgamma(a))*h
        return min(1.0, max(0.0, Q))

def concordance(rows, covs, fit):
    eta = []
    for r in rows:
        eta.append(sum(b*r[c] for b, c in zip(fit["beta"], covs)))
    num = den = 0.0
    bystrat = {}
    for i, r in enumerate(rows): bystrat.setdefault(r["hod"], []).append(i)
    for s, idxs in bystrat.items():
        ev = [i for i in idxs if rows[i]["event"] == 1]
        for i in ev:
            for j in idxs:
                if rows[j]["stop"] > rows[i]["stop"]:
                    den += 1
                    if eta[j] < eta[i]: num += 1
                    elif eta[j] == eta[i]: num += 0.5
    return num/den if den else float("nan")

# -------------------------------------------------------- purged CPCV
def purged_splits(row_ts, n_groups=6, label_h=H_DRY, embargo=H_DRY):
    n = len(row_ts)
    edges = [int(n*g/n_groups) for g in range(n_groups+1)]
    gids = [0]*n
    for g in range(n_groups):
        for i in range(edges[g], edges[g+1]): gids[i] = g
    splits = []
    from itertools import combinations
    for tg in combinations(range(n_groups), 1):
        tm = [i for i in range(n) if gids[i] in tg]
        lo = row_ts[tm[0]]; hi = row_ts[tm[-1]]
        win_lo = lo - embargo; win_hi = hi + label_h + embargo
        train = []
        purged = 0
        for i in range(n):
            if gids[i] in tg: continue
            if (row_ts[i] + label_h >= win_lo) and (row_ts[i] <= win_hi):
                purged += 1
            else:
                train.append(i)
        splits.append(dict(test=tm, train=train, purged=purged,
                            bounds=(lo, hi)))
    return splits

def leakage_assert(split, row_ts, label_h, embargo):
    lo, hi = split["bounds"]
    bad = 0
    for i in split["train"]:
        s = row_ts[i]
        if (s + label_h >= lo - embargo) and (s <= hi + label_h + embargo):
            bad += 1
    return bad

# ------------------------------------------------------------- main
def main():
    T0 = time.time()
    log("=== C1-L2 PIPELINE DRY RUN (unregistered, non-inferential) ===")
    log("data: 2026-10-01 binance-futures BTCUSDT (gate-clean, session 81)")
    ts, mid, spread = load_bbba()
    log(f"stage1 bbba rows: {len(ts)}")
    gmid, gspread = minute_grid(ts, mid, spread)
    sig, rets = sigma_grid(gmid)
    med = spread_median_grid(gspread)
    log(f"stage2 grids: minutes={len(gmid)}, sigma defined={sum(1 for v in sig if v)}, spread_med defined={sum(1 for v in med if v)}")
    tr_ts, tr_px, sells = load_trades()
    log(f"stage3 trades: {len(tr_ts)} (sell aggressor {sells})")

    # ---- primary spec (H=24h): decision rows, labels, purge demonstration
    rows, comp, pct = build_decision_rows(ts, mid, spread, sig, med)
    known = sum(1 for c in comp if c is not None)
    ncomp = sum(1 for c in comp if c == 1)
    log(f"stage4 clock: bbba events with known compression state={known}, compression events={ncomp}, decision rows={len(rows)} (entries={sum(1 for r in rows if r['kind']=='entry')}, hourly anchors={sum(1 for r in rows if r['kind']=='hour')})")
    rows_p = [r for r in rows]
    for r in rows_p:
        label_row(r, H_PRIMARY, tr_ts, tr_px, gmid)
    okp = [r for r in rows_p if r.get("event") is not None]
    evp = sum(1 for r in okp if r["event"] == 1)
    fb = sum(1 for r in okp if r["floor_binding"])
    qt = sum(1 for r in okp if r["quote_touch"])
    log(f"stage5 labels H=24h: spells={len(okp)}, events={evp}, censored={len(okp)-evp}, floor-binding={fb} ({fb/max(len(okp),1):.1%}), quote-touch-before-trade={qt}")
    log(f"  H=24h on a 1-day window: entries late in the day are censored at day end by construction; durations top out at {(min(r['duration_h'] for r in okp)):.2f}..{max(r['duration_h'] for r in okp):.2f}h")
    row_ts_sorted = sorted(r["t0"] for r in okp)
    sp_p = purged_splits(row_ts_sorted, 6, H_PRIMARY, H_PRIMARY)
    for k_, s_ in enumerate(sp_p):
        v = leakage_assert(s_, row_ts_sorted, H_PRIMARY, H_PRIMARY)
        log(f"stage6 purge H=24h fold{k_}: test rows={len(s_['test'])}, purged={s_['purged']}, train rows={len(s_['train'])}, leakage violations={v}")
    log("  -> PRIMARY SPEC CANNOT PROCEED ON 1 DAY: H=24h purge empties every train set (correct machinery behavior; the spec needs 2-4 weeks).")

    # ---- secondary dry-run configuration H=2h: full chain
    log("")
    log("=== SECONDARY DRY-RUN CONFIGURATION H=2h (labeled, machinery exercise) ===")
    rows2 = []
    for r in rows:
        r2 = dict(r)
        if label_row(r2, H_DRY, tr_ts, tr_px, gmid) is not None:
            rows2.append(r2)
    ev2 = sum(1 for r in rows2 if r["event"] == 1)
    fb2 = sum(1 for r in rows2 if r["floor_binding"])
    log(f"stage5b labels H=2h: spells={len(rows2)}, events={ev2}, floor-binding={fb2} ({fb2/max(len(rows2),1):.1%})")
    if fb2 / max(len(rows2), 1) > MAX_FLOOR_BINDING:
        log("  FLOOR-BINDING STOP: >5% of spells floor-bound; spec says stop this spec (machinery executed correctly)")
    long_rows = to_long_format(rows2, H_DRY)
    log(f"stage7 long format rows: {len(long_rows)}")
    row_ts2 = sorted(r["t0"] for r in rows2)
    splits = purged_splits(row_ts2, 6, H_DRY, H_DRY)
    covs3 = ["compression", "time_in_comp", "sigma"]
    # OOF concordance
    oof_num = oof_den = 0.0
    per_fold = []
    for k_, s_ in enumerate(splits):
        v = leakage_assert(s_, row_ts2, H_DRY, H_DRY)
        train_rows = [long_rows[i] if False else None for i in []]  # placeholder
        # map: rebuild long rows per fold from decision-row indices
        tr_rows = []
        for i in s_["train"]:
            r = rows2[i]
            tr_rows.extend(long_format_for(r))
        te_rows = []
        for i in s_["test"]:
            r = rows2[i]
            te_rows.extend(long_format_for(r))
        try:
            fit_tr = cox_fit(tr_rows, covs3)
            c = concordance(te_rows, covs3, fit_tr) if te_rows else float("nan")
        except Exception as ex:
            c = float("nan"); log(f"  fold{k_} fit failed: {ex}")
        nev = sum(1 for r in te_rows if r["event"] == 1)
        # fold-level LR on the FOLD's rows (train side, per repo fold logic)
        try:
            chi2, pv, _ = lr_test(tr_rows, covs3)
        except Exception as ex:
            chi2, pv = float("nan"), float("nan")
        per_fold.append(dict(fold=k_, n_test=len(s_["test"]), n_purged=s_["purged"],
                              n_train_rows=len(tr_rows), test_events=nev,
                              leakage=v, c=c, lr_chi2=chi2, lr_p=pv))
        log(f"stage8 fold{k_}: test={len(s_['test'])}, purged={s_['purged']}, train={len(tr_rows)}, test_events={nev}, leakage={v}, c={c}, LR chi2={chi2:.3f} p={pv:.4f}")
    # pooled OOF concordance (event-weighted mean)
    cs = [(f["c"], f["test_events"]) for f in per_fold if f["c"] == f["c"] and f["test_events"] > 0]
    pooled_c = (sum(c*e for c, e in cs)/sum(e for c, e in cs)) if cs and sum(e for c, e in cs) > 0 else float("nan")

    # full-sample fits for kill lines
    fit3 = cox_fit(long_rows, covs3)
    ct3 = coef_table(fit3)
    covs2 = ["compression", "sigma"]
    fit2 = cox_fit(long_rows, covs2)
    ct2 = coef_table(fit2)
    log(f"stage9 full-sample Cox (compression+time+sigma, HOD strata): ll={fit3['ll']:.2f}, events={fit3['n_events']}")
    for name, b, se, hr, lo, hi, p in ct3:
        log(f"    {name}: beta={b:+.4f} se={se:.4f} HR={hr:.3f} [{lo:.3f},{hi:.3f}] p={p:.4f}")
    log(f"stage9b vol-tautology model (compression+sigma): ll={fit2['ll']:.2f}")
    for name, b, se, hr, lo, hi, p in ct2:
        log(f"    {name}: beta={b:+.4f} se={se:.4f} HR={hr:.3f} [{lo:.3f},{hi:.3f}] p={p:.4f}")

    # ---- kill lines
    log("")
    log("=== KILL LINES (machinery execution; NOT a research verdict) ===")
    kills = {}
    kills[1] = dict(value=pooled_c, threshold=KILL1_C,
                    fired=(pooled_c == pooled_c and pooled_c <= KILL1_C),
                    detail=f"pooled OOF concordance={pooled_c:.4f} (kill if <= {KILL1_C})")
    hr2 = [r for r in ct2 if r[0] == "compression"][0]
    kills[2] = dict(value=(hr2[4], hr2[5]), threshold="CI excludes 1.0",
                    fired=(hr2[4] <= 1.0 <= hr2[5]),
                    detail=f"compression HR CI [{hr2[4]:.3f},{hr2[5]:.3f}] (compression+sigma model)")
    powered = [f for f in per_fold if False]  # fold events from TRAIN rows
    fold_events = []
    for f in per_fold:
        fold_events.append(dict(fold=f["fold"], events=f["test_events"], lr_p=f["lr_p"]))
    merged = filter_powered_folds(fold_events)
    powered_folds = [f for f in merged if f["powered"] and f["lr_p"] == f["lr_p"]]
    if powered_folds:
        frac = sum(1 for f in powered_folds if f["lr_p"] > 0.05)/len(powered_folds)
        kills[3] = dict(value=frac, threshold=0.30, fired=(frac >= 0.30),
                        detail=f"unrejected powered fraction={frac:.2f} among {len(powered_folds)} powered folds")
    else:
        kills[3] = dict(value=None, threshold=0.30, fired=False,
                        detail="NO POWERED FOLDS (all < 50 events; merges insufficient on 1 day) - kill line 3 not evaluable, machinery correctly reports insufficient power")
    hr4 = [r for r in ct3 if r[0] == "compression"][0]
    kills[4] = dict(value=(hr4[4], hr4[5]), threshold="CI excludes 1.0",
                    fired=(hr4[4] <= 1.0 <= hr4[5]),
                    detail=f"compression HR CI [{hr4[4]:.3f},{hr4[5]:.3f}] under HOD stratification (compression+time+sigma)")
    for k_ in (1, 2, 3, 4):
        log(f"KILL LINE {k_}: {'FIRED' if kills[k_]['fired'] else 'not fired'} - {kills[k_]['detail']}")

    # ---- kill line 5: standardized-mid block bootstrap (reduced reps D5)
    obs_gap = abs(math.log(hr2[3])) if hr2[3] > 0 else float("nan")
    gaps = bootstrap_null(sig, rets, gmid, med, tr_ts, tr_px)
    if gaps:
        gaps_sorted = sorted(gaps)
        lo = gaps_sorted[max(0, int(0.025*len(gaps)))]
        hi = gaps_sorted[min(len(gaps)-1, int(0.975*len(gaps)))]
        fired5 = (lo <= obs_gap <= hi) if obs_gap == obs_gap else None
        kills[5] = dict(value=(obs_gap, lo, hi), threshold="observed outside null band",
                        fired=bool(fired5), detail=f"observed |logHR|={obs_gap:.4f}, null band [{lo:.4f},{hi:.4f}] from {len(gaps)} reps (reduced)")
    else:
        kills[5] = dict(value=None, threshold="", fired=False,
                        detail="null reps produced no valid fits (degenerate synthetic states) - not evaluable")
    log(f"KILL LINE 5: {'FIRED' if kills[5]['fired'] else 'not fired'} - {kills[5]['detail']}")

    log("")
    log(f"=== DRY RUN COMPLETE in {time.time()-T0:.1f}s ===")
    log("MACHINERY VERDICT (not research): stages 1-9 + kill lines 1-5 EXECUTED without crash.")
    log("Every number above is a tooling output on 1 day of data with labeled adaptations D1-D6.")
    log("NO research verdict is drawn; C1-L2 remains unregistered pending full data + dated amendment.")

def long_format_for(r):
    """Long-format split rows for one decision row at H_DRY."""
    out = []
    t0 = r["t0"]
    if r.get("event") is None: return out
    touch = t0 + r["duration_h"]*3600*10**6
    end = min(t0 + H_DRY, DAY_END_US)
    s = t0
    while s < end - 1:
        nxt = min(s + 3600*10**6, end)
        ev = 1 if (r["event"] == 1 and touch > s and touch <= nxt) else 0
        out.append(dict(start=(s - DAY_START_US)/(3600*10**6),
                        stop=(nxt - DAY_START_US)/(3600*10**6),
                        event=ev, compression=r["compression"],
                        time_in_comp=math.log1p(r["time_in_comp"]),
                        sigma=r["sigma"], hod=r["hod"]))
        s = nxt
    return out

def filter_powered_folds(folds, min_events=POWER_MIN_EVENTS):
    out = []
    for i, f in enumerate(folds):
        rec = dict(f); rec.setdefault("fold_id", i)
        rec["powered"] = int(f["events"]) >= min_events
        out.append(rec)
    changed = True
    while changed:
        changed = False
        for i, rec in enumerate(out):
            if rec["powered"] or rec["events"] >= min_events: continue
            j = i+1 if i+1 < len(out) else i-1
            if j < 0: break
            tgt = out[j]
            tgt["events"] += rec["events"]
            tgt["powered"] = tgt["events"] >= min_events
            tgt["lr_p"] = None
            out.pop(i); changed = True; break
    return out

def bootstrap_null(sig, rets, gmid, med, tr_ts, tr_px):
    """Kill-line-5 machinery, REDUCED (D5): standardized-mid 1h block
    bootstrap, 10 reps, synthetic pipeline on the 1-min grid."""
    nmin = len(gmid)
    resid = []
    for m in range(nmin):
        if rets[m] is not None and sig[m] and sig[m] > 0:
            s1m = sig[m]/SIG_SCALE
            if s1m > 0:
                resid.append((m, rets[m]/s1m))
    # 1h blocks
    blocks = {}
    for m, r_ in resid:
        blocks.setdefault(m//60, []).append((m, r_))
    bkeys = sorted(blocks)
    gaps = []
    import random
    rng = random.Random(424242)
    for rep in range(BOOT_REPS):
        synth = [None]*nmin
        p0 = gmid[1] or 83000.0
        # walk resampled blocks in order, mapping block b -> minutes
        cur = p0
        order = []
        for m in range(nmin):
            order.append(m)
        bsel = [rng.choice(bkeys) for _ in range(24)]
        mi = 0
        synth[0] = cur
        for b in bsel:
            for (om, r_) in blocks[b]:
                # map original minute's sigma to target minute mi
                if mi >= nmin: break
                s_t = sig[mi] if sig[mi] else 0.0
                s1m = s_t/SIG_SCALE if s_t else 0.0
                cur = cur * (1.0 + s1m*r_)
                mi += 1
                if mi < nmin: synth[mi] = cur
            if mi >= nmin: break
            while mi < nmin and (mi % 60) != 0:
                synth[mi] = cur; mi += 1
        # fill gaps forward
        last = synth[0]
        for m in range(nmin):
            if synth[m] is None: synth[m] = last
            else: last = synth[m]
        # re-run clock on synthetic grid mid (grid granularity, labeled)
        try:
            gap = synth_pipeline_gap(synth, med)
            if gap == gap:
                gaps.append(gap)
        except Exception as ex:
            pass
    return gaps

def synth_pipeline_gap(synth, med):
    """Compression clock + hourly decision rows + H=2h barrier labels +
    Cox(compression+sigma) on the synthetic 1-min path; returns |log HR|."""
    nmin = len(synth)
    # sigma on synthetic path
    rets = [None]*nmin
    for m in range(1, nmin):
        if synth[m-1] and synth[m]:
            rets[m] = synth[m]/synth[m-1] - 1.0
    sig = [None]*nmin
    p1 = [0.0]*(nmin+1); p2 = [0.0]*(nmin+1); cnt = [0]*(nmin+1)
    for m in range(nmin):
        p1[m+1] = p1[m] + (rets[m] if rets[m] is not None else 0.0)
        p2[m+1] = p2[m] + (rets[m]**2 if rets[m] is not None else 0.0)
        cnt[m+1] = cnt[m] + (1 if rets[m] is not None else 0)
    for m in range(nmin):
        lo_ = max(0, m-SIG_WIN_MIN); hi_ = m
        c = cnt[hi_]-cnt[lo_]
        if c < 30: continue
        s1_ = p1[hi_]-p1[lo_]; s2_ = p2[hi_]-p2[lo_]
        var = max(s2_/c - (s1_/c)**2, 0.0)
        sig[m] = math.sqrt(var)*SIG_SCALE
    # clock on grid
    range_units = [None]*nmin; comp = [None]*nmin
    pools = [[] for _ in range(HOD_BUCKETS)]
    maxv = deque(); minv = deque()
    for m in range(nmin):
        v = synth[m]
        while maxv and synth[maxv[-1]] <= v: maxv.pop()
        maxv.append(m)
        while minv and synth[minv[-1]] >= v: minv.pop()
        minv.append(m)
        lo_m = m - SIG_WIN_MIN + 1
        while maxv[0] < lo_m: maxv.popleft()
        while minv[0] < lo_m: minv.popleft()
        if m < SIG_WIN_MIN or sig[m] is None: continue
        rng = synth[maxv[0]] - synth[minv[0]]
        ru = (rng/synth[m])/sig[m]
        b = (m//60//4) % HOD_BUCKETS
        pool = pools[b]
        if len(pool) >= MIN_POOL:
            p = 100.0*sum(1 for x in pool if x <= ru)/len(pool)
            comp[m] = 1 if p <= PCT_THRESHOLD else 0
        pool.append(ru)
    # decision rows hourly + transitions
    rows = []
    prev = None
    for m in range(nmin):
        if comp[m] is None: continue
        if prev is None or (prev == 0 and comp[m] == 1):
            rows.append((m, comp[m], sig[m], med[m], synth[m]))
        prev = comp[m]
    for h in range(1, 24):
        m = h*60
        if m < nmin and comp[m] is not None:
            rows.append((m, comp[m], sig[m], med[m], synth[m]))
    if len(rows) < 8: return float("nan")
    # labels: synthetic grid mid crossing barrier (labeled trade-touch approx)
    labeled = []
    for (m, c_, s_, sp_, p0_) in rows:
        if s_ is None or sp_ is None: continue
        barrier = max(K*s_*math.sqrt(1.0/12.0)*p0_, BARRIER_MIN_SPREADS*sp_)
        up, dn = p0_+barrier, p0_-barrier
        ev = 0; dur = 2.0
        for mm in range(m+1, min(m+121, nmin)):
            if synth[mm] >= up or synth[mm] <= dn:
                ev = 1; dur = (mm-m)/60.0; break
        labeled.append(dict(start=m/60.0, stop=m/60.0+dur, event=ev,
                            compression=c_, sigma=s_,
                            hod=(m//60//4) % HOD_BUCKETS))
    long_rows = []
    for r in labeled:
        long_rows.append(dict(start=r["start"], stop=max(r["stop"], r["start"]+1e-6),
                              event=r["event"], compression=r["compression"],
                              time_in_comp=0.0, sigma=r["sigma"], hod=r["hod"]))
    nev = sum(r["event"] for r in long_rows)
    if nev < 3: return float("nan")
    fit = cox_fit(long_rows, ["compression", "sigma"])
    hr = math.exp(fit["beta"][0])
    if hr <= 0: return float("nan")
    return abs(math.log(hr))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/c1l2_dryrun.py

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/f6_cw1_dep_audit.py (13504 bytes)
"""F6 AUDIT #2 (session 141, 19 Nov 2026): signed Amendment 13 C-W1 BTC weekly
arm, Stage-A entry-KM DEPENDENCE audit.

PRE-DECLARED in docs/smh_frontier_v0.md (session 141 CLAIM line) BEFORE any
computation: the audited object (the frozen A13 Stage A entry KM: 14
compression entries, 72 person-weeks, 14 first events, cum4 0.4286, cum12
1.0000 with the saturated collapsed band, matched climatology cum12 0.6132
band (0.560, 0.613, 0.667), gap +0.3868 bookkeeping-never-proof, LOW_BTC
0.062377 by the frozen rank transfer), the ONE KNOB (the dependence/variance
construction ONLY, reused verbatim from the session-62/67/137 family), all
cells reported, NONE selected, every frozen quantity untouched BY FIAT.

Cells (verbatim conventions from the session-67/137 family):
  C0  anchor pass: pooled entry KM + Greenwood recomputed from
      data/btc_weekly_iso.csv (design rows only) must reproduce the frozen
      stage0x_a13_cw1_btcweekly_report.json numbers before any cell is
      reported; person-week consistency enforced; LOW_BTC loaded FROZEN from
      the report, never recomputed.
  C1  era-block dispersion: two-year blocks anchored to the arm's own design
      start (era 0 = 2014-2015, ..., era 3 = 2020-2021, 2020 only), the
      session-67 convention; per-era entry KM cum12, across-era mean/sd
      (ddof=1 over the K eras with a defined cum12), dependence-honest band
      mean +/- 1.96*sd/sqrt(K); nulls reported as null.
  C2  leave-one-era-out pooled cum12, all 4 exclusions reported.
  C3  declustered entry KM: pre-committed 8-week rule (an entry joins the
      previous entry's cluster iff within 8 x 7 days of it; anchors = first
      entries), the session-67/137 convention.

WHAT THIS REFUSES: any 2021+ row (design rows only, the 2021-01-01 seal is
the file boundary itself and the loader asserts it); any cell selection; any
comparison licensing any choice; re-tuning of LOW_BTC, the trigger, the event
bar, the horizon or the kill lines; direction; any promotion of the +0.3868
design gap. The audit adds NO evidence; it bounds the saturated band's
honesty. Stdlib-only by the session-137 precedent.
Usage: python3 research/f6_cw1_dep_audit.py
"""
import csv
import json
import math
from datetime import date
from pathlib import Path

REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
DESIGN_END = date(2021, 1, 1)
HORIZON = 12
BAR_K = 2.5
Z95 = 1.959963985
ERAS = 4


def load_design(path):
    """Design-window weekly rows: r1, sig_w (8-week ddof=1), ev_next
    (|r1_{t+1}| >= 2.5 * sig_w_t; None at boundary/warmup by construction)."""
    rows = []
    with open(path, newline="") as f:
        for r in csv.DictReader(f):
            rows.append({"date": date.fromisoformat(r["date"][:10]),
                         "close": float(r["close"])})
    rows.sort(key=lambda r: r["date"])
    assert rows, "empty design file"
    assert rows[-1]["date"] < DESIGN_END, "seal breach: 2021+ row in design file"
    prev_close = None
    for r in rows:
        r["r1"] = None if prev_close is None else math.log(r["close"] / prev_close)
        prev_close = r["close"]
    for i, r in enumerate(rows):
        window = [rows[j]["r1"] for j in range(max(0, i - 7), i + 1)]
        if len(window) < 8 or any(w is None for w in window):
            r["sig_w"] = None
        else:
            m = sum(window) / 8.0
            r["sig_w"] = math.sqrt(sum((w - m) ** 2 for w in window) / 7.0)
    for i, r in enumerate(rows):
        nxt = rows[i + 1] if i + 1 < len(rows) else None
        if nxt is None or r["sig_w"] is None or nxt["r1"] is None:
            r["ev_next"] = None
        else:
            r["ev_next"] = 1.0 if abs(nxt["r1"]) >= BAR_K * r["sig_w"] else 0.0
    return rows


def cross_entries_comp(rows, low_btc):
    """Frozen cross semantics verbatim: in_region = sig_w <= cut (low side,
    compression), first crossing only, dwell refused, valid sig_w only."""
    idx, prev_inside = [], False
    for i, r in enumerate(rows):
        inside = r["sig_w"] is not None and r["sig_w"] <= low_btc
        if inside and not prev_inside:
            idx.append(i)
        prev_inside = inside
    return idx


def person_weeks(rows, idx):
    """Stdlib port of stage0x_stageA.person_weeks (identical loop)."""
    out = []
    for i, t0 in enumerate(idx):
        for h in range(1, HORIZON + 1):
            if t0 + h > len(rows) - 2:
                break
            e = rows[t0 + h - 1]["ev_next"]
            if e is None:
                break
            out.append({"ep": i, "t0": t0, "h": h, "event": int(e > 0)})
            if e > 0:
                break
    return out


def km_table(pw):
    return {h: (len([r for r in pw if r["h"] == h]),
                sum(r["event"] for r in pw if r["h"] == h))
            for h in range(1, HORIZON + 1)}


def greenwood_cum(km):
    S, acc, out = 1.0, 0.0, {}
    for h in range(1, HORIZON + 1):
        n, ev = km[h]
        if n > 0:
            S *= 1 - ev / n
            if ev and n - ev:
                acc += ev / (n * (n - ev))
            out[h] = (1 - S, math.sqrt(S * S * acc))
    return out


def band(gw, h):
    cum, se = gw[h]
    return (round(max(0.0, cum - Z95 * se), 3), round(cum, 3),
            round(min(1.0, cum + Z95 * se), 3))


def median_lag(pw):
    lags, seen = [], set()
    for r in pw:
        if r["event"] == 1 and r["ep"] not in seen:
            seen.add(r["ep"])
            lags.append(r["h"])
    if not lags:
        return None
    s = sorted(lags)
    m = len(s) // 2
    return (s[m] + s[-m - 1]) / 2 if len(s) % 2 == 0 else s[m]


def era_of(d):
    """Two-year blocks anchored to the arm's own design start (session-67
    convention shape; labels match the frozen A13 era_dispersion)."""
    return max(0, (d.year - 2014)) // 2


def era_years(E):
    return f"{2014 + 2 * E}-{2015 + 2 * E}" + (" (2020 only)" if E == 3 else "")


def cum12_of(idx, rows):
    gw = greenwood_cum(km_table(person_weeks(rows, idx)))
    return gw, (gw[12][0] if 12 in gw else None)


def main():
    frozen = json.loads((REPO / "research" /
                         "stage0x_a13_cw1_btcweekly_report.json").read_text())
    sA = frozen["stageA"]
    FROZEN = {
        "entries": len(sA["entries"]),
        "person_weeks": sA["person_weeks"],
        "events": sA["first_events"],
        "cum4": sA["cum4"],
        "cum12": sA["cum12"],
        "band12": sA["band12"],
        "median_lag": sA["median_lag"],
        "low_btc": frozen["low_btc"]["low_btc"],
    }
    low_btc = FROZEN["low_btc"]          # frozen BY FIAT, never recomputed

    rows = load_design(REPO / "data" / "btc_weekly_iso.csv")
    cidx = cross_entries_comp(rows, low_btc)
    pw = person_weeks(rows, cidx)
    km = km_table(pw)
    gw = greenwood_cum(km)
    consistency = len(pw) == sum(km[h][0] for h in km)

    c0 = {
        "entries": len(cidx), "person_weeks": len(pw),
        "events": len({r["ep"] for r in pw if r["event"] == 1}),
        "cum4": round(gw[4][0], 4) if 4 in gw else None,
        "cum12": round(gw[12][0], 4) if 12 in gw else None,
        "band12": band(gw, 12) if 12 in gw else None,
        "median_lag": median_lag(pw),
        "person_week_consistency": consistency,
        "km_table": [[km[h][0], km[h][1]] for h in range(1, HORIZON + 1)],
        "first_note": ("every design entry resolved (14/14 events) matches the "
                       "frozen 'first_events: 14'"),
    }
    checks = {
        "entries": c0["entries"] == FROZEN["entries"],
        "person_weeks": c0["person_weeks"] == FROZEN["person_weeks"],
        "events": c0["events"] == FROZEN["events"],
        "cum4": c0["cum4"] is not None and abs(c0["cum4"] - FROZEN["cum4"]) < 0.002,
        "cum12": c0["cum12"] is not None and abs(c0["cum12"] - FROZEN["cum12"]) < 0.002,
        "band12": all(abs(a - b) < 0.003 for a, b in zip(c0["band12"], FROZEN["band12"])),
        "median_lag": c0["median_lag"] == FROZEN["median_lag"],
        "consistency": consistency,
    }
    if not all(checks.values()):
        print(json.dumps({"C0_ANCHOR": "FAIL", "checks": checks,
                          "c0": c0, "frozen": {k: FROZEN[k] for k in
                          ("entries", "person_weeks", "events", "cum4",
                           "cum12", "band12", "median_lag")}}, indent=1))
        raise SystemExit(1)

    # C1 era-block dispersion (session-67 convention, design-anchored blocks).
    eras, era_cum = [], []
    for E in range(ERAS):
        sub = [i for i in cidx if era_of(rows[i]["date"]) == E]
        years = era_years(E)
        if not sub:
            eras.append({"era": E, "years": years, "entries": 0})
            continue
        g, cum12 = cum12_of(sub, rows)
        epw = person_weeks(rows, sub)
        firsts = len({r["ep"] for r in epw if r["event"] == 1})
        if cum12 is not None:
            era_cum.append(cum12)
        eras.append({
            "era": E, "years": years, "entries": len(sub),
            "person_weeks": len(epw), "events": firsts,
            "cum12": round(cum12, 4) if cum12 is not None else None,
            "band12": band(g, 12) if 12 in g else None,
        })
    K = len(era_cum)
    if K > 1:
        mean = sum(era_cum) / K
        sd = math.sqrt(sum((v - mean) ** 2 for v in era_cum) / (K - 1))
        dep_band = [round(mean - Z95 * sd / math.sqrt(K), 4),
                    round(mean + Z95 * sd / math.sqrt(K), 4)]
    else:
        mean, sd, dep_band = (era_cum[0] if K == 1 else None), None, None
    c1 = {"eras": eras, "K_with_defined_cum12": K,
          "mean": round(mean, 4) if mean is not None else None,
          "sd": round(sd, 4) if sd is not None else None,
          "dep_band": dep_band,
          "dep_band_constructible": dep_band is not None,
          "dep_band_brackets_frozen_point": bool(
              dep_band and dep_band[0] <= FROZEN["cum12"] <= dep_band[1])}

    # C2 leave-one-era-out pooled cum12, all 4 exclusions reported.
    c2 = []
    for E in range(ERAS):
        sub = [i for i in cidx if era_of(rows[i]["date"]) != E]
        _, cum12 = cum12_of(sub, rows)
        c2.append({"drop_era": E, "years": era_years(E), "entries": len(sub),
                   "cum12": round(cum12, 4) if cum12 is not None else None})
    loo_vals = [r["cum12"] for r in c2 if r["cum12"] is not None]
    c2_range = [min(loo_vals), max(loo_vals)] if loo_vals else None

    # C3 declustered entry KM (pre-committed 8-week rule; anchors = firsts).
    clusters, cur = [], [cidx[0]]
    for i in cidx[1:]:
        if (rows[i]["date"] - rows[cur[-1]]["date"]).days <= 8 * 7:
            cur.append(i)
        else:
            clusters.append(cur)
            cur = [i]
    clusters.append(cur)
    anchors = [c[0] for c in clusters]
    apw = person_weeks(rows, anchors)
    agw, acum12 = cum12_of(anchors, rows)
    c3 = {"clusters": len(clusters), "anchors": len(anchors),
          "person_weeks": len(apw),
          "events": len({r["ep"] for r in apw if r["event"] == 1}),
          "cum12": round(acum12, 4) if acum12 is not None else None,
          "band12": band(agw, 12) if 12 in agw else None,
          "cluster_sizes": [len(c) for c in clusters],
          "anchor_weeks": [str(rows[c[0]]["date"]) for c in clusters]}

    report = {
        "report": ("F6 audit #2 (session 141, 19 Nov 2026): signed Amendment 13 "
                   "C-W1 BTC weekly arm, Stage-A entry-KM dependence audit"),
        "predeclaration": ("docs/smh_frontier_v0.md session 141 CLAIM line "
                           "(appended BEFORE any computation)"),
        "scope": ("unsealed pre-2021 BTC design rows only, seal asserted by the "
                  "loader; all cells reported, NONE selected; LOW_BTC 0.062377, "
                  "the trigger, the event bar, the horizon, the kill lines and "
                  "the +0.3868 design gap sentence unchanged BY FIAT; the audit "
                  "adds NO evidence, it bounds the saturated band"),
        "C0_anchor": c0, "C0_anchor_ok": True,
        "C1": c1, "C2": {"rows": c2, "loo_range": c2_range}, "C3": c3,
        "notes": [
            "era convention: session-67 two-year blocks anchored to the arm's "
            "own design start (2014-2015 .. 2020-2021), labels matching the "
            "frozen A13 era_dispersion",
            "the saturated cum12 1.0000 (every design entry resolved within 12 "
            "weeks) makes the pooled Greenwood band degenerate BY CONSTRUCTION "
            "(Appendix A.2); cells that come back degenerate are reported as "
            "they are, never smoothed, never selected",
            "any promotion of the design gap from any cell here is refused; "
            "the arm's proof objects remain the forward kill lines n10 9 / "
            "n20 19 / n40 39 / n60 59, never this audit",
        ],
    }
    out = REPO / "research" / "f6_cw1_dep_audit_report.json"
    out.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=1) + "\n")
    print(json.dumps({"C0_ANCHOR": "PASS",
                      "C1": {"K": c1["K_with_defined_cum12"],
                             "mean": c1["mean"], "sd": c1["sd"],
                             "dep_band": c1["dep_band"]},
                      "C2_range": c2_range,
                      "C3": {"clusters": c3["clusters"],
                             "anchors": c3["anchors"],
                             "cum12": c3["cum12"],
                             "band12": c3["band12"]},
                      "report": str(out)}, indent=1))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

{

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/f6_stress_dep_audit.py (11455 bytes)
"""F6 AUDIT #1 (session 137, 19 Nov 2026): NQ STRESS arm Stage-A dependence audit.

PRE-DECLARED in docs/smh_frontier_v0.md (session 137 CLAIM line) BEFORE any
computation: the audited object (frozen NQ stress arm entry KM: 33 entries,
258 person-weeks, cum12 0.636, Greenwood band h12 (0.472, 0.636, 0.801),
comparator coin 0.5), the ONE KNOB (the dependence/variance construction,
reused verbatim from the session-62/67 family), all cells reported, NONE
selected, every frozen quantity untouched BY FIAT.

Cells (verbatim conventions from research/smh_entrydep_report.json, session 67):
  C0  frozen anchor: pooled stress KM + Greenwood, asserted identical to the
      frozen constants (33/258/21/0.636/band) before any cell is reported;
      the standing person-week consistency check enforced.
  C1  era-block dispersion: same era convention as session 67 (era 0 =
      2000-2002 tail, then two-year blocks 2003-04 .. 2019-20); per-era
      stress KM cum12, across-era mean/sd (ddof=1 over the K eras with
      entries), dependence-honest band mean +/- 1.96*sd/sqrt(K).
  C2  leave-one-era-out pooled cum12, all 10 reported.
  C3  declustered stress KM: pre-committed 8-week rule (an entry joins the
      previous entry's cluster iff within 8 weeks of it; anchors = first
      entries), the session-67 convention.

WHAT THIS REFUSES: any 2021+ row (hard seal at 2021-01-01); any cell
selection; any comparison licensing any choice; re-tuning; direction; the
compression side (audited session 67); the climatology side (session 62).
The audit adds NO evidence; it bounds the band. Stdlib-only by sandbox fact.
Usage: python3 research/f6_stress_dep_audit.py
"""
import csv
import json
import math
from datetime import date
from pathlib import Path

REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
DESIGN_END = date(2021, 1, 1)
HORIZON = 12
BAR_K = 2.5
NQ_HIGH = 0.02697
Z95 = 1.959963985

# Frozen NQ stress constants (stage0x_stageA.py NQ_FROZEN, the parity gate).
FROZEN = {
    "stress_entries": 33, "stress_person_weeks": 258, "stress_events": 21,
    "stress_cum4": 0.273, "stress_cum12": 0.636,
    "stress_band12": (0.472, 0.636, 0.801), "stress_median_lag": 6,
}


def load_weekly(path):
    """Stdlib port of stage0x_stageA.load_weekly (identical semantics)."""
    rows = []
    with open(path, newline="") as f:
        for r in csv.DictReader(f):
            rows.append({"date": date.fromisoformat(r["date"]),
                         "close": float(r["close"])})
    rows.sort(key=lambda r: r["date"])
    prev_close = None
    for i, r in enumerate(rows):
        if prev_close is None:
            r["r1"] = None
        else:
            r["r1"] = math.log(r["close"] / prev_close)
        prev_close = r["close"]
    for i, r in enumerate(rows):
        window = [rows[j]["r1"] for j in range(max(0, i - 7), i + 1)]
        if len(window) < 8 or any(w is None for w in window):
            r["sig_w"] = None
        else:
            m = sum(window) / 8.0
            r["sig_w"] = math.sqrt(sum((w - m) ** 2 for w in window) / 7.0)
    for i, r in enumerate(rows):
        nxt = rows[i + 1] if i + 1 < len(rows) else None
        if nxt is None or r["sig_w"] is None or nxt["sig_w"] is None:
            r["destress_next"] = None
        else:
            r["destress_next"] = 1.0 if nxt["sig_w"] <= NQ_HIGH else 0.0
    return rows


def cross_entries_stress(rows):
    """Frozen cross semantics: in_region & ~prev_in_region, valid sig_w only."""
    idx, prev_inside = [], False
    for i, r in enumerate(rows):
        inside = r["sig_w"] is not None and r["sig_w"] > NQ_HIGH
        if inside and not prev_inside and r["sig_w"] is not None:
            idx.append(i)
        prev_inside = inside
    return idx


def person_weeks(rows, idx):
    """Stdlib port of stage0x_stageA.person_weeks (identical loop)."""
    out = []
    for i, t0 in enumerate(idx):
        for h in range(1, HORIZON + 1):
            if t0 + h > len(rows) - 2:
                break
            e = rows[t0 + h - 1]["destress_next"]
            if e is None:
                break
            out.append({"ep": i, "t0": t0, "h": h, "event": int(e > 0)})
            if e > 0:
                break
    return out


def km_table(pw):
    return {h: (len([r for r in pw if r["h"] == h]),
                sum(r["event"] for r in pw if r["h"] == h))
            for h in range(1, HORIZON + 1)}


def greenwood_cum(km):
    S, acc, out = 1.0, 0.0, {}
    for h in range(1, HORIZON + 1):
        n, ev = km[h]
        if n > 0:
            S *= 1 - ev / n
            if ev and n - ev:
                acc += ev / (n * (n - ev))
            out[h] = (1 - S, math.sqrt(S * S * acc))
    return out


def band(gw, h):
    cum, se = gw[h]
    return (round(max(0.0, cum - Z95 * se), 3), round(cum, 3),
            round(min(1.0, cum + Z95 * se), 3))


def median_lag(pw):
    lags, seen = [], set()
    for r in pw:
        if r["event"] == 1 and r["ep"] not in seen:
            seen.add(r["ep"])
            lags.append(r["h"])
    if not lags:
        return None
    s = sorted(lags)
    m = len(s) // 2
    return (s[m] + s[-m - 1]) / 2 if len(s) % 2 == 0 else s[m]


def era_of(d):
    """Session-67 convention: era 0 = 2000-2002 tail, then two-year blocks."""
    return 0 if d.year <= 2002 else 1 + (d.year - 2003) // 2


def cum12_of(idx, rows):
    gw = greenwood_cum(km_table(person_weeks(rows, idx)))
    return gw, (gw[12][0] if 12 in gw else None)


def main():
    rows_all = load_weekly(REPO / "data" / "nq_weekly_canonical.csv")
    pre = [r for r in rows_all if r["date"] < DESIGN_END]
    sidx = cross_entries_stress(pre)
    pw = person_weeks(pre, sidx)
    km = km_table(pw)
    gw = greenwood_cum(km)
    consistency = len(pw) == sum(km[h][0] for h in km)

    # C0 frozen anchor gate: must reproduce the frozen stress constants.
    c0 = {
        "entries": len(sidx), "person_weeks": len(pw),
        "events": len({r["ep"] for r in pw if r["event"] == 1}),
        "cum4": round(gw[4][0], 4) if 4 in gw else None,
        "cum12": round(gw[12][0], 4) if 12 in gw else None,
        "band12": band(gw, 12) if 12 in gw else None,
        "median_lag": median_lag(pw),
        "person_week_consistency": consistency,
        "km_table": [[km[h][0], km[h][1]] for h in range(1, HORIZON + 1)],
    }
    checks = {
        "entries": c0["entries"] == FROZEN["stress_entries"],
        "person_weeks": c0["person_weeks"] == FROZEN["stress_person_weeks"],
        "events": c0["events"] == FROZEN["stress_events"],
        "cum4": c0["cum4"] is not None and abs(c0["cum4"] - FROZEN["stress_cum4"]) < 0.002,
        "cum12": c0["cum12"] is not None and abs(c0["cum12"] - FROZEN["stress_cum12"]) < 0.002,
        "band12": all(abs(a - b) < 0.003 for a, b in zip(c0["band12"], FROZEN["stress_band12"])),
        "median_lag": c0["median_lag"] == FROZEN["stress_median_lag"],
        "consistency": consistency,
    }
    if not all(checks.values()):
        print(json.dumps({"C0_ANCHOR": "FAIL", "checks": checks}, indent=1))
        raise SystemExit(1)

    # C1 era-block dispersion (session-67 conventions verbatim).
    eras, era_cum = [], []
    for E in range(0, 10):
        sub = [i for i in sidx if era_of(pre[i]["date"]) == E]
        years = ("2000-2002 tail" if E == 0
                 else f"{2003 + 2 * (E - 1)}-{2004 + 2 * (E - 1)}")
        if not sub:
            eras.append({"era": E, "years": years, "entries": 0})
            continue
        g, cum12 = cum12_of(sub, pre)
        epw = person_weeks(pre, sub)
        ev = sum(1 for r in epw if r["event"] == 1 and
                 next(j for j, q in enumerate(epw) if q["ep"] == r["ep"]) == r["ep"])
        firsts = len({r["ep"] for r in epw if r["event"] == 1})
        if cum12 is not None:
            era_cum.append(cum12)
        eras.append({
            "era": E, "years": years, "entries": len(sub),
            "person_weeks": len(epw), "events": firsts,
            "cum12": round(cum12, 4) if cum12 is not None else None,
            "band12": band(g, 12) if 12 in g else None,
        })
    K = len(era_cum)
    mean = sum(era_cum) / K
    sd = math.sqrt(sum((v - mean) ** 2 for v in era_cum) / (K - 1)) if K > 1 else None
    dep_band = ([round(mean - Z95 * sd / math.sqrt(K), 4),
                 round(mean + Z95 * sd / math.sqrt(K), 4)] if sd is not None else None)
    c1 = {"eras": eras, "K": K, "mean": round(mean, 4),
          "sd": round(sd, 4) if sd is not None else None,
          "dep_band": dep_band,
          "dep_band_brackets_frozen_point": bool(
              dep_band and dep_band[0] <= FROZEN["stress_cum12"] <= dep_band[1])}

    # C2 leave-one-era-out pooled cum12, all 10 reported.
    c2 = []
    for E in range(0, 10):
        sub = [i for i in sidx if era_of(pre[i]["date"]) != E]
        _, cum12 = cum12_of(sub, pre)
        c2.append({"drop_era": E, "entries": len(sub),
                   "cum12": round(cum12, 4) if cum12 is not None else None})
    loo_vals = [r["cum12"] for r in c2 if r["cum12"] is not None]
    c2_range = [min(loo_vals), max(loo_vals)] if loo_vals else None

    # C3 declustered KM (pre-committed 8-week rule; anchors = first entries).
    clusters, cur = [], [sidx[0]]
    for i in sidx[1:]:
        d_prev = pre[cur[-1]]["date"]
        d_i = pre[i]["date"]
        if (d_i - d_prev).days <= 8 * 7:
            cur.append(i)
        else:
            clusters.append(cur)
            cur = [i]
    clusters.append(cur)
    anchors = [c[0] for c in clusters]
    apw = person_weeks(pre, anchors)
    agw, acum12 = cum12_of(anchors, pre)
    c3 = {"clusters": len(clusters), "anchors": len(anchors),
          "person_weeks": len(apw),
          "events": len({r["ep"] for r in apw if r["event"] == 1}),
          "cum12": round(acum12, 4) if acum12 is not None else None,
          "band12": band(agw, 12) if 12 in agw else None,
          "cluster_sizes": [len(c) for c in clusters]}

    report = {
        "report": "F6 audit #1 (session 137, 19 Nov 2026): NQ STRESS arm Stage-A dependence audit",
        "predeclaration": "docs/smh_frontier_v0.md session 137 CLAIM line (appended BEFORE any computation)",
        "scope": ("unsealed pre-2021 design bookkeeping only; the spent NQ holdout 2021+ never touched; "
                  "all cells reported, NONE selected; frozen definition, cut 0.02697, comparator 0.5 and "
                  "kill lines unchanged BY FIAT; the audit adds NO evidence, it bounds the band"),
        "C0_anchor": c0, "C0_anchor_ok": True,
        "C1": c1, "C2": {"rows": c2, "loo_range": c2_range}, "C3": c3,
        "notes": ["era convention identical to session 67 (era 0 = 2000-2002 tail)",
                  "the stress arm event is de-stress (sig_w falls back at or below the 0.02697 cut), "
                  "so its dependence face is the spell-arrival clustering the 8-week rule measures"],
    }
    out = REPO / "research" / "f6_stress_dep_audit_report.json"
    out.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=1) + "\n")
    print(json.dumps({"C0_ANCHOR": "PASS", "C1": {"K": K, "mean": c1["mean"], "sd": c1["sd"],
                                                  "dep_band": c1["dep_band"]},
                      "C2_range": c2_range,
                      "C3": {"clusters": c3["clusters"], "cum12": c3["cum12"],
                             "band12": c3["band12"]},
                      "report": str(out)}, indent=1))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/f6_stress_dep_audit.py
