##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/f6_stress_dep_audit_report.json

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/ftep_monitor.py (24106 bytes)
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""FTEP monitor: Forward Track Evaluation Protocol (Amendment 6, DRAFT).

WHY THIS EXISTS. The holdout 2021+ is SPENT and forward paper time is the
programme's sole remaining evaluation path. Before this protocol, the
forward tracks (rule set T weekly stage 7, rule set V weekly parallel row,
V4h 4h experimental) had a booking convention ("26 entries before any
evaluation") but NO pre-committed inferential endpoint: no test statistic,
no multiplicity budget across concurrent tracks, no interim-look schedule,
no futility rule. Without those, "keep an eye on the paper results" decays
into optional peeking. This tool makes the forward phase a group-sequential
test registered in advance.

WHAT IT DOES (and refuses to do):
  power     - the honesty table: years needed to detect a given Sharpe.
  schedule  - the O'Brien-Fleming alpha-spending boundaries and each
              track's milestone thresholds. Descriptive math only; works
              before sign-off.
  status    - per track: feed availability, next milestone, sign-off
              state. NO performance statistics.
  test      - the milestone test. REFUSES unless ALL guards pass:
              (1) docs/amendment6_signed.json says signed:true (Andrea's
                  explicit sign-off of Amendment 6),
              (2) the track is a registered TESTED track,
              (3) the look index is valid for the amount of forward data
                  (a look window missed is expired and can never run),
              (4) the (track, look) pair is not already in the ledger,
              (5) the feed parses and covers the milestone.
  selftest  - validates the boundary computation by Monte Carlo and unit
              checks. Math only, no programme data.

STATISTICAL DESIGN (frozen in the Amendment 6 draft):
  - Family: one-sided alpha 0.05 split equally across the THREE tested
    forward tracks (T_weekly, V_weekly, V4h) => per-track alpha 0.05/3.
    U3 stays a descriptive comparison row and is never tested here.
  - Looks: K=10 per track, one per 26 completed weeks (weekly tracks:
    26 resolved weeks per look; V4h: 26 calendar weeks). Horizon 5 years.
  - Spending: Lan-DeMets O'Brien-Fleming, alpha(t) = 1 - Phi(z_a/sqrt(t)),
    equal information increments t_k = k/K, boundaries found by recursive
    numerical integration (exact, not the sqrt(K/k) approximation).
  - Statistic at look k: the full-track Sharpe z-stat (Lo 2002 iid and
    Mertens 2002 robust SE; the Mertens z is primary), net of booked costs,
    compared with the look's boundary.
  - Futility (looks >= 2): if the one-sided 80% upper confidence bound on
    annualized Sharpe < S_MIN = 0.5, the track is recorded FUTILE
    (recommend retirement). Futility is advisory-to-record; retirement
    still requires the programme's normal decision process.
  - Same-span buy-and-hold is reported descriptively at every test:
    a CONFIRM is "edge vs zero"; the buy-and-hold comparison governs
    tradeability, per lesson 2 (controls or it did not happen).

NOT PERMITTED by this tool, ever: backtesting any rule set, touching the
spent holdout, re-running the battery or H8/H9, computing statistics
outside a milestone, or editing any frozen constant of T / V / V4h.
"""

import argparse
import json
import math
import re
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from statistics import NormalDist

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

REPO = Path("/home/user/projects/quant")
DOCS = REPO / "docs"
DATA = REPO / "data" / "v4h"
SIGNED_F = DOCS / "amendment6_signed.json"
LOOKS_F = REPO / "research" / "ftep_looks.json"
REPORT_F = REPO / "research" / "ftep_report.json"
SUNDAY_LOG = DOCS / "stage7_sunday_log.md"
CANON = REPO / "data" / "nq_weekly_canonical.csv"

MALTA = timezone(timedelta(hours=1))  # Europe/Malta, November (CET)

# ---- frozen protocol constants (Amendment 6 draft) ----
FAMILY_ALPHA = 0.05          # one-sided, across all tested forward tracks
TESTED_TRACKS = ("T_weekly", "V_weekly", "V4h")
DESCRIPTIVE_TRACKS = ("U3_weekly",)   # never tested by this protocol
PER_TRACK_ALPHA = FAMILY_ALPHA / len(TESTED_TRACKS)
K_MAX = 10                    # looks per track
LOOK_WEEKS = 26               # calendar/resolved weeks per look (horizon 5y)
S_MIN = 0.50                  # minimum interesting annualized Sharpe
FUT_Z = NormalDist().inv_cdf(0.80)   # 0.8416, one-sided 80% upper bound

TRACKS = {
    "T_weekly":  {"feed": "weekly", "ppy": 52},
    "V_weekly":  {"feed": "weekly", "ppy": 52},
    "U3_weekly": {"feed": "weekly", "ppy": 52},   # descriptive only
    "V4h":       {"feed": "v4h",   "ppy": 1560},  # 30 globex 4h bars/week
}


def now_malta() -> datetime:
    return datetime.now(MALTA)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- boundaries
def obf_boundaries(alpha: float, K: int = K_MAX, n_grid: int = 4001,
                   lo: float = -10.0, hi: float = 10.0):
    """Exact Lan-DeMets O'Brien-Fleming boundaries on the BM scale.

    Information times t_k = k/K (equal increments). Spending
    a(t) = 1 - Phi(z_a / sqrt(t)). Returns (c_list, z_list) where
    c_k is the Brownian-motion-scale boundary at look k and
    z_k = c_k / sqrt(t_k) is the boundary for the Sharpe z-stat.
    """
    nd = NormalDist()
    z_a = nd.inv_cdf(1.0 - alpha)
    ts = [(k + 1) / K for k in range(K)]
    spend = [1.0 - nd.cdf(z_a / math.sqrt(t)) for t in ts]
    grid = np.linspace(lo, hi, n_grid)
    dx = float(grid[1] - grid[0])
    cs = []
    f = None
    for k, t in enumerate(ts):
        if k == 0:
            # B(t_1) ~ N(0, t_1); exit prob = spend(t_1) => c_1 = z_a
            f = NormalDist(0.0, math.sqrt(t)).pdf  # unused; kept for clarity
            f = np.array([NormalDist(0.0, math.sqrt(t)).pdf(x) for x in grid])
            c = z_a
        else:
            dt = t - ts[k - 1]
            surv = np.where(grid <= cs[-1], f, 0.0)
            # subdensity of B(t_k) among survivors: convolve with N(0, dt)
            sd = math.sqrt(dt)
            half = int(4.0 * sd / dx) + 1
            offs = np.arange(-half, half + 1) * dx
            ker = np.array([NormalDist(0.0, sd).pdf(o) for o in offs])
            f = np.convolve(surv, ker, mode="same") * dx
            target = spend[k] - spend[k - 1]

            def tail(c):  # exit probability at look k if boundary is c
                return float(f[grid > c].sum() * dx)

            lo_b, hi_b = grid[0], grid[-1]
            if tail(lo_b) < target or tail(hi_b) > target:
                raise RuntimeError("boundary outside grid")
            for _ in range(80):
                mid = 0.5 * (lo_b + hi_b)
                if tail(mid) > target:
                    lo_b = mid
                else:
                    hi_b = mid
            c = 0.5 * (lo_b + hi_b)
        cs.append(float(c))
    zs = [c / math.sqrt(t) for c, t in zip(cs, ts)]
    return cs, zs, spend


def power_table():
    nd = NormalDist()
    z_final = nd.inv_cdf(1.0 - PER_TRACK_ALPHA)
    rows = []
    for s in (0.25, 0.50, 0.75, 1.00, 1.25, 1.50):
        t_z196 = (1.96 / s) ** 2
        t_final = (z_final / s) ** 2
        t_80 = ((z_final + FUT_Z) / s) ** 2
        rows.append({"annualized_sharpe": s,
                     "years_to_reach_z1.96": round(t_z196, 1),
                     "years_to_reach_final_boundary": round(t_final, 1),
                     "years_for_80pct_power_final": round(t_80, 1)})
    return rows


# ------------------------------------------------------------------- feeds
def parse_weekly_equity(pattern: str):
    """Ordered (week_close, equity) from the append-only Sunday log."""
    text = SUNDAY_LOG.read_text()
    hits = re.findall(pattern, text)
    if not hits:
        return None
    out = []
    for wk, eq in hits:
        out.append((wk, float(eq.replace(",", ""))))
    return out


def load_track_returns(track: str):
    """Net-cost strategy return series for a tested track.

    Weekly feeds: multiplicative equity from the Sunday log's post-outcome
    lines. V4h: log-diffs of equity_after in the booking CSV.
    Returns (periods, rets) or raises ValueError with the honest reason.
    """
    spec = TRACKS[track]
    if spec["feed"] == "weekly":
        if track == "T_weekly":
            pat = (r"Paper equity:\s*([\d,.]+)\s*USD \(post-outcome, "
                   r"week closing (\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2})\)")
            hits = parse_weekly_equity(pat)
            if not hits:
                raise ValueError(
                    "T feed empty: no resolved post-outcome equity lines in "
                    "the Sunday log yet (Entry 0 resolves Sunday 2026-11-15).")
            eq = [e for _, e in hits]
        else:  # V_weekly
            pat = (r"V row equity:\s*([\d,.]+)\s*USD \(post-outcome, "
                   r"week closing (\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2})\)")
            hits = parse_weekly_equity(pat)
            if not hits:
                raise ValueError(
                    "V feed empty: no 'V row equity (post-outcome...)' lines "
                    "resolved yet. The V-row equity convention must be "
                    "confirmed against the first real resolved entries; the "
                    "adapter is finalized then. Guarded refusal, not an error.")
            eq = [e for _, e in hits]
        if len(eq) < 2 or any(b <= 0 for b in eq):
            raise ValueError("weekly feed parsed but degenerate (<2 points).")
        rets = np.diff(np.log(np.array(eq)))
        return hits, rets
    # V4h
    log_f = DATA / "v4h_log.csv"
    if not log_f.exists():
        raise ValueError("V4h booking log does not exist yet (nightly task).")
    df = pd.read_csv(log_f)
    eq = df["equity_after"].to_numpy(dtype=float)
    if len(eq) < 2 or any(b <= 0 for b in eq):
        raise ValueError("V4h feed degenerate (<2 booked bars).")
    rets = np.diff(np.log(eq))
    dates = df["bar_end"].tolist()
    return list(zip(dates, eq[1:].tolist())), rets


def v4h_track_start():
    st = json.loads((DATA / "v4h_state.json").read_text())
    ts = datetime.fromisoformat(st["track_start"])
    return ts, st


def weekly_look_available(track: str, look: int):
    """Weekly: the look guard is the COUNT of resolved weeks."""
    periods, rets = load_track_returns(track)
    n = len(rets) + 1
    lo_n, hi_n = LOOK_WEEKS * look, LOOK_WEEKS * (look + 1)
    ok = lo_n <= n < hi_n
    return ok, n, periods, rets


def v4h_look_available(look: int):
    """V4h: the look guard is elapsed CALENDAR time plus data coverage."""
    ts, st = v4h_track_start()
    elapsed_w = (now_malta() - ts).total_seconds() / (7 * 86400.0)
    ok_time = LOOK_WEEKS * look <= elapsed_w < LOOK_WEEKS * (look + 1)
    if not ok_time:
        return False, elapsed_w, None, None
    periods, rets = load_track_returns("V4h")
    # data must cover the milestone instant
    cover = datetime.fromisoformat(periods[-1][0])
    milestone = ts + timedelta(weeks=LOOK_WEEKS * look)
    ok_cover = cover >= milestone - timedelta(days=3)
    return (ok_time and ok_cover), elapsed_w, periods, rets


# -------------------------------------------------------------- statistics
def sharpe_z(rets: np.ndarray, ppy: int):
    """Lo (2002) iid and Mertens (2002) robust Sharpe z-statistics."""
    r = np.asarray(rets, dtype=float)
    n = len(r)
    if n < 10 or float(r.std(ddof=1)) == 0.0:
        raise ValueError("insufficient/non-varying return series")
    mu = float(r.mean())
    sd = float(r.std(ddof=1))
    sr_p = mu / sd
    sr_ann = sr_p * math.sqrt(ppy)
    z = ((r - mu) / sd)
    skew = float(np.mean(z ** 3))
    kurt = float(np.mean(z ** 4))
    var_lo = (1.0 + sr_p ** 2 / 2.0) / n
    var_me = max(1e-12, (1.0 - skew * sr_p + (kurt - 1.0) / 4.0 * sr_p ** 2) / n)
    se_ann_me = math.sqrt(var_me) * math.sqrt(ppy)
    return {
        "n_periods": n,
        "sr_period": round(sr_p, 6),
        "sr_ann": round(sr_ann, 4),
        "z_lo_iid": round(sr_p / math.sqrt(var_lo), 4),
        "z_mertens": round(sr_p / math.sqrt(var_me), 4),
        "se_ann_mertens": round(se_ann_me, 5),
        "skew": round(skew, 4),
        "excess_kurt": round(kurt - 3.0, 4),
        "sr_ann_ci80_upper": round(sr_ann + FUT_Z * se_ann_me, 4),
    }


def buy_hold_same_span(track: str, periods, rets):
    """Descriptive same-span buy-and-hold (lesson 2: always report it)."""
    spec = TRACKS[track]
    if spec["feed"] == "v4h":
        # hold the instrument over exactly the booked bars
        log_f = DATA / "v4h_log.csv"
        df = pd.read_csv(log_f)
        c = df["close"].to_numpy(dtype=float)
        if len(c) >= 2:
            bh = float(np.log(c[-1] / c[0]))
            return {"span_bh_logret": round(bh, 4), "n_bars": len(c)}
        return {"span_bh_logret": None}
    # weekly: from the canonical series between the first and last resolved week
    d0, d1 = periods[0][0], periods[-1][0]
    wk = pd.read_csv(CANON)
    wk["date"] = pd.to_datetime(wk["date"])
    m = (wk["date"] >= pd.Timestamp(d0)) & (wk["date"] <= pd.Timestamp(d1))
    c = wk.loc[m, "close"].to_numpy(dtype=float)
    if len(c) >= 2:
        return {"span_bh_logret": round(float(np.log(c[-1] / c[0])), 4),
                "n_weeks": len(c)}
    return {"span_bh_logret": None}


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ ledger
def load_ledger():
    if LOOKS_F.exists():
        return json.loads(LOOKS_F.read_text())
    return []


def save_ledger(led):
    LOOKS_F.write_text(json.dumps(led, indent=2))


def signed():
    if not SIGNED_F.exists():
        return False, "Amendment 6 sign-off file does not exist."
    try:
        d = json.loads(SIGNED_F.read_text())
        return bool(d.get("signed")), d
    except Exception:
        return False, "sign-off file unreadable."


# ------------------------------------------------------------- subcommands
def cmd_power(args):
    rows = power_table()
    print("FTEP power honesty (no programme data; pure arithmetic).")
    print(f"per-track one-sided alpha = {PER_TRACK_ALPHA:.5f} "
          f"(family 0.05 across {len(TESTED_TRACKS)} tested tracks)")
    print(f"{'SR_ann':>7} {'yrs to z=1.96':>14} {'yrs to final bound':>18} "
          f"{'yrs, 80% power':>15}")
    for r in rows:
        print(f"{r['annualized_sharpe']:>7.2f} "
              f"{r['years_to_reach_z1.96']:>14.1f} "
              f"{r['years_to_reach_final_boundary']:>18.1f} "
              f"{r['years_for_80pct_power_final']:>15.1f}")
    print("\nRead: confirming a Sharpe-0.75 forward edge takes ~7-9 YEARS.")
    print("Stage-7-style 26-week blocks validate process, never expectancy.")


def cmd_schedule(args):
    cs, zs, spend = obf_boundaries(PER_TRACK_ALPHA)
    print(f"FTEP schedule (Amendment 6 DRAFT; Lan-DeMets OBF spending, "
          f"alpha={PER_TRACK_ALPHA:.5f}, K={K_MAX}, "
          f"{LOOK_WEEKS}-week looks)")
    print(f"{'look':>4} {'t(years)':>9} {'BM bound c_k':>12} {'z bound':>8} "
          f"{'cum spend':>10}")
    for k, (c, z, s) in enumerate(zip(cs, zs, spend), start=1):
        print(f"{k:>4} {k/ K_MAX:>9.1f} {c:>12.4f} {z:>8.4f} {s:>10.2e}")
    print(f"\nNote: BM bounds are near the OBF constant z_a={NormalDist().inv_cdf(1-PER_TRACK_ALPHA):.4f}; "
          f"the final z-boundary is {zs[-1]:.4f} > z_a because the spending "
          f"curve has already spent {spend[-2]*100:.1f}% of alpha by look {K_MAX-1}.")
    for t in TESTED_TRACKS:
        feed = TRACKS[t]["feed"]
        if feed == "weekly":
            print(f"\n{t}: look k opens at {LOOK_WEEKS}*k RESOLVED weeks "
                  f"(feed: Sunday-log post-outcome equity lines)")
        else:
            ts, _ = v4h_track_start()
            print(f"\n{t}: look k at track_start + {LOOK_WEEKS}*k weeks "
                  f"(start {ts.date().isoformat()}, elapsed-based)")
    print(f"\nSign-off state: {'SIGNED' if signed()[0] else 'UNSIGNED (test subcommand refuses)'}")
    return cs, zs


def cmd_status(args):
    sgn, info = signed()
    print(f"FTEP status @ {now_malta().isoformat(timespec='seconds')}")
    print(f"Amendment 6: {'SIGNED' if sgn else 'DRAFT / UNSIGNED'}\n")
    for t in TESTED_TRACKS + DESCRIPTIVE_TRACKS:
        kind = "tested" if t in TESTED_TRACKS else "descriptive-only (never tested)"
        try:
            if t == "V4h":
                ts, st = v4h_track_start()
                n = 0
                if (DATA / "v4h_log.csv").exists():
                    n = int(len(pd.read_csv(DATA / "v4h_log.csv")))
                print(f"{t:10s} [{kind}] booked_bars={n} "
                      f"track_start={ts.date().isoformat()} "
                      f"equity={float(st['equity']):,.2f}")
            else:
                periods, _ = load_track_returns(t) if t in TESTED_TRACKS else (None, None)
                n = len(periods) if periods else 0
                extra = ""
                if periods:
                    extra = f" last_equity={periods[-1][1]:,.2f}"
                print(f"{t:10s} [{kind}] resolved_weeks={n}{extra}")
        except ValueError as e:
            print(f"{t:10s} [{kind}] feed not available: {e}")
    print(f"\nNext look (all tested tracks): look 1 at "
          f"{LOOK_WEEKS} weeks; horizon {K_MAX * LOOK_WEEKS} weeks "
          f"({K_MAX * LOOK_WEEKS // 52} years) per track.")


def cmd_test(args):
    track = args.track
    look = args.look
    # guard 1: sign-off
    sgn, info = signed()
    if not sgn:
        sys.exit("REFUSED: Amendment 6 is not signed by Andrea. Forward-track "
                 "evaluation is not permitted without the signed dated "
                 "amendment. Draft, boundaries and schedule remain available "
                 "via the other subcommands.")
    # guard 2: registered tested track
    if track not in TESTED_TRACKS:
        sys.exit(f"REFUSED: {track} is not a tested track "
                 f"({', '.join(TESTED_TRACKS)}).")
    if not (1 <= look <= K_MAX):
        sys.exit(f"REFUSED: look must be 1..{K_MAX}.")
    # guard 3: ledger
    led = load_ledger()
    if any(e["track"] == track and e["look"] == look for e in led):
        sys.exit(f"REFUSED: ({track}, look {look}) already in the ledger.")
    # guard 4+5: availability, feed coverage
    try:
        if track == "V4h":
            ok, elapsed, periods, rets = v4h_look_available(look)
            if not ok:
                sys.exit(f"REFUSED: look {look} window not active for V4h "
                         f"(elapsed {elapsed:.1f} weeks; window is "
                         f"[{LOOK_WEEKS*look}, {LOOK_WEEKS*(look+1)})). Missed "
                         f"looks are expired and can never run.")
        else:
            ok, n, periods, rets = weekly_look_available(track, look)
            if not ok:
                sys.exit(f"REFUSED: look {look} window not active for {track} "
                         f"({n} resolved weeks; window is "
                         f"[{LOOK_WEEKS*look}, {LOOK_WEEKS*(look+1)})). Missed "
                         f"looks are expired and can never run.")
    except ValueError as e:
        sys.exit(f"REFUSED: feed problem: {e}")

    # ---- the milestone test ----
    cs, zs, _ = obf_boundaries(PER_TRACK_ALPHA)
    z_bound = zs[look - 1]
    ppy = TRACKS[track]["ppy"]
    st = sharpe_z(rets, ppy)
    z = st["z_mertens"]
    confirmed = z > z_bound
    futile = (look >= 2) and (st["sr_ann_ci80_upper"] < S_MIN)
    decision = "CONFIRM" if confirmed else ("FUTILE" if futile else "CONTINUE")

    rec = {
        "ts": now_malta().isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
        "track": track,
        "look": look,
        "t_years": round(look * LOOK_WEEKS / 52.0, 3),
        "boundary_z": round(z_bound, 4),
        "decision": decision,
        "statistic": st,
        "buy_hold_same_span": buy_hold_same_span(track, periods, rets),
        "notes": ("CONFIRM = statistical edge vs zero at the alpha-spent "
                  "level. Tradeability vs the same-span buy-and-hold is "
                  "reported descriptively and governs any future live "
                  "decision separately. FUTILE = the 80% upper bound on "
                  "annualized Sharpe is below the minimum interesting "
                  "effect (0.50): retirement recommended."),
    }
    led.append({"track": track, "look": look, "status": decision,
                "ts": rec["ts"]})
    save_ledger(led)
    rep = json.loads(REPORT_F.read_text()) if REPORT_F.exists() else []
    rep.append(rec)
    REPORT_F.write_text(json.dumps(rep, indent=2))
    print(json.dumps(rec, indent=2))
    print(f"\nLedger and report updated (append-only): {LOOKS_F.name}, "
          f"{REPORT_F.name}")


def cmd_selftest(args):
    print("FTEP selftest (math only; no programme data).")
    ok = True
    cs, zs, spend = obf_boundaries(PER_TRACK_ALPHA)
    nd = NormalDist()
    z_a = nd.inv_cdf(1 - PER_TRACK_ALPHA)
    # 1. spending monotone and totals alpha
    if not all(spend[i] < spend[i + 1] for i in range(len(spend) - 1)):
        print("FAIL spending not monotone"); ok = False
    if abs(spend[-1] - PER_TRACK_ALPHA) > 1e-9:
        print("FAIL total spending != alpha"); ok = False
    # 2. z-boundaries decreasing; BM-scale c_k near-constant (OBF property)
    if not all(zs[i] > zs[i + 1] for i in range(len(zs) - 1)):
        print("FAIL boundaries not decreasing"); ok = False
    else:
        print("PASS z-boundaries strictly decreasing")
    if not all(abs(c - z_a) / z_a < 0.15 for c in cs):
        print("FAIL BM boundaries far from OBF constant"); ok = False
    else:
        print(f"PASS BM boundaries near z_a (max dev "
              f"{max(abs(c - z_a) / z_a for c in cs) * 100:.1f}%)")
    # 3. Monte Carlo crossing probability under H0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(42)
    M = 200_000
    steps = rng.normal(0.0, math.sqrt(1.0 / K_MAX), size=(M, K_MAX))
    B = np.cumsum(steps, axis=1)
    cb = np.array(cs)
    crossed = np.any(B > cb, axis=1)
    p_hat = float(crossed.mean())
    se = math.sqrt(PER_TRACK_ALPHA * (1 - PER_TRACK_ALPHA) / M)
    if abs(p_hat - PER_TRACK_ALPHA) > 4 * se:
        print(f"FAIL MC crossing {p_hat:.5f} vs alpha {PER_TRACK_ALPHA:.5f} "
              f"(tol {4*se:.5f})"); ok = False
    else:
        print(f"PASS MC crossing prob {p_hat:.5f} ~ alpha "
              f"{PER_TRACK_ALPHA:.5f}")
    # 4. Lo iid SE sanity: z == sr/sqrt((1+sr^2/2)/n)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(7)
    r = rng.normal(0.001, 0.02, 260)
    stt = sharpe_z(r, 52)
    manual = stt["sr_period"] / math.sqrt(
        (1 + stt["sr_period"] ** 2 / 2) / stt["n_periods"])
    if abs(manual - stt["z_lo_iid"]) > 1e-3:
        print("FAIL Lo iid z mismatch"); ok = False
    else:
        print("PASS Lo iid z matches closed form")
    # 5. buy-and-hold helper refuses gracefully on absent feed data
    try:
        sharpe_z(np.zeros(50), 52)
        print("FAIL degenerate series accepted"); ok = False
    except ValueError:
        print("PASS degenerate return series refused")
    print("SELFTEST " + ("PASS" if ok else "FAIL"))
    return 0 if ok else 1


def main():
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__,
                                 formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    sub = p.add_subparsers(dest="cmd", required=True)
    sub.add_parser("power", help="power honesty table (math only)")
    sub.add_parser("schedule", help="OBF boundaries + milestone thresholds")
    sub.add_parser("status", help="feed/sign-off status (no statistics)")
    tp = sub.add_parser("test", help="milestone test (guarded)")
    tp.add_argument("--track", required=True, choices=sorted(TRACKS))
    tp.add_argument("--look", required=True, type=int)
    sp = sub.add_parser("selftest", help="boundary/statistics selftest")
    args = p.parse_args()
    if args.cmd == "power":
        cmd_power(args)
    elif args.cmd == "schedule":
        cmd_schedule(args)
    elif args.cmd == "status":
        cmd_status(args)
    elif args.cmd == "test":
        cmd_test(args)
    elif args.cmd == "selftest":
        sys.exit(cmd_selftest(args))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/ftep_monitor.py

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/gen_thesis_pdf.py (10278 bytes)
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""gen_thesis_pdf.py - deterministic stdlib-only PDF edition of the SMH thesis.

Reads docs/smh_thesis_v1.md and emits an uncompressed, ASCII-only PDF
(Helvetica / Helvetica-Bold / Helvetica-Oblique) so the file is byte-stable
and transmissible as plain text. No dependencies, no network, double-run
deterministic. Usage:

    python3 gen_thesis_pdf.py [input.md] [output.pdf]

Defaults: docs/smh_thesis_v1.md
       -> docs/The-Salient-Market-Hypothesis-thesis-v1.0.pdf
"""
import os
import sys
import unicodedata

PAGE_W, PAGE_H = 595.28, 841.89
MARG_L, MARG_R, MARG_T, MARG_B = 64.0, 64.0, 72.0, 64.0

F_BODY, F_BOLD, F_ITAL = "F1", "F2", "F3"
SZ_BODY = 9.5
LEAD = 13.2
FOOTER = "SMH thesis v1.0"


def sanitize(s: str) -> str:
    """Force pure ASCII (the PDF bytes must stay 7-bit-clean)."""
    repl = {"ő": "o", "Ő": "O", "—": "-", "–": "-", "’": "'", "‘": "'",
            "“": '"', "”": '"', "×": "x", "·": "."}
    out = []
    for ch in s:
        out.append(repl.get(ch, ch))
    s = unicodedata.normalize("NFKD", "".join(out))
    s = "".join(c for c in s if not unicodedata.combining(c))
    return "".join(c if 32 <= ord(c) < 127 or c == "\n" else ""
                   for c in s)


def esc(s: str) -> str:
    return s.replace("\\", r"\\").replace("(", r"\(").replace(")", r"\)")


def strip_md(line: str) -> str:
    return line.replace("**", "").replace("`", "")


def wrap(text: str, width: int, cont_indent: int = 0) -> list:
    lines, cur = [], []
    for w in text.split():
        trial = " ".join(cur + [w])
        limit = width if not lines else width - cont_indent
        if cur and len(trial) > limit:
            lines.append(" ".join(cur))
            cur = [w]
        else:
            cur.append(w)
    if cur:
        lines.append(" ".join(cur))
    return lines


class Doc:
    def __init__(self):
        self.page_no = 0
        self.buf = []
        self.y = 0.0
        self.pages = []

    # -- internals --
    def _newpage(self):
        if self.buf:
            self.pages.append("\n".join(self.buf))
        self.buf = []
        self.y = PAGE_H - MARG_T
        self.page_no += 1

    def _room(self, need):
        if self.y - need < MARG_B:
            self._newpage()

    def _text(self, x, y, font, size, s):
        self.buf.append("BT /%s %.1f Tf %.2f %.2f Td (%s) Tj ET"
                        % (font, size, x, y, esc(s)))

    def _footer(self, content, n):
        label = "%s - page %d" % (FOOTER, n)
        width = len(label) * SZ_BODY * 0.50
        x = (PAGE_W - width) / 2.0
        return content + "\nBT /F1 8 Tf %.2f 40 Td (%s) Tj ET" % (
            x, esc(label))

    # -- public --
    def start(self):
        self._newpage()

    def line_at(self, x, dy, font, size, s, newpage_top=False):
        if newpage_top:
            self._newpage()
        self._text(x, self.y - size, font, size, s)
        self.y -= dy

    def para_lines(self, x, lines, font, size, dy):
        for ln in lines:
            self._room(dy + 2)
            self._text(x, self.y - size, font, size, ln)
            self.y -= dy

    def bullet(self, x, text):
        w = wrap(text, 84, 14)
        for k, ln in enumerate(w):
            self._room(LEAD + 2)
            if k == 0:
                self._text(x, self.y - SZ_BODY, F_BODY, SZ_BODY, "-")
            self._text(x + 14, self.y - SZ_BODY, F_BODY, SZ_BODY, ln)
            self.y -= LEAD

    def rule(self):
        self._room(14)
        self.buf.append("%.2f %.2f m %.2f %.2f l 0.5 w S"
                        % (MARG_L, self.y - 6, PAGE_W - MARG_R, self.y - 6))
        self.y -= 16

    def gap(self, pts):
        self.y -= pts

    def heading(self, text, level):
        if level == 0:  # part
            self._newpage()
            self.y = PAGE_H - MARG_T - 30
            self.line_at(MARG_L, 34, F_BOLD, 17, text)
        elif level == 1:  # chapter / major heading
            self._room(50)
            self.gap(8)
            for ln in wrap(text, 66):
                self._text(MARG_L, self.y - 12.5, F_BOLD, 12.5, ln)
                self.y -= 17
            self.y -= 6
        else:  # minor heading
            self._room(34)
            self.gap(3)
            for ln in wrap(text, 70):
                self._text(MARG_L, self.y - 11, F_BOLD, 11, ln)
                self.y -= 15
            self.y -= 4

    def finish(self):
        self.pages.append("\n".join(self.buf))
        self.buf = []
        return [self._footer(p, i + 1) for i, p in enumerate(self.pages)]


def build_pdf(page_contents) -> bytes:
    objects = {}
    objects[1] = b"<< /Type /Catalog /Pages 2 0 R >>"
    resources = b"<< /Font << /F1 3 0 R /F2 4 0 R /F3 5 0 R >> >>"
    kids = []
    num = 6
    for pc in page_contents:
        payload = pc.encode("ascii")
        objects[num] = (b"<< /Length " + str(len(payload)).encode()
                        + b" >>\nstream\n" + payload + b"\nendstream")
        sid = num
        num += 1
        objects[num] = (b"<< /Type /Page /Parent 2 0 R /MediaBox [0 0 "
                        + ("%.2f %.2f" % (PAGE_W, PAGE_H)).encode()
                        + b"] /Resources " + resources + b" /Contents "
                        + str(sid).encode() + b" 0 R >>")
        kids.append(str(num) + " 0 R")
        num += 1
    objects[2] = (b"<< /Type /Pages /Kids [ " + " ".join(kids).encode()
                  + b" ] /Count " + str(len(page_contents)).encode() + b" >>")
    objects[3] = (b"<< /Type /Font /Subtype /Type1 /BaseFont /Helvetica "
                  b"/Encoding /StandardEncoding >>")
    objects[4] = (b"<< /Type /Font /Subtype /Type1 /BaseFont "
                  b"/Helvetica-Bold /Encoding /StandardEncoding >>")
    objects[5] = (b"<< /Type /Font /Subtype /Type1 /BaseFont "
                  b"/Helvetica-Oblique /Encoding /StandardEncoding >>")

    out = bytearray(b"%PDF-1.4\n")
    offsets = {}
    for k in sorted(objects):
        offsets[k] = len(out)
        out += (str(k) + " 0 obj\n").encode() + objects[k] + b"\nendobj\n"
    xref_pos = len(out)
    maxnum = max(objects)
    out += b"xref\n0 " + str(maxnum + 1).encode() + b"\n"
    out += b"0000000000 65535 f \n"
    for k in range(1, maxnum + 1):
        out += ("%010d 00000 n \n" % offsets[k]).encode()
    out += (b"trailer\n<< /Size " + str(maxnum + 1).encode()
            + b" /Root 1 0 R >>\nstartxref\n" + str(xref_pos).encode()
            + b"\n%%EOF\n")
    assert all(b < 128 for b in out), "PDF is not ASCII-clean"
    return bytes(out)


COVER_STATUS = (
    "Status at freezing (19 November 2026, the Board's directive): every "
    "quantitative statement in this thesis is a frozen design figure "
    "produced on unsealed pre-2021 weekly NQ data; design statistics are "
    "bookkeeping, never proof. The proof object is the forward calibration "
    "record. The NQ weekly track is archived by the Board's directive with "
    "the forward log empty, so the hypothesis stands published, unfalsified, "
    "and unproved. The active research front is the L2 order-book track "
    "(C1-L2 -> C8 -> convexity sizing), testing the same hypothesis at book "
    "resolution. Kill criteria of every claim are stated with the claim.")


def cover(doc: Doc):
    doc.y = PAGE_H - 140
    doc.line_at(MARG_L, 36, F_BOLD, 26, "THE SALIENT MARKET HYPOTHESIS")
    doc.line_at(MARG_L, 32, F_BODY, 13,
                "A Falsifiable Theory of Liquidity and Time on the Weekly "
                "Chart")
    doc.line_at(MARG_L, 26, F_BOLD, 12, "Thesis, version 1.0")
    for s in ["Written 19 November 2026.",
              "Accepted and frozen by the Board's directive, 19 November 2026."]:
        doc.line_at(MARG_L, 15, F_BODY, 10, s)
    doc.gap(10)
    doc.rule()
    doc.para_lines(MARG_L, wrap("The two load-bearing sentences, absolute "
                                "and permanent:", 78), F_BOLD, 10, 14)
    doc.para_lines(MARG_L, wrap("The default assumption is NO EDGE. The "
                                "burden of proof is absolute.", 78),
                   F_ITAL, 10.5, 15)
    doc.gap(12)
    doc.rule()
    doc.gap(4)
    doc.para_lines(MARG_L, wrap(COVER_STATUS, 88), F_BODY, SZ_BODY, LEAD)


def body(md: str, doc: Doc):
    lines = [sanitize(strip_md(l.rstrip())) for l in md.split("\n")]
    start = next((i for i, l in enumerate(lines)
                  if l.startswith("## Abstract")), 0)
    i = start
    while i < len(lines):
        s = lines[i].strip()
        if s == "":
            i += 1
            continue
        if s.startswith("# "):
            doc.heading(s[2:].strip(), 0)
        elif s.startswith("## "):
            t = s[3:].strip()
            is_part = t.startswith("PART ") or t.startswith("APPENDIX") \
                or t == "Abstract"
            doc.heading(t, 0 if is_part else 1)
        elif s.startswith("### "):
            doc.heading(s[4:].strip(), 2)
        elif s == "---":
            doc.gap(8)
            doc.rule()
        elif s.startswith("- "):
            doc.bullet(MARG_L, s[2:])
            doc.gap(2)
        else:
            para = [s]
            j = i + 1
            while j < len(lines):
                nxt = lines[j].strip()
                if nxt == "" or nxt.startswith(("## ", "### ", "# ", "- ",
                                                "---")):
                    break
                para.append(nxt)
                j += 1
            doc.para_lines(MARG_L, wrap(" ".join(para), 88),
                           F_BODY, SZ_BODY, LEAD)
            doc.gap(6)
            i = j
            continue
        i += 1


def main():
    here = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
    inp = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else os.path.join(
        here, "..", "docs", "smh_thesis_v1.md")
    outp = sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else os.path.join(
        here, "..", "docs", "The-Salient-Market-Hypothesis-thesis-v1.0.pdf")
    with open(inp, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        md = f.read()
    doc = Doc()
    doc.start()
    cover(doc)
    body(md, doc)
    pages = doc.finish()
    pdf = build_pdf(pages)
    with open(outp, "wb") as f:
        f.write(pdf)
    print("pages=%d bytes=%d file=%s" % (len(pages), len(pdf), outp))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/l2gate/checkpoints.awk (630 bytes)
# Reservoir-sample k=100 book_snapshot_25 rows (seeded, deterministic mawk rand),
# sorted by timestamp, as gate checkpoints. Output: ts \t fields 5..104.
BEGIN{ srand(seed); k=100 }
NR==1{ next }
{
  n++
  if(n<=k){ slot[n]=$0 }
  else { j=int(rand()*n)+1; if(j<=k) slot[j]=$0 }
}
END{
  for(a=2;a<=k;a++){
    v=slot[a]; split(v,f,","); tsv=f[3]+0
    b=a-1
    while(b>=1){ split(slot[b],g,","); if(g[3]+0>tsv){ slot[b+1]=slot[b]; b-- } else break }
    slot[b+1]=v
  }
  for(a=1;a<=k;a++){
    split(slot[a],f,",")
    line=f[3]
    for(i=5;i<=104;i++) line=line "\t" f[i]
    print line > outfile
  }
  print "SNAPROWS " n
}

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/l2gate/convert_to_parquet.py (2502 bytes)
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Chunked Tardis CSV -> Parquet converter for binance-futures BTCUSDT L2 data.

AUTHORED 2026-11-15, NEVER EXECUTED in the Vibe sandbox (no pandas/pyarrow,
pip forbidden there). Intended for a Python environment with pandas >= 1.5
and pyarrow installed - e.g. Andrea's local machine. Sizes seen at authoring
time (real files downloaded and schema-verified this session):
  incremental_book_L2_2026-10-01.csv.gz  796,135,847 bytes, 145,203,455 rows
  book_snapshot_25_2026-10-01.csv.gz       90,294,106 bytes,  2,380,018 rows
Schema (datasets API, fixed):
  incremental_book_L2: exchange,symbol,timestamp,local_timestamp,is_snapshot,side,price,amount
  book_snapshot_25:    exchange,symbol,timestamp,local_timestamp,asks[i].price/amount,bids[i]....
Usage: python3 convert_to_parquet.py <indir> <outdir> --dtypes incremental_book_L2,book_snapshot_25,trades
Memory rule: chunksize=1,000,000 rows, stream gzip via pandas read_csv compression,
write with pyarrow compression="zstd". One parquet per UTC day per dtype.
"""
import argparse, glob, os, sys
import pandas as pd  # noqa: intentional hard dependency - this script does not run in the sandbox

CHUNK = 1_000_000

def convert_one(path, outdir):
    day = os.path.basename(path).split("_20")[0]  # crude; parse properly in real use
    out = os.path.join(outdir, os.path.basename(path).replace(".csv.gz", ".parquet"))
    if os.path.exists(out):
        print(f"skip existing {out}")
        return
    first = True
    for chunk in pd.read_csv(path, compression="gzip", chunksize=CHUNK,
                             dtype={"exchange": "category", "symbol": "category",
                                    "is_snapshot": "bool"} if "incremental" in path else None):
        # microsecond ints must stay int64
        for c in ("timestamp", "local_timestamp", "id"):
            if c in chunk.columns:
                chunk[c] = chunk[c].astype("int64")
        mode = "w" if first else "a"
        chunk.to_parquet(out, engine="pyarrow", compression="zstd", index=False,
                         append=not first)
        first = False
    print(f"converted {path} -> {out}")

def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("indir"); ap.add_argument("outdir")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    os.makedirs(args.outdir, exist_ok=True)
    for path in sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(args.indir, "*.csv.gz"))):
        convert_one(path, args.outdir)
    return 0

if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/l2gate/convert_to_parquet.py

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/l2gate/gate.awk (4823 bytes)
# Streaming L2 gate (mawk, bounded memory): replays incremental_book_L2 diffs
# (is_snapshot=true rows = resync blocks, cleared via split("",arr) idiom),
# fires 100 checkpoint comparisons vs book_snapshot_25 ground truth.
# Criteria (per Andrea's 2026-11-15 directive): mid within 1 tick (0.1),
# per-level size within 5% rel tol for levels within 10bps of mid,
# pass if >= 99/100 checkpoints and overall level ratio >= 0.99.
# real_fail_no_collision counts failures where the checkpoint ts did NOT
# collide with same-microsecond updates (collision misses are CSV-ordering
# artifacts, not replay divergence).
BEGIN{
  FS=","
  n=0
  while((getline line < cpfile)>0){
    if(substr(line,1,8)=="SNAPROWS") continue
    n++
    split(line,f,"\t")
    cpTS[n]=f[1]+0
    for(l=0;l<25;l++){
      cpAT[n,l]=int((f[2+4*l]+0)*10+0.5)
      cpAQ[n,l]=f[3+4*l]+0
      cpBT[n,l]=int((f[4+4*l]+0)*10+0.5)
      cpBQ[n,l]=f[5+4*l]+0
    }
  }
  close(cpfile)
  fp=1
  nupd=0; ntot=0; nsnap=0; blocks=0; ndel=0
  minTS=0; maxTS=0; maxgap=-1; insnap=0; prevU=0
  midpass=0; dppPost=0; nColl=0; realFail=0; cmTot=0; mtTot=0
}
/^exchange,/ { next }
{
  ntot++
  ts=$3+0
  if(minTS==0||ts<minTS) minTS=ts
  if(ts>maxTS) maxTS=ts
  snapflag=($5=="true")
  if(snapflag){
    nsnap++
    if(!insnap){
      blocks++; insnap=1
      split("", bid); split("", ask)
      bg=((prevU>0)?(ts-prevU)/1e6:-1)
      printf "BLOCK %d ts=%.6f gap_s=%.6f\n", blocks, ts/1e6, bg > blocksfile
    }
  } else {
    insnap=0
    nupd++
    if(prevU>0 && ts-prevU>maxgap){ maxgap=ts-prevU; gapAt=prevU }
    prevU=ts
    hh=int(ts/3600000000)
    hcount[hh]++
  }
  p10=int($7*10+0.5)
  amt=$8+0
  while(fp<=n && cpTS[fp]<ts){ fire(fp); fp++ }
  cp=fp
  while(cp<=n && cpTS[cp]==ts){
    if(!capDone[cp]){
      for(l=0;l<25;l++){
        p=cpAT[cp,l]; capAQ[cp,l]=(p in ask)?ask[p]+0:0
        p=cpBT[cp,l]; capBQ[cp,l]=(p in bid)?bid[p]+0:0
      }
      capDone[cp]=1
    }
    eqCount[cp]++
    cp++
  }
  if($6=="ask"){ if(amt==0){ if(p10 in ask) delete ask[p10] } else ask[p10]=amt }
  else { if(amt==0){ if(p10 in bid) delete bid[p10] } else bid[p10]=amt }
  if(amt==0) ndel++
}
END{
  while(fp<=n){ fire(fp); fp++ }
  hmin=-1; hmax=-1
  for(h in hcount){
    c=hcount[h]+0
    if(hmin<0||c<hmin){ hmin=c; hminH=h }
    if(c>hmax){ hmax=c; hmaxH=h }
  }
  printf "DAY %s\n", day
  printf "rows_total %d\n", ntot
  printf "rows_updates %d\n", nupd
  printf "rows_snapshot %d\n", nsnap
  printf "deletions %d\n", ndel
  printf "snapshot_blocks %d\n", blocks
  printf "min_ts %.6f\n", minTS/1e6
  printf "max_ts %.6f\n", maxTS/1e6
  printf "max_update_gap_s %.6f at %.6f\n", maxgap/1e6, gapAt/1e6
  printf "hourly_updates_min %d (epochhour %d)\n", hmin, hminH
  printf "hourly_updates_max %d (epochhour %d)\n", hmax, hmaxH
  printf "checkpoints_mid_pass %d/100\n", midpass
  printf "checkpoints_depth_pass_postLE %d/100\n", dppPost
  printf "collision_cps %d\n", nColl
  printf "real_fail_no_collision %d\n", realFail
  printf "levels_compared %d levels_matched %d ratio %.6f\n", cmTot, mtTot, (cmTot?mtTot/cmTot:0)
  for(h in hcount) print h, hcount[h] > hourlyfile
  ok=(midpass>=99 && realFail==0 && dppPost>=99 && cmTot>0 && mtTot/cmTot>=0.99)
  printf "GATE_EXIT %d\n", (ok?0:1)
  exit (ok?0:1)
}
function fire(jj,  bb,ba,k,midr,mids,rng,l,pa,qa,q,d,pb,qb,cm,mt,mp,rop,coll){
  bb=-1; for(k in bid) if(bid[k]>0 && k+0>bb) bb=k+0
  ba=-1; for(k in ask) if(ask[k]>0 && (ba<0 || k+0<ba)) ba=k+0
  mids=(cpAT[jj,0]+cpBT[jj,0])/2
  if(bb>=0 && ba>=0) midr=(bb+ba)/2; else midr=-1
  mp=(midr>=0 && midr-mids<=1 && mids-midr<=1)
  if(mp) midpass++
  rng=0.001*mids
  coll=(eqCount[jj]+0>0)
  if(coll) nColl++
  cm=0; mt=0
  for(l=0;l<25;l++){
    pa=cpAT[jj,l]; qa=cpAQ[jj,l]
    if(pa>0 && qa>0 && pa<=mids+rng){
      q=(pa in ask)?ask[pa]+0:0
      d=q-qa; if(d<0)d=-d
      cm++; if(d<=0.05*qa) mt++
    }
    pb=cpBT[jj,l]; qb=cpBQ[jj,l]
    if(pb>0 && qb>0 && pb>=mids-rng){
      q=(pb in bid)?bid[pb]+0:0
      d=q-qb; if(d<0)d=-d
      cm++; if(d<=0.05*qb) mt++
    }
  }
  cmTot+=cm; mtTot+=mt
  rop=(cm>0)?mt/cm:0
  if(rop>=0.99) dppPost++
  else {
    if(!coll) realFail++
    printf "FAILCP idx=%d ts=%.6f eq=%d mid=%d rop=%.4f\n", jj, cpTS[jj]/1e6, eqCount[jj]+0, (mp?1:0), rop > diagfile
    for(l=0;l<25;l++){
      pa=cpAT[jj,l]; qa=cpAQ[jj,l]
      if(pa>0 && qa>0 && pa<=mids+rng){
        q=(pa in ask)?ask[pa]+0:0
        d=q-qa; if(d<0)d=-d
        if(d>0.05*qa) printf "  MISM ask lvl=%d p=%.1f q_replay=%.4f q_snap=%.4f\n", l, pa/10, q, qa > diagfile
      }
      pb=cpBT[jj,l]; qb=cpBQ[jj,l]
      if(pb>0 && qb>0 && pb>=mids-rng){
        q=(pb in bid)?bid[pb]+0:0
        d=q-qb; if(d<0)d=-d
        if(d>0.05*qb) printf "  MISM bid lvl=%d p=%.1f q_replay=%.4f q_snap=%.4f\n", l, pb/10, q, qb > diagfile
      }
    }
  }
}

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/l2gate/gate.awk

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/l2gate/run_day.sh (1411 bytes)
#!/bin/sh
# Self-contained per-day pipeline: download L2 + snapshot_25 for the day
# (Tardis datasets API, trial key from env), extract 100 seeded checkpoints,
# run the streaming gate, persist all outputs to the durable knowledge tree.
D=$1
B=/home/user/l2
DG=/home/user/knowledge/trading-research/pipeline/research/l2gate
OUT=/home/user/knowledge/trading-research/pipeline/data/l2_gate
K="$TARDIS_KEY"
curl -sS --max-time 120 -H "Authorization: Bearer $K" -o $B/raw/incremental_book_L2_$D.csv.gz "https://datasets.tardis.dev/v1/binance-futures/incremental_book_L2/2026/10/${D#2026-10-}/BTCUSDT.csv.gz" || exit 98
curl -sS --max-time 90 -H "Authorization: Bearer $K" -o $B/raw/book_snapshot_25_$D.csv.gz "https://datasets.tardis.dev/v1/binance-futures/book_snapshot_25/2026/10/${D#2026-10-}/BTCUSDT.csv.gz" || exit 97
zcat $B/raw/book_snapshot_25_$D.csv.gz | mawk -v seed=424242 -v outfile=$B/gate/ck_$D.tsv -f $DG/checkpoints.awk > $B/gate/ckmeta_$D.txt
zcat $B/raw/incremental_book_L2_$D.csv.gz | mawk -v day=$D -v cpfile=$B/gate/ck_$D.tsv -v hourlyfile=$B/gate/hourly_$D.tsv -v diagfile=$B/gate/diag_$D.txt -v blocksfile=$B/gate/blocks_$D.txt -f $DG/gate.awk > $B/gate/gate_$D.txt
RC=$?
cp $B/gate/gate_$D.txt $B/gate/ckmeta_$D.txt $B/gate/hourly_$D.tsv $B/gate/blocks_$D.txt $OUT/ 2>/dev/null
[ -s $B/gate/diag_$D.txt ] && cp $B/gate/diag_$D.txt $OUT/ || touch $OUT/diag_$D.txt
echo "$D EXIT $RC"
exit $RC

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/l2gate/run_day.sh

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/paper_fetch.py (18531 bytes)
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""paper_fetch.py — stdlib-only article fetch + PDF text extraction toolkit.

Replaces pypdf/pdfminer for this sandbox (package installation is forbidden).
Python standard library only: urllib, zlib, re, json, argparse.

Subcommands:
  fetch URL OUT            download a paper with a browser User-Agent
  extract IN.pdf [OUT.txt]  extract text; writes a JSON report next to OUT
  run URL OUT_PREFIX        fetch + extract in one step
  validate FILES...         extract several PDFs, write paper_fetch_report.json

Handled PDF features:
  - FlateDecode streams (zlib) and raw streams
  - page /Contents as a single reference or an array of references
  - indirect /Resources dictionaries
  - per-font decoding: ToUnicode CMaps (bfchar/bfrange), /Differences arrays
    (Adobe glyph names), 2-byte CID (Type0) codes, plain ASCII fallback
  - object streams (ObjStm) for page/font objects in PDF 1.5+ files
  - image-only scan detection (fonts==0, many Image XObjects -> OCR needed)

Not handled (flagged in the report rather than guessed):
  - scanned/image-only PDFs (no OCR in stdlib) -> "scan": true
  - encrypted PDFs, Type3 fonts, DCTDecode/JPX image text

Usage under programme discipline: research tooling only. It touches no market
data, runs no strategy code, and changes no frozen constants.
"""

import argparse
import json
import os
import re
import sys
import urllib.request
import zlib

UA = ("Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 "
      "(KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/120.0 Safari/537.36")
MAX_BYTES = 25 * 1024 * 1024

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- fetching

def fetch(url, out, timeout=90):
    """Download url to out with a browser UA. Returns a small facts dict."""
    req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={"User-Agent": UA})
    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as r:
        ctype = r.headers.get("Content-Type", "")
        data = r.read(MAX_BYTES + 1)
    if len(data) > MAX_BYTES:
        raise ValueError("file exceeds %d bytes" % MAX_BYTES)
    is_pdf = data[:5] == b"%PDF-"
    if not is_pdf and "pdf" not in ctype.lower():
        raise ValueError("not a PDF (content-type %r, magic %r)" % (ctype, data[:8]))
    with open(out, "wb") as f:
        f.write(data)
    return {"url": url, "out": out, "bytes": len(data),
            "content_type": ctype, "pdf": is_pdf}

# ------------------------------------------------------------ PDF plumbing

class PDF:
    def __init__(self, data):
        self.data = data
        self.objs = {}          # num -> body bytes
        self._scan_objects()
        self._load_object_streams()
        self.cache = {}

    # -- object table ------------------------------------------------------
    def _scan_objects(self):
        for m in re.finditer(rb"(?<![0-9])(\d+)\s+0\s+obj", self.data):
            num = int(m.group(1))
            if num in self.objs:
                continue
            e = self.data.find(b"endobj", m.end())
            if e < 0:
                continue
            self.objs[num] = self.data[m.end():e]

    def _load_object_streams(self):
        """Merge objects stored inside /Subtype /Type0 /Type /ObjStm streams."""
        for num, body in list(self.objs.items()):
            if b"/ObjStm" not in body or b"stream" not in body:
                continue
            c = self.stream_of(body)
            if c is None:
                continue
            n = re.search(rb"/N\s+(\d+)", body)
            first = re.search(rb"/First\s+(\d+)", body)
            if not n or not first:
                continue
            head = c[:int(n.group(1)) and int(first.group(1)) or 0]
            try:
                head = c[:int(first.group(1))]
                pairs = [int(x) for x in re.findall(rb"(\d+)", head)]
            except Exception:
                continue
            it = iter(pairs)
            for onum, off in zip(it, it):
                if onum not in self.objs:
                    self.objs[onum] = c[int(first.group(1)) + off:]

    def obj(self, num):
        return self.objs.get(num)

    def stream_of(self, obj_body):
        i = obj_body.find(b"stream")
        if i < 0:
            return None
        j = obj_body.find(b"endstream", i)
        if j < 0:
            return None
        raw = obj_body[i + 6:j].lstrip(b"\r\n").rstrip(b"\r\n")
        if b"/FlateDecode" in obj_body[:i]:
            try:
                return zlib.decompress(raw)
            except Exception:
                pass
        if raw[:2] == b"\x78\x9c" or raw[:2] == b"\x78\x01" or raw[:2] == b"\x78\xda":
            try:
                return zlib.decompress(raw)
            except Exception:
                pass
        return raw

    def ref_after(self, body, key):
        m = re.search(key + rb"\s+(\d+)\s+0\s+R", body)
        return int(m.group(1)) if m else None

    # -- fonts ---------------------------------------------------------------
    @staticmethod
    def parse_cmap(c):
        mp = {}
        for bf in re.finditer(rb"beginbfchar(.*?)endbfchar", c, re.S):
            for m in re.finditer(rb"<([0-9A-Fa-f]+)>\s*<([0-9A-Fa-f]+)>", bf.group(1)):
                try:
                    mp[int(m.group(1), 16)] = bytes.fromhex(
                        m.group(2).decode()).decode("utf-16-be", "replace")
                except Exception:
                    pass
        for bf in re.finditer(rb"beginbfrange(.*?)endbfrange", c, re.S):
            for m in re.finditer(rb"<([0-9A-Fa-f]+)>\s*<([0-9A-Fa-f]+)>\s*<([0-9A-Fa-f]+)>",
                                 bf.group(1)):
                lo, hi, d0 = (int(m.group(1), 16), int(m.group(2), 16),
                              int(m.group(3), 16))
                for k in range(lo, min(hi, lo + 65535) + 1):
                    mp[k] = chr(d0 + k - lo)
        return mp

    GLYPHS = {
        "space": " ", "exclam": "!", "quotedbl": '"', "numbersign": "#",
        "dollar": "$", "percent": "%", "ampersand": "&", "quotesingle": "'",
        "parenleft": "(", "parenright": ")", "asterisk": "*", "plus": "+",
        "comma": ",", "hyphen": "-", "period": ".", "slash": "/",
        "colon": ":", "semicolon": ";", "less": "<", "equal": "=", "greater": ">",
        "question": "?", "at": "@", "bracketleft": "[", "backslash": "\\",
        "bracketright": "]", "asciicircum": "^", "underscore": "_",
        "grave": "`", "braceleft": "{", "bar": "|", "braceright": "}",
        "asciitilde": "~", "quoteleft": "\u2018", "quoteright": "\u2019",
        "quotedblleft": "\u201c", "quotedblright": "\u201d", "endash": "\u2013",
        "emdash": "\u2014", "ellipsis": "\u2026", "bullet": "\u2022",
        "fi": "\ufb01", "fl": "\ufb02", "ffi": "\ufb03", "ffl": "\ufb04",
        "germandbls": "\u00df", "acute": "\u00b4", "dieresis": "\u00a8",
        "paragraph": "\u00b6", "section": "\u00a7", "copyright": "\u00a9",
        "registered": "\u00ae", "trademark": "\u2122", "degree": "\u00b0",
        "plusminus": "\u00b1", "multiply": "\u00d7", "divide": "\u00f7",
        "mu": "\u03bc", "beta": "\u03b2", "alpha": "\u03b1", "gamma": "\u03b3",
        "delta": "\u03b4", "pi": "\u03c0", "sigma": "\u03c3", "Sigma": "\u03a3",
        "omega": "\u03c9", "lambda": "\u03bb", "theta": "\u03b8", "epsilon": "\u03b5",
        "rho": "\u03c1", "tau": "\u03c4", "phi": "\u03c6", "partialdiff": "\u2202",
        "infinity": "\u221e", "integral": "\u222b", "summation": "\u2211",
        "radical": "\u221a", "approximate": "\u2248", "notequal": "\u2260",
        "lessequal": "\u2264", "greaterequal": "\u2265", "minus": "\u2212",
        "Agrave": "\u00c0", "Eacute": "\u00c9", "Oacute": "\u00d3",
        "Uuml": "\u00dc", "uuml": "\u00fc", "ouml": "\u00f6", "auml": "\u00e4",
        "eacute": "\u00e9", "egrave": "\u00e8", "ccedilla": "\u00e7", "ntilde": "\u00f1",
    }

    @classmethod
    def glyph_to_char(cls, name):
        name = name.decode("latin-1") if isinstance(name, bytes) else name
        if name in cls.GLYPHS:
            return cls.GLYPHS[name]
        if len(name) == 1:
            return name
        m = re.fullmatch(r"uni([0-9A-Fa-f]{4})", name)
        if m:
            try:
                return chr(int(m.group(1), 16))
            except Exception:
                return ""
        return ""

    def build_font_map(self, font_body):
        """Return (map, width) for a font object body. width: 1 or 2 bytes."""
        subtype = re.search(rb"/Subtype\s*/(\w+)", font_body)
        sub = subtype.group(1).decode() if subtype else ""
        width = 2 if sub == "Type0" else 1
        mp = {}
        tu = self.ref_after(font_body, rb"/ToUnicode")
        if tu is not None:
            tu_obj = self.obj(tu)
            if tu_obj is not None:
                c = self.stream_of(tu_obj)
                if c:
                    mp = self.parse_cmap(c)
                    if mp:
                        return mp, (2 if max(mp) > 255 else width)
        # /Differences array
        dm = re.search(rb"/Differences\s*\[(.*?)\]", font_body, re.S)
        if dm:
            code = 0
            for tok in re.finditer(rb"(\d+)|/([A-Za-z0-9_.]+)", dm.group(1)):
                if tok.group(1):
                    code = int(tok.group(1))
                else:
                    ch = self.glyph_to_char(tok.group(2))
                    if ch:
                        mp[code] = ch
                    code += 1
            if mp:
                return mp, width
        return mp, width

    def fonts_for_resources(self, res):
        """Map resource names (F1...) -> (font map, width)."""
        fonts = {}
        if res is None:
            return fonts
        fm = re.search(rb"/Font\s*<<(.*?)>>", res, re.S)
        if fm is None:
            # nested: /Font 205 0 R
            fref = self.ref_after(res, rb"/Font")
            if fref is not None:
                fdict = self.obj(fref)
                if fdict is not None:
                    inner = re.search(rb"<<(.*)>>", fdict, re.S)
                    fm = inner
        if fm is None:
            return fonts
        for f in re.finditer(rb"/(F\w+)\s+(\d+)\s+0\s+R", fm.group(1)):
            fbody = self.obj(int(f.group(2)))
            if fbody is not None:
                fonts[f.group(1).decode()] = self.build_font_map(fbody)
        return fonts

    # -- content rendering ---------------------------------------------------
    @staticmethod
    def unescape(s):
        out, i = [], 0
        esc = {b"n": "\n", b"r": "\r", b"t": "\t", b"(": "(", b")": ")", b"\\": "\\"}
        while i < len(s):
            c = s[i:i + 1]
            if c == b"\\" and i + 1 < len(s):
                n = s[i + 1:i + 2]
                if n in esc:
                    out.append(esc[n]); i += 2; continue
                if n.isdigit():
                    j, o = i + 1, b""
                    while j < len(s) and s[j:j + 1].isdigit() and len(o) < 3:
                        o += s[j:j + 1]; j += 1
                    out.append(chr(int(o, 8) & 0xFF)); i = j; continue
                i += 1; continue
            out.append(chr(c[0])); i += 1
        return "".join(out)

    def decode_str(self, s, mp, width):
        bs = s.encode("latin-1", "replace")
        if width == 2 and not mp:
            return ""
        out = []
        if width == 2:
            for k in range(0, len(bs) - 1, 2):
                code = bs[k] * 256 + bs[k + 1]
                out.append(mp.get(code, ""))
        else:
            for b in bs:
                if mp and b in mp:
                    out.append(mp[b])
                elif 32 <= b < 127:
                    out.append(chr(b))
        return "".join(out)

    def render(self, content, fonts):
        cur = (None, 1)
        out = []
        for m in re.finditer(
                rb"/(F\w+)\s+[\d.]+\s+Tf"
                rb"|\(((?:[^()\\]|\\.)*)\)\s*Tj"
                rb"|<([0-9A-Fa-f\s]+)>\s*Tj"
                rb"|\[(.*?)\]\s*TJ"
                rb"|\(((?:[^()\\]|\\.)*)\)\s*'"
                rb"|\(((?:[^()\\]|\\.)*)\)\s*\"",
                content, re.S):
            if m.group(1) is not None:
                cur = fonts.get(m.group(1).decode(), (None, 1))
            elif m.group(2) is not None:
                out.append(self.decode_str(self.unescape(m.group(2)), cur[0], cur[1]))
            elif m.group(3) is not None:
                hx = re.sub(rb"\s", b"", m.group(3))
                try:
                    out.append(self.decode_str(bytes.fromhex(hx.decode()).decode("latin-1"), cur[0], cur[1]))
                except Exception:
                    pass
            elif m.group(4) is not None:
                for t in re.finditer(rb"\(((?:[^()\\]|\\.)*)\)|<([0-9A-Fa-f\s]+)>", m.group(4)):
                    if t.group(1) is not None:
                        out.append(self.decode_str(self.unescape(t.group(1)), cur[0], cur[1]))
                    else:
                        try:
                            hx = re.sub(rb"\s", b"", t.group(2))
                            out.append(self.decode_str(bytes.fromhex(hx.decode()).decode("latin-1"), cur[0], cur[1]))
                        except Exception:
                            pass
                out.append(" ")
            elif m.group(5) is not None:
                out.append("\n" + self.decode_str(self.unescape(m.group(5)), cur[0], cur[1]))
            elif m.group(6) is not None:
                out.append("\n" + self.decode_str(self.unescape(m.group(6)), cur[0], cur[1]))
        return "".join(out)

    # -- pages ---------------------------------------------------------------
    def pages(self):
        for num, body in self.objs.items():
            if re.search(rb"/Type\s*/Page[^s\w]", body + b" "):
                yield num, body

    def extract(self):
        chunks = []
        page_reports = []
        for num, body in self.pages():
            res_ref = self.ref_after(body, rb"/Resources")
            res = self.obj(res_ref) if res_ref is not None else (
                body if b"/Font" in body else None)
            fonts = self.fonts_for_resources(res)
            parts = []
            cm = re.search(rb"/Contents\s+(\d+)\s+0\s+R", body)
            if cm:
                cobj = self.obj(int(cm.group(1)))
                c = self.stream_of(cobj) if cobj is not None else None
                if c:
                    parts.append(c)
            else:
                ca = re.search(rb"/Contents\s*\[(.*?)\]", body, re.S)
                if ca:
                    for r in re.finditer(rb"(\d+)\s+0\s+R", ca.group(1)):
                        cobj = self.obj(int(r.group(1)))
                        c = self.stream_of(cobj) if cobj is not None else None
                        if c:
                            parts.append(c)
            text = "".join(self.render(c, fonts) for c in parts)
            page_reports.append(len(text))
            chunks.append(text)
        return "\n".join(chunks), page_reports

    def is_scanned(self):
        n_fonts = len(re.findall(rb"/BaseFont", self.data)) or len(
            re.findall(rb"/Subtype\s*/(Type1|TrueType|Type0|CIDFontType0|Type1C)", self.data))
        n_img = len(re.findall(rb"/Subtype\s*/Image", self.data))
        n_objstm = self.data.count(b"/ObjStm")
        return n_fonts == 0 and n_img > 0, {"fonts": n_fonts,
                                            "images": n_img,
                                            "objstm": n_objstm}

# ------------------------------------------------------------ driver

def extract_pdf(path, out_txt=None):
    data = open(path, "rb").read()
    pdf = PDF(data)
    text, page_reports = pdf.extract()
    scanned, facts = pdf.is_scanned()
    ascii_ratio = (sum(1 for c in text if 32 <= ord(c) < 127 or c in "\n\r\t")
                   / max(1, len(text)))
    letters_ratio = (sum(1 for c in text if c.isalpha() or c == " ")
                     / max(1, len(text)))
    scanned, facts = pdf.is_scanned()
    # Deciding signal for scans is extracted length, not raw font counts:
    # font dicts can hide inside compressed object streams.
    if len(text.strip()) >= 500:
        scanned = False
    garbled = (len(text.strip()) < 500) is False and letters_ratio < 0.55
    if out_txt:
        with open(out_txt, "w") as f:
            f.write(text)
    report = {
        "file": os.path.basename(path),
        "pages": len(page_reports),
        "chars": len(text),
        "ascii_ratio": round(ascii_ratio, 3),
        "letters_ratio": round(letters_ratio, 3),
        "scanned_ocr_needed": scanned,
        "garbled_encoding": bool(garbled and not scanned),
        "pdf_facts": facts,
        "first_160": text[:160],
    }
    return text, report

def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="stdlib paper fetch + PDF extract")
    sub = ap.add_subparsers(dest="cmd", required=True)

    p1 = sub.add_parser("fetch")
    p1.add_argument("url")
    p1.add_argument("out")

    p2 = sub.add_parser("extract")
    p2.add_argument("infile")
    p2.add_argument("outfile", nargs="?")

    p3 = sub.add_parser("run")
    p3.add_argument("url")
    p3.add_argument("prefix")

    p4 = sub.add_parser("validate")
    p4.add_argument("files", nargs="+")
    p4.add_argument("--report", default="paper_fetch_report.json")

    a = ap.parse_args()
    if a.cmd == "fetch":
        print(json.dumps(fetch(a.url, a.out)))
    elif a.cmd == "extract":
        out = a.outfile or os.path.splitext(a.infile)[0] + ".txt"
        text, rep = extract_pdf(a.infile, out)
        rep["out"] = out
        print(json.dumps(rep))
    elif a.cmd == "run":
        pdf_path = a.prefix + ".pdf"
        fetch(a.url, pdf_path)
        text, rep = extract_pdf(pdf_path, a.prefix + ".txt")
        print(json.dumps(rep))
    elif a.cmd == "validate":
        reports = []
        for f in a.files:
            if not os.path.exists(f):
                reports.append({"file": f, "missing": True})
                continue
            try:
                _, rep = extract_pdf(f)
                reports.append(rep)
            except Exception as e:
                reports.append({"file": f, "error": repr(e)[:200]})
        with open(a.report, "w") as f:
            json.dump({"tool": "paper_fetch.py", "reports": reports}, f, indent=1)
        for r in reports:
            print(r.get("file"), r.get("chars", r.get("error", "missing")),
                  "scan" if r.get("scanned_ocr_needed") else "")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/paper_fetch.py
