##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/platform_ingest_v0.py (25182 bytes)
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""ORDERFLOW platform, Phase 1 build step P-1 (docs/platform_charter_v0.md).

The ingestion and normalization layer: standalone stdlib module parsing the
two named archive formats into one canonical, schema-validated event stream.

  Format A: Bybit public archive orderbook JSONL (snapshot + delta records),
            and Bybit publicTrade JSONL (aggressor side included).
  Format B: Tardis.dev incremental_book_L2 CSV (the cross-check source).

Canonical record (schema v0), one dict per event:
  seq      int     stream-global sequence, 0-based, strictly increasing
  venue    str     "bybit" | "tardis"
  symbol   str     e.g. "BTCUSDT"
  kind     str     "book_snapshot" | "book_update" | "trade"
  ts_ns    int     nanoseconds since epoch (normalized from the source unit)
  side     str     "bid"|"ask" for book events; "buy"|"sell" (aggressor) for trades
  level    int|None 0-based rank for snapshot rows; None for deltas/trades
  action   str|None "set" (snapshot row) | "upsert" | "delete"; None for trades
  price    str     exact decimal string, as carried by the source
  size     str     exact decimal string; "0" on deletes

HONESTY LABELS (per the charter's KB-5/KB-6 rules, no fabricated provenance):
  The TARGET SCHEMAS of the two vendor formats are modeled from the public
  documentation family (the Bybit history-data archive; the Tardis
  incremental_book_L2 spec). The exact archive field spellings and the
  timestamp unit are UNCERTAIN UNTIL CONFIRMED against the first real file
  (Phase 2, the owner's download). Engineering consequence: every spelling
  lives in the two FIELD_MAP dicts below (a single point of change), the
  parsers refuse silently-wrong guesses (they validate structure, sides,
  prices and sizes and raise on anything malformed), and any Phase 2 mismatch
  is an adapter fix at ONE place, never a rebuilt pipeline. This module is
  SYNTHETIC DATA ONLY (charter Phase 1 gate); it has never seen market data.
"""

import csv
import hashlib
import io
import json
import sys
from decimal import Decimal, InvalidOperation

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- schema maps

BYBIT_OB_FIELD_MAP = {
    "type_field": "type",            # expected values per map: snap_values / delta_values
    "snap_values": ["snapshot"],
    "delta_values": ["delta"],
    "ts_field": "ts",                # UNCERTAIN unit until the first real file; see ts_unit
    "ts_unit": "ms",
    "symbol_field": ("data", "s"),
    "bids_field": ("data", "b"),
    "asks_field": ("data", "a"),
    # level entries accepted in both archive styles, tried in order:
    "level_styles": ["pairs", "dicts"],
    "dict_price_keys": ("price", "p"),
    "dict_size_keys": ("size", "v"),
    "delete_sizes": ("0", "0.0", ""),
}

BYBIT_TRADE_FIELD_MAP = {
    "type_field": "type",            # "publicTrade"
    "ts_field": "ts",                # line-level timestamp, UNCERTAIN unit until first file
    "ts_unit": "ms",
    "payload_field": "data",
    "symbol_key": "s",
    "side_key": "S",                 # "Buy" / "Sell" (aggressor)
    "price_key": "p",
    "size_key": "v",
    "trade_ts_key": "T",             # per-trade timestamp, same unit as ts_field
    "buy_values": ["Buy", "buy", "BUY"],
    "sell_values": ["Sell", "sell", "SELL"],
}

TARDIS_CSV_COLUMNS = ["exchange", "symbol", "timestamp", "local_timestamp",
                      "is_snapshot", "side", "price", "amount"]
TARDIS_FIELD_MAP = {
    "ts_field": "timestamp",         # documented as ms with ns p in newer dumps;
    "ts_unit": "ms",                 # UNCERTAIN: parse int, accept ns-bearing values
    "is_snap_values": ["true", "1", "True"],
    "buy_side_values": ["buy"],      # Tardis convention: "buy" side = bid, "sell" = ask
    "sell_side_values": ["sell"],
}

UNIT_TO_NS = {"ms": 10**6, "us": 10**3, "s": 10**9, "ns": 1}


class IngestError(Exception):
    """Malformed source line: refused, never guessed around."""

    def __init__(self, code, line_no, detail):
        super().__init__(f"{code} at line {line_no}: {detail}")
        self.code = code
        self.line_no = line_no
        self.detail = detail


# ----------------------------------------------------------------- utilities

def _ns(raw, unit, line_no, what):
    try:
        v = int(str(raw).strip())
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        raise IngestError("E-TS-NOT-INT", line_no, f"{what}={raw!r}")
    if v < 0:
        raise IngestError("E-TS-NEGATIVE", line_no, f"{what}={v}")
    return v * UNIT_TO_NS[unit]


def _dec(raw, line_no, what, allow_zero=False):
    try:
        d = Decimal(str(raw).strip())
    except (InvalidOperation, ValueError, TypeError):
        raise IngestError("E-DEC-UNDECODABLE", line_no, f"{what}={raw!r}")
    if not d.is_finite():
        raise IngestError("E-DEC-NONFINITE", line_no, f"{what}={raw!r}")
    if d < 0 or (d == 0 and not allow_zero):
        raise IngestError("E-DEC-NONPOSITIVE", line_no, f"{what}={d}")
    return d


def _get(d, path, line_no, what):
    cur = d
    for k in path:
        if not isinstance(cur, dict) or k not in cur:
            raise IngestError("E-FIELD-MISSING", line_no, f"{what} at {'.'.join(path)}")
        cur = cur[k]
    return cur


def _one(d, keys, line_no, what):
    for k in keys:
        if k in d:
            return d[k]
    raise IngestError("E-FIELD-MISSING", line_no, f"{what} keys={list(d)[:6]}")


# ------------------------------------------------------- Bybit orderbook JSONL

def parse_bybit_ob_line(line_no, obj, fm=BYBIT_OB_FIELD_MAP):
    """One archive JSONL object -> list of canonical records (one per level)."""
    rtype = obj.get(fm["type_field"])
    if rtype in fm["snap_values"]:
        kind, action = "book_snapshot", "set"
    elif rtype in fm["delta_values"]:
        kind, action = "book_update", None
    else:
        raise IngestError("E-TYPE-UNKNOWN", line_no, f"type={rtype!r}")
    ts_ns = _ns(obj.get(fm["ts_field"]), fm["ts_unit"], line_no, fm["ts_field"])
    symbol = _get(obj, fm["symbol_field"], line_no, "symbol")
    out = []
    for side, key in (("bid", fm["bids_field"]), ("ask", fm["asks_field"])):
        levels = _get(obj, key, line_no, f"{side}s")
        if not isinstance(levels, list):
            raise IngestError("E-LEVELS-NOT-LIST", line_no, f"{side}s={type(levels)}")
        for idx, ent in enumerate(levels):
            price_raw, size_raw = _level_pair(ent, fm, line_no, f"{side}[{idx}]")
            price = _dec(price_raw, line_no, f"{side} price")
            is_delete = str(size_raw).strip() in fm["delete_sizes"]
            if is_delete:
                size = Decimal(0)
            else:
                size = _dec(size_raw, line_no, f"{side} size", allow_zero=True)
                is_delete = size == 0
            if kind == "book_update":
                action = "delete" if is_delete else "upsert"
            out.append({
                "seq": None, "venue": "bybit", "symbol": symbol, "kind": kind,
                "ts_ns": ts_ns, "side": side, "level": idx if kind == "book_snapshot" else None,
                "action": action, "price": str(price), "size": str(size),
            })
    return out


def _level_pair(ent, fm, line_no, what):
    if isinstance(ent, (list, tuple)) and len(ent) == 2:            # pairs style
        return ent[0], ent[1]
    if isinstance(ent, dict):                                       # dicts style
        return _one(ent, fm["dict_price_keys"], line_no, f"{what} price"), \
               _one(ent, fm["dict_size_keys"], line_no, f"{what} size")
    raise IngestError("E-LEVEL-MALFORMED", line_no, f"{what}={ent!r}")


# ------------------------------------------------------- Bybit publicTrade JSONL

def parse_bybit_trade_line(line_no, obj, fm=BYBIT_TRADE_FIELD_MAP):
    """One publicTrade archive object -> one canonical record per fill."""
    if obj.get(fm["type_field"]) != "publicTrade":
        raise IngestError("E-TYPE-UNKNOWN", line_no, f"type={obj.get(fm['type_field'])!r}")
    line_ts = _ns(obj.get(fm["ts_field"]), fm["ts_unit"], line_no, fm["ts_field"])
    payload = obj.get(fm["payload_field"])
    if not isinstance(payload, list) or not payload:
        raise IngestError("E-PAYLOAD-NOT-LIST", line_no, "data")
    out = []
    for i, tr in enumerate(payload):
        side = tr.get(fm["side_key"]) if isinstance(tr, dict) else None
        if side in fm["buy_values"]:
            s = "buy"
        elif side in fm["sell_values"]:
            s = "sell"
        else:
            raise IngestError("E-SIDE-UNKNOWN", line_no, f"S={side!r}")
        p = _dec(_get(tr, (fm["price_key"],), line_no, f"trade[{i}] price"),
                 line_no, "trade price")
        v = _dec(_get(tr, (fm["size_key"],), line_no, f"trade[{i}] size"),
                 line_no, "trade size")
        ts = _ns(tr.get(fm["trade_ts_key"], obj.get(fm["ts_field"])),
                 fm["ts_unit"], line_no, f"trade[{i}] T")
        out.append({
            "seq": None, "venue": "bybit",
            "symbol": _get(tr, (fm["symbol_key"],), line_no, "trade symbol"),
            "kind": "trade", "ts_ns": ts, "side": s, "level": None,
            "action": None, "price": str(p), "size": str(v),
            "line_ts_ns": line_ts,
        })
    return out


# ------------------------------------------------------ Tardis incremental CSV

def parse_tardis_row(line_no, row, fm=TARDIS_FIELD_MAP):
    """One incremental_book_L2 CSV row -> one canonical record."""
    side_raw = row.get("side")
    if side_raw in fm["buy_side_values"]:
        side = "bid"
    elif side_raw in fm["sell_side_values"]:
        side = "ask"
    else:
        raise IngestError("E-SIDE-UNKNOWN", line_no, f"side={side_raw!r}")
    ts_ns = _ns(row.get("timestamp"), fm["ts_unit"], line_no, "timestamp")
    price = _dec(row.get("price"), line_no, "price")
    size = _dec(row.get("amount"), line_no, "amount", allow_zero=True)
    is_snap = str(row.get("is_snapshot", "")).strip() in fm["is_snap_values"]
    kind = "book_snapshot" if is_snap else "book_update"
    if kind == "book_snapshot":
        action = "set"
    else:
        action = "delete" if size == 0 else "upsert"
    return {
        "seq": None, "venue": "tardis", "symbol": row.get("symbol", ""),
        "kind": kind, "ts_ns": ts_ns, "side": side, "level": None,
        "action": action, "price": str(price), "size": str(size),
    }


# ------------------------------------------------------------- stream assembly

def assemble(events_iter):
    """Assign global seq; return (records, stats)."""
    records, stats = [], {"by venue/kind": {}}
    for rec in events_iter:
        rec["seq"] = len(records)
        records.append(rec)
        k = f"{rec['venue']}/{rec['kind']}"
        stats["by venue/kind"][k] = stats["by venue/kind"].get(k, 0) + 1
    return records, stats


def validate_stream(records):
    """Cross-record checks. Returns a violation list (never raises).

    Per-record domain checks happen at parse time (parse-time refusals).
    Stream-level checks here: global time ordering, trade-record shape
    (no level, no action), and snapshot level ordering (bids strictly
    decreasing, asks strictly increasing, per venue/timestamp/side).
    """
    v = []
    last_ts = None
    for r in records:
        q = r["seq"]
        if r["kind"] == "trade":
            if r["side"] not in ("buy", "sell"):
                v.append(("V-TRADE-SIDE", q, r["side"]))
            if r["level"] is not None or r["action"] is not None:
                v.append(("V-TRADE-EXTRA", q, (r["level"], r["action"])))
        else:
            if r["side"] not in ("bid", "ask"):
                v.append(("V-BOOK-SIDE", q, r["side"]))
            if r["kind"] == "book_snapshot":
                if r["action"] != "set" or r["level"] is None:
                    v.append(("V-SNAPSHOT-SHAPE", q, (r["action"], r["level"])))
        if last_ts is not None and r["ts_ns"] < last_ts:
            v.append(("V-TIME-INVERSION", q, (last_ts, r["ts_ns"])))
        last_ts = r["ts_ns"]
    snap_groups = {}
    for r in records:
        if r["kind"] == "book_snapshot":
            snap_groups.setdefault((r["venue"], r["ts_ns"], r["side"]), []).append(r)
    for key, rows in snap_groups.items():
        rows.sort(key=lambda x: x["level"])
        prices = [Decimal(x["price"]) for x in rows]
        bad = (any(prices[i] <= prices[i + 1] for i in range(len(prices) - 1))
               if key[2] == "bid"
               else any(prices[i] >= prices[i + 1] for i in range(len(prices) - 1)))
        if bad:
            v.append(("V-LEVEL-ORDER", rows[0]["seq"],
                      (key, "bids not decreasing" if key[2] == "bid"
                       else "asks not increasing")))
    return v


# ------------------------------------------------------- synthetic fixtures
# All fixtures are hand-constructed (deterministic by construction, no RNG,
# no market data: prices are round synthetic numbers).

def fixture_bybit_snapshot():
    return {"type": "snapshot", "topic": "orderbook.500.BTCUSDT",
            "ts": 1_700_000_000_000,
            "data": {"s": "BTCUSDT",
                     "b": [["42000.5", "1.5"], ["42000.0", "2.0"], ["41999.5", "0.75"]],
                     "a": [["42001.0", "1.0"], ["42001.5", "3.25"], ["42002.0", "0.5"]]}}


def fixture_bybit_delta_dicts():
    return {"type": "delta", "ts": 1_700_000_060_000,
            "data": {"s": "BTCUSDT",
                     "b": [{"price": "41999.0", "size": "0.9"},
                           {"price": "42000.0", "size": "0"}],
                     "a": [{"p": "42003.0", "v": "2.0"}]}}


def fixture_bybit_delta_pairs_delete():
    return {"type": "delta", "ts": 1_700_000_120_000,
            "data": {"s": "BTCUSDT",
                     "b": [["41999.0", ""]],
                     "a": [["42004.0", "4.0"]]}}


def fixture_bybit_trades():
    return {"type": "publicTrade", "ts": 1_700_000_180_000,
            "data": [{"s": "BTCUSDT", "S": "Buy", "v": "0.125", "p": "42001.0",
                      "T": 1_700_000_179_500},
                     {"s": "BTCUSDT", "S": "Sell", "v": "0.5", "p": "42000.5",
                      "T": 1_700_000_180_100}]}


def tardis_csv_text():
    rows = [
        TARDIS_CSV_COLUMNS,
        ["bybit", "BTCUSDT", "1700000000000", "1700000000123", "true", "buy", "42000.5", "1.5"],
        ["bybit", "BTCUSDT", "1700000000000", "1700000000123", "true", "sell", "42001.0", "1.0"],
        ["bybit", "BTCUSDT", "1700000000600", "1700000000610", "false", "sell", "42002.0", "2.75"],
        ["bybit", "BTCUSDT", "1700000001200", "1700000001215", "false", "buy", "41999.0", "0"],
    ]
    return "\n".join(",".join(r) for r in rows) + "\n"


def fixture_bad_ob_rows():
    return [
        ("missing-price", {"type": "delta", "ts": 1, "data": {"s": "X", "b": [{"size": "1"}], "a": []}}),
        ("undecodable-price", {"type": "delta", "ts": 1, "data": {"s": "X", "b": [["abc", "1"]], "a": []}}),
        ("negative-size", {"type": "delta", "ts": 1, "data": {"s": "X", "b": [["10", "-1"]], "a": []}}),
        ("unknown-type", {"type": "weird", "ts": 1, "data": {"s": "X", "b": [], "a": []}}),
        ("zero-price", {"type": "snapshot", "ts": 1, "data": {"s": "X", "b": [["0", "1"]], "a": []}}),
    ]


def fixture_time_inverted_stream():
    a = fixture_bybit_snapshot()
    b = dict(fixture_bybit_delta_dicts())
    b["ts"] = a["ts"] - 60_000             # earlier than the snapshot: must be flagged
    return [a, b]


def fixture_unsorted_snapshot():
    s = fixture_bybit_snapshot()
    s["data"]["a"] = [["42003.0", "1.0"], ["42001.0", "1.0"]]
    return s


# ----------------------------------------------------------------- self-tests

def run_tests(report_path=None):
    tests = []

    def check(tid, name, fn):
        try:
            detail = fn()
            tests.append({"id": tid, "name": name, "pass": True, "detail": detail})
        except AssertionError as e:
            tests.append({"id": tid, "name": name, "pass": False, "detail": str(e)})
        except IngestError as e:
            tests.append({"id": tid, "name": name, "pass": False,
                          "detail": f"unexpected IngestError {e.code}: {e}"})

    def t01():
        recs = parse_bybit_ob_line(1, fixture_bybit_snapshot())
        assert len(recs) == 6, len(recs)
        b0 = recs[0]
        assert (b0["kind"], b0["side"], b0["level"], b0["action"]) == ("book_snapshot", "bid", 0, "set")
        assert (b0["price"], b0["size"], b0["ts_ns"]) == ("42000.5", "1.5", 1_700_000_000_000_000_000)
        a2 = recs[5]
        assert (a2["side"], a2["level"], a2["price"]) == ("ask", 2, "42002.0")
        return "6 snapshot rows, ranked, set; best bid/ask exact"

    def t02():
        recs = parse_bybit_ob_line(2, fixture_bybit_delta_dicts())
        acts = [(r["side"], r["price"], r["action"], r["size"], r["level"]) for r in recs]
        assert acts[:2] == [("bid", "41999.0", "upsert", "0.9", None),
                            ("bid", "42000.0", "delete", "0", None)], acts
        assert acts[2] == ("ask", "42003.0", "upsert", "2.0", None), acts
        return "dict-style delta: upsert + zero-size delete, level None"

    def t03():
        recs = parse_bybit_ob_line(3, fixture_bybit_delta_pairs_delete())
        assert recs[0]["action"] == "delete" and recs[0]["price"] == "41999.0"
        assert recs[1]["action"] == "upsert" and recs[1]["size"] == "4.0"
        return "pairs-style delta with empty-string delete size"

    def t04():
        recs = parse_bybit_trade_line(1, fixture_bybit_trades())
        assert len(recs) == 2
        assert (recs[0]["kind"], recs[0]["side"], recs[0]["price"], recs[0]["size"]) == \
            ("trade", "buy", "42001.0", "0.125")
        assert recs[1]["side"] == "sell" and recs[1]["ts_ns"] == 1_700_000_180_100_000_000
        assert recs[0]["seq"] is None           # assembly assigns seq later
        return "two trades, aggressor sides Buy->buy Sell->sell, per-trade T honored"

    def t05():
        rows = list(csv.DictReader(io.StringIO(tardis_csv_text())))
        recs = [parse_tardis_row(i + 2, r) for i, r in enumerate(rows)]
        assert (recs[0]["venue"], recs[0]["kind"], recs[0]["side"], recs[0]["action"]) == \
            ("tardis", "book_snapshot", "bid", "set")
        assert (recs[1]["side"], recs[1]["price"]) == ("ask", "42001.0")
        assert recs[2]["kind"] == "book_update" and recs[2]["action"] == "upsert" \
            and recs[2]["size"] == "2.75"
        assert recs[3]["action"] == "delete" and recs[3]["size"] == "0"
        return "tardis rows: snapshot set, delta upsert, zero-amount delete"

    def t06():
        # Cross-format integration: the same synthetic book through both venues.
        bybit = parse_bybit_ob_line(1, fixture_bybit_snapshot())
        tardis_rows = list(csv.DictReader(io.StringIO(tardis_csv_text())))
        tardis = [parse_tardis_row(i + 2, r) for i, r in enumerate(tardis_rows)]
        bb = {(r["side"]): (Decimal(r["price"]), Decimal(r["size"])) for r in bybit
              if r["kind"] == "book_snapshot" and r["level"] == 0}
        td = {(r["side"]): (Decimal(r["price"]), Decimal(r["size"])) for r in tardis
              if r["kind"] == "book_snapshot"}
        assert bb["bid"] == td["bid"] == (Decimal("42000.5"), Decimal("1.5"))
        assert bb["ask"] == td["ask"] == (Decimal("42001.0"), Decimal("1.0"))
        return "synthetic best bid/ask identical through both adapters"

    def t07():
        ob = parse_bybit_ob_line(1, fixture_bybit_snapshot())
        tr = parse_bybit_trade_line(2, fixture_bybit_trades())
        records, stats = assemble(iter(ob + tr))
        assert [r["seq"] for r in records] == list(range(8))
        assert stats["by venue/kind"] == {"bybit/book_snapshot": 6,
                                          "bybit/trade": 2}, stats
        assert records[6]["kind"] == "trade" and records[7]["kind"] == "trade"
        return "mixed stream assembled: 8 seq-ordered records, stats exact"

    def t08():
        records, _ = assemble(iter(parse_bybit_ob_line(1, fixture_bybit_snapshot())))
        v = validate_stream(records)
        assert v == [], v
        return "clean snapshot: zero violations"

    def t09():
        expect = {"missing-price": "E-FIELD-MISSING",
                  "undecodable-price": "E-DEC-UNDECODABLE",
                  "negative-size": "E-DEC-NONPOSITIVE",
                  "unknown-type": "E-TYPE-UNKNOWN",
                  "zero-price": "E-DEC-NONPOSITIVE"}
        for name, obj in fixture_bad_ob_rows():
            try:
                parse_bybit_ob_line(1, obj)
                raise AssertionError(f"{name}: parsed but must refuse")
            except IngestError as e:
                assert e.code == expect[name], (name, e.code)
        return "all 5 malformed fixtures refused with the mapped error codes"

    def t10():
        stream = fixture_time_inverted_stream()
        recs = []
        for i, obj in enumerate(stream):
            recs.extend(parse_bybit_ob_line(i + 1, obj))
        records, _ = assemble(iter(recs))
        v = validate_stream(records)
        codes = {x[0] for x in v}
        assert "V-TIME-INVERSION" in codes, v
        return f"time inversion flagged: {[x for x in v if x[0]=='V-TIME-INVERSION'][0][:3]}"

    def t11():
        recs = parse_bybit_ob_line(1, fixture_unsorted_snapshot())
        records, _ = assemble(iter(recs))
        v = validate_stream(records)
        assert any(x[0] == "V-LEVEL-ORDER" and "asks not increasing" in str(x[2]) for x in v), v
        assert not any(x[0] == "V-TIME-INVERSION" for x in v), v
        return "unsorted asks flagged V-LEVEL-ORDER, no false time violation"

    def t12():
        # Tardis malformed rows: bad side and missing timestamp must refuse.
        base = {k: "" for k in TARDIS_CSV_COLUMNS}
        for patch, code in [
            ({"side": "middle", "timestamp": "1", "price": "1", "amount": "1"},
             "E-SIDE-UNKNOWN"),
            ({"side": "buy"}, "E-TS-NOT-INT"),
        ]:
            row = {**base, **patch}
            try:
                parse_tardis_row(1, row)
                raise AssertionError("parsed but must refuse: " + str(patch))
            except IngestError as e:
                assert e.code == code, (code, e.code)
        return "tardis malformed rows refused with mapped codes"

    t = [("PI1", "bybit snapshot parse, ranking, exact values", t01),
         ("PI2", "bybit dict-style delta upsert/delete", t02),
         ("PI3", "bybit pairs-style delta, empty-string delete", t03),
         ("PI4", "bybit publicTrade aggressor sides", t04),
         ("PI5", "tardis CSV snapshot/delta/delete", t05),
         ("PI6", "cross-format canonical equality", t06),
         ("PI7", "mixed-stream assembly and stats", t07),
         ("PI8", "clean stream validates clean", t08),
         ("PI9", "malformed bybit rows refused", t09),
         ("PI10", "time inversion flagged", t10),
         ("PI11", "unsorted snapshot flagged", t11),
         ("PI12", "malformed tardis rows refused", t12)]
    for tid, name, fn in t:
        check(tid, name, fn)

    all_pass = all(x["pass"] for x in tests)
    for x in tests:
        print(("PASS " if x["pass"] else "FAIL ") + x["id"] + ": " + x["name"] +
              (" | " + str(x["detail"]) if not x["pass"] else ""))
    report = {
        "module": "research/platform_ingest_v0.py",
        "phase": "P-1, charter Phase 1 (SYNTHETIC DATA ONLY)",
        "all_passed": all_pass,
        "n_tests": len(tests),
        "tests": tests,
        "record_schema_v0": ["seq", "venue", "symbol", "kind", "ts_ns", "side",
                             "level", "action", "price", "size"],
        "uncertainty_notes": [
            "Bybit archive field spellings and timestamp unit UNCERTAIN until "
            "the first real file (Phase 2, owner download); field maps are the "
            "single point of change; both known level styles supported.",
            "Tardis incremental_book_L2 column set per the public spec; "
            "timestamp unit (ms vs ns) to be confirmed on the first real file; "
            "the parser accepts only well-formed ints and refuses guesses.",
            "No market data was read or produced anywhere in this module or "
            "its tests; every fixture is hand-constructed synthetic.",
        ],
        "governance": "nothing registered, nothing evaluated, nothing booked; "
                      "C-E1 and all real-data gates untouched; charter Phase 1 gate honored.",
    }
    report["digest"] = hashlib.sha256(
        json.dumps({k: report[k] for k in report}, sort_keys=True,
                   default=str).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
    if report_path:
        with open(report_path, "w") as f:
            json.dump(report, f, indent=1, default=str)
        print("report: " + report_path + " digest=" + report["digest"])
    print("all_passed: " + str(all_pass))
    return report


if __name__ == "__main__":
    out = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "research/platform_ingest_v0_report.json"
    run_tests(out)

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/platform_ingest_v0.py

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/power_readout_h10_h11.py (19951 bytes)
"""Pre-signing power readout for Amendment 4 (H10a) and Amendment 5 (H11a).

Both amendments PRE-COMMIT: "Power readout FIRST: before execution, a power
analysis on unsealed data is computed and shown to Andrea. He may revise or
withdraw the amendment after seeing the power numbers but before any
outcome." This script is that readout. Stage 6c did the same for the battery
(stage6c_power_analysis.py); this extends it to the two unsigned amendments.

WHAT IS COMPUTED (and what is deliberately NOT):
  - H11a power: Monte Carlo of the registered stage2b v2 two-sample test
    (G2Top run starts, n = 11, vs the fresh-20w-high control pool outside
    G2 regimes, 4-week forward returns, B = 2000, block = 13, seed = 42,
    one-sided alpha 0.05). Synthetic event samples are drawn from the
    CONTROL POOL MARGINAL (unsealed, same convention as the executed and
    closed H8/H9 study) shifted by an ASSUMED effect. The real G2Top
    events' forward returns are NEVER read. The registered null is
    reproduced exactly (same seed, B, block, draw order as
    stage2b.cond_mean_p), precomputed once; event sampling uses a
    separate documented MC seed.
  - H10a calibration and power: Monte Carlo of the registered Fisher-z
    difference test (Spearman of sp_z_clv vs sp_z against forward 8-week
    realized vol, n = 299) on SURROGATE data calibrated to unsealed
    structural facts only: predictor intercorrelation r(sp_z, sp_z_clv),
    lag-1 persistence of sp_z, backward-8-week-vol persistence (the
    forward vol object is never computed on real data here). No real
    correlation between either predictor and any forward return/vol is
    computed anywhere in this script. The readout measures the test's
    FALSE-POSITIVE RATE at delta = 0 and its rejection rate under
    ASSUMED true differences in Spearman rho, for (a) the registered
    within-sample block bootstrap as drafted, (b) longer blocks, and
    (c) a circular-shift (Theiler-style) alignment null that preserves
    each series' persistence and randomizes only the alignment.
  - Unsealed only. No 2021+ forward return is computed by this script
    beyond what the executed H8/H9 convention already established for
    the control pool's boundary weeks.
  - No H10a or H11a outcome is computed. No rule, default, or frozen
    hypothesis is changed. This file informs Andrea's sign-off decision.

Interpretation note (H10a): the registered test says "demeaned stationary
block bootstrap on the paired weekly differences, B = 2000, block = 13,
seed = 42". This readout implements: resample weeks in paired stationary
blocks, recompute the Fisher-z difference per replicate, demean the
bootstrap distribution, one-sided p = frac(D_boot_demeaned >= D_obs),
alpha = 0.05. The final registered implementation must be pinned at
execution time; the interpretation is recorded in the report.

Run:  python3 research/power_readout_h10_h11.py
Output: research/power_readout_h10_h11_report.json (+ console summary)
"""
import json
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

REPO = Path("/home/user/projects/quant")
HOLDOUT_START = pd.Timestamp("2021-01-01")
B, BLOCK, SEED = 2000, 13, 42          # registered test constants
MC_SEED = 20261111                      # event/surrogate sampling (this readout)
ALPHA = 0.05


# ---------- stationary bootstrap -------------------------------------------
def stat_boot_draw_seq(n, m, block, rng):
    """One stationary-bootstrap index draw; EXACT copy of stage2b's
    stat_boot_draw (same rng consumption order)."""
    p = 1.0 / block
    pos = int(rng.integers(n))
    out = np.empty(m, dtype=int)
    for i in range(m):
        out[i] = pos
        if rng.random() < p:
            pos = int(rng.integers(n))
        else:
            pos = (pos + 1) % n
    return out


def registered_null_diff(control, n_e, b=B, block=BLOCK, seed=SEED):
    """Precompute the registered stage2b v2 null (seed 42, same draw order
    as cond_mean_p): per replicate null diff = mean(draw_E) - mean(draw_C).
    The registered test's rejection region is exactly frac(null <= obs)
    < alpha for top arms."""
    ct = np.asarray(control, float)
    n_c = len(ct)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    null = np.empty(b)
    for i in range(b):
        de = ct[stat_boot_draw_seq(n_c, n_e, block, rng)]
        dc = ct[stat_boot_draw_seq(n_c, n_c, block, rng)]
        null[i] = de.mean() - dc.mean()
    return null


def stat_boot_matrix(n, m, b, block, rng):
    """(b, m) stationary-bootstrap index matrix, vectorized: geometric
    block lengths, uniform positions, wrap-around. Used only for the
    Monte Carlo power estimation (registered stream not required)."""
    p = 1.0 / block
    out = np.empty((b, m), dtype=np.int64)
    filled = np.zeros(b, dtype=np.int64)
    while (filled < m).any():
        pend = np.flatnonzero(filled < m)
        L = rng.geometric(p, size=len(pend))
        start = rng.integers(n, size=len(pend))
        take = np.minimum(L, m - filled[pend])
        total = int(take.sum())
        ends = np.cumsum(take)
        starts_in_batch = np.concatenate(([0], ends[:-1]))
        offs = np.arange(total) - np.repeat(starts_in_batch, take)
        row = np.repeat(pend, take)
        col = np.repeat(filled[pend], take) + offs
        out[row, col] = (np.repeat(start, take) + offs) % n
        filled[pend] += take
    return out


# ---------- H10a surrogate machinery ----------------------------------------
def fisher_z(r):
    r = np.clip(r, -1 + 1e-12, 1 - 1e-12)
    return 0.5 * np.log((1 + r) / (1 - r))


def spearman_full(a, b):
    return float(pd.Series(a).corr(pd.Series(b), method="spearman"))


def spearman_rows(a, bmat):
    """Row-wise Spearman correlation of (b, n) a against (b, n) bmat.
    Ordinal ranks via double argsort: exact for the continuous Gaussian
    surrogates used here (ties have measure zero)."""
    ra = np.argsort(np.argsort(a, axis=1), axis=1).astype(float)
    rb = np.argsort(np.argsort(bmat, axis=1), axis=1).astype(float)
    ra -= ra.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)
    rb -= rb.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)
    num = (ra * rb).sum(axis=1)
    den = np.sqrt((ra ** 2).sum(axis=1) * (rb ** 2).sum(axis=1))
    return num / den


def spearman_vec(x, bmat):
    """Spearman of fixed vector x against each row of bmat."""
    rx = pd.Series(x).rank().to_numpy(float)
    rx -= rx.mean()
    sxx = float((rx ** 2).sum())
    rb = np.argsort(np.argsort(bmat, axis=1), axis=1).astype(float)
    rb -= rb.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)
    num = rb @ rx
    den = np.sqrt((rb ** 2).sum(axis=1) * sxx)
    return num / den


def ar1_levels(innov, phi):
    """Filter innovations into AR(1) levels (innov[0] seeds the chain)."""
    x = np.empty(len(innov))
    prev = 0.0
    for t, e in enumerate(innov):
        prev = phi * prev + e
        x[t] = prev
    return x


def make_surrogate(n, phi, r3, rho1, rho2, phie, rng, burn=200):
    """Returns (x, w, v) with the target correlation structure, or None.
    Both predictors share the same AR(1) phi and innovation scale; their
    innovations correlate at r3, giving level correlation ~r3 and unit
    stationary variance each."""
    nn = n + burn
    s_innov = np.sqrt(1 - phi ** 2)
    u1 = rng.standard_normal(nn)
    u2 = rng.standard_normal(nn)
    e1 = u1 * s_innov
    e2 = (r3 * u1 + np.sqrt(max(0.0, 1 - r3 ** 2)) * u2) * s_innov
    x = ar1_levels(e1, phi)[burn:]
    w = ar1_levels(e2, phi)[burn:]
    a_ = (rho1 - r3 * rho2) / (1 - r3 ** 2)
    b_ = (rho2 - r3 * rho1) / (1 - r3 ** 2)
    s2 = 1 - (a_ ** 2 + b_ ** 2 + 2 * a_ * b_ * r3)
    if s2 < 0:
        return None
    v = a_ * x + b_ * w + np.sqrt(s2) * ar1_levels(
        rng.standard_normal(nn) * np.sqrt(1 - phie ** 2), phie)[burn:]
    return x, w, v


def h10a_test_block(x, w, v, block, rng, b=B):
    """Registered interpretation: paired stationary block bootstrap,
    demeaned, one-sided. Returns (rejected, d_obs)."""
    d_obs = fisher_z(spearman_full(w, v)) - fisher_z(spearman_full(x, v))
    idx = stat_boot_matrix(len(v), len(v), b, block, rng)
    d_boot = (fisher_z(spearman_rows(w[idx], v[idx]))
              - fisher_z(spearman_rows(x[idx], v[idx])))
    d_null = d_boot - d_boot.mean()
    p = float((d_null >= d_obs).mean())
    return (p < ALPHA), d_obs


def h10a_test_shift(x, w, v):
    """Circular-shift (Theiler-style) alignment null: v is rolled by every
    non-zero shift; the null spread of D comes from broken alignments with
    each series' persistence preserved. Returns (rejected, d_obs)."""
    n = len(v)
    d_obs = fisher_z(spearman_full(w, v)) - fisher_z(spearman_full(x, v))
    rolls = np.stack([np.roll(v, s) for s in range(1, n)])
    d_shift = fisher_z(spearman_vec(w, rolls)) - fisher_z(spearman_vec(x, rolls))
    p = float((d_shift >= d_obs).mean())
    return (p < ALPHA), d_obs


# ---------- main -----------------------------------------------------------
def main():
    rep2b = json.load(open(REPO / "research" /
                           "stage2b_h8h9_eventstudy_report.json"))
    sigma = float(rep2b["weekly_sigma_unsealed"])

    df = pd.read_csv(REPO / "data" / "nq_weekly_canonical.csv",
                     parse_dates=["date"])
    av = pd.read_csv(REPO / "data" / "avfi_series.csv", parse_dates=["date"])
    v34 = pd.read_csv(REPO / "data" / "avfi_v34_series.csv",
                      parse_dates=["date"])
    pgt = pd.read_csv(REPO / "data" / "avfi_pgt_series.csv",
                      parse_dates=["date"])

    m = (df[["date", "close"]]
         .merge(av[["date", "sp_z"]], on="date")
         .merge(v34[["date", "sp_z_clv"]], on="date")
         .merge(pgt[["date", "newHigh", "g2Top"]], on="date"))
    un = m[m["date"] < HOLDOUT_START].copy()
    valid = un[un["sp_z"].notna() & un["sp_z_clv"].notna()].copy()
    assert (valid["date"] < HOLDOUT_START).all(), "HOLDOUT LEAK"
    n_valid = len(valid)

    x = valid["sp_z"].to_numpy(float)
    w = valid["sp_z_clv"].to_numpy(float)
    r3 = float(np.corrcoef(x, w)[0, 1])
    phi_x = float(np.corrcoef(x[:-1], x[1:])[0, 1])
    # backward 8-week realized vol persistence (structural; the forward
    # vol object is never computed on real data in this readout)
    lr = np.log(df["close"] / df["close"].shift(1))
    bv = (pd.DataFrame({"date": df["date"], "bv": lr.rolling(8).std()})
          .merge(valid[["date"]], on="date")["bv"].to_numpy(float))
    bv = bv[~np.isnan(bv)]
    phi_v = float(np.corrcoef(bv[:-1], bv[1:])[0, 1])

    # ---- H11a control pool (unsealed, stage2b convention) ----
    f4 = pd.DataFrame({"date": df["date"],
                       "f4": np.log(df["close"] / df["close"].shift(4))
                             .shift(-4)}).merge(valid[["date"]], on="date")
    valid = valid.assign(f4=f4["f4"].to_numpy(float))
    ctrl_mask = valid["newHigh"].to_numpy(bool) & (~valid["g2Top"].to_numpy(bool))
    ctrl = valid.loc[ctrl_mask, "f4"].to_numpy(float)
    ctrl = ctrl[~np.isnan(ctrl)]
    n_e, n_c = 11, len(ctrl)

    # registered null, exactly seed 42 / B 2000 / block 13 / draw order
    null_top = registered_null_diff(ctrl, n_e)
    print(f"registered H11a null: sd={null_top.std(ddof=1):.4f}, "
          f"q05={np.quantile(null_top, 0.05):.4f}", flush=True)

    mc = np.random.default_rng(MC_SEED)
    deltas = [0.0, 0.10, 0.20, 0.30, 0.50, 0.75, 1.00, 1.50, 2.00, 3.00]
    m_sims = 500
    h11 = {}
    for d in deltas:
        rej = 0
        for _ in range(m_sims):
            ev = ctrl[stat_boot_matrix(n_c, n_e, 1, BLOCK, mc)[0]]
            ev = ev - d * sigma          # top arm: weaker returns
            obs = ev.mean() - ctrl.mean()
            p = float((null_top <= obs).mean())
            rej += int(p < ALPHA)
        h11[f"delta_{d:.2f}sigma"] = {"power": round(rej / m_sims, 3),
                                      "mc_sims": m_sims}
        print(f"H11a  delta={d:.2f} sigma  power={rej / m_sims:.3f}",
              flush=True)
    pw = [h11[f"delta_{d:.2f}sigma"]["power"] for d in deltas]
    mde = None
    for i in range(1, len(deltas)):
        if pw[i] >= 0.80:
            mde = round(deltas[i - 1]
                        + (0.80 - pw[i - 1]) / (pw[i] - pw[i - 1])
                        * (deltas[i] - deltas[i - 1]), 2)
            break
    mde = mde if mde else "> 3.0"

    # ---- H10a surrogate readout ----
    bases = [0.10, 0.20, 0.30]
    hdeltas = [0.0, 0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.30]
    hm = 150

    # (a) registered interpretation as drafted (block 13), false positive
    # at delta 0 across bases, plus rejection rates under true deltas
    registered = {}
    for base in bases:
        for d in hdeltas:
            rng = np.random.default_rng(MC_SEED + 100 * int(base * 10) + 1)
            rej = used = 0
            for _ in range(hm):
                s = make_surrogate(n_valid, phi_x, r3, base, base + d,
                                   phi_v, rng)
                if s is None:
                    continue
                used += 1
                rej += int(h10a_test_block(s[0], s[1], s[2], BLOCK, rng)[0])
            registered[f"base_{base:.2f}_delta_{d:.2f}"] = {
                "rejection_rate": round(rej / max(1, used), 3),
                "mc_sims": used}
            print(f"H10a block13 base={base:.2f} delta={d:.2f} "
                  f"rej={rej / max(1, used):.3f}", flush=True)

    # (b) block-length sensitivity at delta 0, base 0.20
    blocks = [13, 67, 130, 260]
    blen = {}
    for blk in blocks:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(MC_SEED + 7)
        rej = used = 0
        for _ in range(hm):
            s = make_surrogate(n_valid, phi_x, r3, 0.20, 0.20, phi_v, rng)
            if s is None:
                continue
            used += 1
            rej += int(h10a_test_block(s[0], s[1], s[2], blk, rng)[0])
        blen[f"block_{blk}"] = {"false_positive_rate": round(rej / max(1, used), 3),
                                "mc_sims": used}
        print(f"H10a blocklen={blk} FP={rej / max(1, used):.3f}", flush=True)

    # (c) circular-shift alignment null: calibration + power
    shift = {}
    for base in bases:
        for d in hdeltas:
            rng = np.random.default_rng(MC_SEED + 200 + int(base * 10))
            rej = used = 0
            for _ in range(hm):
                s = make_surrogate(n_valid, phi_x, r3, base, base + d,
                                   phi_v, rng)
                if s is None:
                    continue
                used += 1
                rej += int(h10a_test_shift(s[0], s[1], s[2])[0])
            shift[f"base_{base:.2f}_delta_{d:.2f}"] = {
                "rejection_rate": round(rej / max(1, used), 3),
                "mc_sims": used}
            print(f"H10a shift base={base:.2f} delta={d:.2f} "
                  f"rej={rej / max(1, used):.3f}", flush=True)

    # surrogate validity checks (structure realized vs targeted), incl. the
    # predictor-vs-V correlations against their targets (would have caught
    # the innovation-scale bug of the first draft)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(MC_SEED + 999)
    lv_corrs, pers, rx_hit, rw_hit = [], [], [], []
    for _ in range(20):
        s = make_surrogate(n_valid, phi_x, r3, 0.20, 0.25, phi_v, rng)
        lv_corrs.append(float(np.corrcoef(s[0], s[1])[0, 1]))
        pers.append(float(np.corrcoef(s[0][:-1], s[0][1:])[0, 1]))
        rx_hit.append(spearman_full(s[0], s[2]))
        rw_hit.append(spearman_full(s[1], s[2]))
    validity = {
        "target_level_corr": round(r3, 4),
        "realized_level_corr_mean": round(float(np.mean(lv_corrs)), 4),
        "target_lag1": round(phi_x, 4),
        "realized_lag1_mean": round(float(np.mean(pers)), 4),
        "target_corr_xV_base_0.20": 0.20,
        "realized_spearman_xV_mean": round(float(np.mean(rx_hit)), 4),
        "target_corr_wV_base_0.25": 0.25,
        "realized_spearman_wV_mean": round(float(np.mean(rw_hit)), 4),
    }

    report = {
        "purpose": ("Pre-signing power readout for Amendment 4 (H10a) and "
                    "Amendment 5 (H11a). No outcome computed. Unsealed "
                    "data only."),
        "calibration": {
            "n_valid_unsealed_weeks": int(n_valid),
            "window": ("2015-04-10..2020-12-25 (inherited 500-wk "
                       "normalization)"),
            "predictor_intercorr_pearson": round(r3, 4),
            "predictor_intercorr_spearman": round(spearman_full(x, w), 4),
            "lag1_autocorr_sp_z": round(phi_x, 4),
            "lag1_autocorr_backward_8w_vol": round(phi_v, 4),
            "persistence_scale_weeks": round(1 / (1 - phi_x), 1),
            "weekly_sigma_stage2b_units": sigma,
            "h11a_control_pool_n": n_c,
            "h11a_control_f4_mean": round(float(ctrl.mean()), 5),
            "h11a_control_f4_sd": round(float(ctrl.std(ddof=1)), 5),
            "h11a_control_f4_sd_over_weekly_sigma":
                round(float(ctrl.std(ddof=1)) / sigma, 2),
            "h11a_n_events": n_e,
            "note": ("G2Top events' forward returns NEVER read; synthetic "
                     "events are control-pool draws plus assumed shifts."),
        },
        "surrogate_validity": validity,
        "h11a_g2top_power": {
            "test": ("registered stage2b v2 two-sample test, B=2000 "
                     "block=13 seed=42, one-sided alpha=0.05, top arm"),
            "registered_null_reproduced_exactly": True,
            "registered_null_sd": round(float(null_top.std(ddof=1)), 5),
            "event_sampling": (f"stationary block draws from control pool, "
                               f"MC seed {MC_SEED}, {m_sims} sims per delta"),
            "powers": h11,
            "minimum_detectable_effect_80pct_sigma": mde,
        },
        "h10a_readout": {
            "test_as_drafted": ("Fisher-z difference of Spearman "
                                "correlations, paired stationary block "
                                "bootstrap B=2000 block=13, demeaned, "
                                "one-sided alpha=0.05"),
            "headline": ("AS DRAFTED THE TEST IS MISCALIBRATED on series "
                         "with the registered structure: at delta=0 its "
                         "false-positive rate is far above the nominal 5% "
                         "because within-sample resampling with mean block "
                         "13 (vs a persistence scale of ~67 weeks) "
                         "understates the sampling variability of a "
                         "correlation between near-integrated series."),
            "registered_block13_rejection_rates": registered,
            "block_length_sensitivity_false_positive_delta0": blen,
            "circular_shift_null": {
                "test": ("D_obs = Fisher-z difference; null from all "
                         "non-zero circular shifts of the forward-vol "
                         "surrogate (Theiler-style alignment null, "
                         "persistence preserved); one-sided alpha=0.05"),
                "rejection_rates": shift,
                "note": ("correctly sized alternative; a revision of "
                         "Amendment 4 must pin a persistence-preserving "
                         "null before any execution"),
            },
            "caveat": ("surrogate calibration, not a claim about the real "
                       "correlations; the real rho values are unknown and "
                       "deliberately not computed"),
        },
        "discipline": {
            "holdout": "2021+ never evaluated; no forward vol computed",
            "outcomes": ("no H10a/H11a outcome computed; events' forward "
                         "returns never read; informs sign-off only"),
            "paper_trading_only": True,
        },
    }
    out = REPO / "research" / "power_readout_h10_h11_report.json"
    json.dump(report, open(out, "w"), indent=1)
    print("report written:", out)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/power_readout_h10_h11.py

const DATES: string[] = [
  "2000-09-22", "2000-09-29", "2000-10-06", "2000-10-13", "2000-10-20", "2000-10-27", 
  "2000-11-03", "2000-11-10", "2000-11-17", "2000-11-24", "2000-12-01", "2000-12-08", 
  "2000-12-15", "2000-12-22", "2000-12-29", "2001-01-05", "2001-01-12", "2001-01-19", 
  "2001-01-26", "2001-02-02", "2001-02-09", "2001-02-16", "2001-02-23", "2001-03-02", 
  "2001-03-09", "2001-03-16", "2001-03-23", "2001-03-30", "2001-04-06", "2001-04-13",

// (payload above is the same canonical pre-2021 closes as smh_horizon_ts_mirror.ts, verbatim)
const LOW = 0.01739;
const Z95 = 1.959963985;
const H = 12;

// ---- series, conventions 1:1 from smh_forecast/smh_theorem (as in the horizon mirror) ----
const T = CLOSE.length;
const r1: number[] = new Array(T).fill(NaN);
for (let t = 1; t < T; t++) r1[t] = Math.log(CLOSE[t] / CLOSE[t - 1]);

function buildSig(r: number[]): number[] {
  const s = new Array(T).fill(NaN);
  for (let t = 7; t < T; t++) {
    let m = 0;
    for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) m += r[t - 7 + k];
    m /= 8;
    let v = 0;
    for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) { const d = r[t - 7 + k] - m; v += d * d; }
    s[t] = Math.sqrt(v / 7);
  }
  return s;
}
function buildEv(r: number[], s: number[]): number[] {
  const e = new Array(T).fill(NaN);
  for (let t = 0; t < T - 1; t++) {
    if (!isNaN(s[t]) && !isNaN(r[t + 1])) e[t] = Math.abs(r[t + 1]) >= 2.5 * s[t] ? 1 : 0;
  }
  return e;
}
const sigW = buildSig(r1);
const evNext = buildEv(r1, sigW);

function personWeeksFrom(idx: number[]): { ep: number; h: number; e: number }[] {
  const rows: { ep: number; h: number; e: number }[] = [];
  idx.forEach((t0, ep) => {
    for (let h = 1; h <= H; h++) {
      if (t0 + h > T - 2) break;
      const e = evNext[t0 + h - 1];
      if (isNaN(e)) break;
      rows.push({ ep, h, e });
      if (e > 0) break;
    }
  });
  return rows;
}
function kmGreenwood(pw: { ep: number; h: number; e: number }[]) {
  let S = 1, acc = 0;
  for (let h = 1; h <= H; h++) {
    const sub = pw.filter(r => r.h === h);
    const n = sub.length, d = sub.filter(r => r.e > 0).length;
    if (n > 0) {
      S *= 1 - d / n;
      if (d > 0 && n - d > 0) acc += d / (n * (n - d));
    }
  }
  const cum = 1 - S;
  const se = Math.sqrt(S * S * acc);
  return { cum, se, nWindows: new Set(pw.map(r => r.ep)).size, personWeeks: pw.length };
}

const CLIM: number[] = [];
for (let t = 0; t < T; t++) if (!isNaN(sigW[t])) CLIM.push(t);

// C0 ANCHOR: the frozen overlapping pool
const c0 = kmGreenwood(personWeeksFrom(CLIM));
// entry-side anchor constants (for the disjointness readouts only, frozen by fiat)
const ENTRIES: number[] = [];
for (let t = 0; t < T; t++) {
  if (isNaN(sigW[t])) continue;
  const inLow = sigW[t] <= LOW;
  const prevLow = t > 0 && !isNaN(sigW[t - 1]) && sigW[t - 1] <= LOW;
  if (inLow && !prevLow) ENTRIES.push(t);
}
const entry = kmGreenwood(personWeeksFrom(ENTRIES));
const ENTRY_CUM12 = 0.634, ENTRY_LB = 0.487; // frozen constants, asserted below

// C1a/C1b: non-overlapping 12-week tiles, offsets 0 and 6
function tiles(offset: number): number[] {
  const out: number[] = [];
  for (let i = offset; i < CLIM.length; i += 12) out.push(CLIM[i]);
  return out;
}
const c1a = kmGreenwood(personWeeksFrom(tiles(0)));
const c1b = kmGreenwood(personWeeksFrom(tiles(6)));

// C2: era-block dispersion (10 blocks of 105 consecutive sig_w-valid decision weeks)
const BLOCK = 105, NB = Math.floor(CLIM.length / BLOCK);
const blockEst: number[] = [];
for (let b = 0; b < NB; b++) {
  const idx = CLIM.slice(b * BLOCK, (b + 1) * BLOCK);
  blockEst.push(kmGreenwood(personWeeksFrom(idx)).cum);
}
const bMean = blockEst.reduce((a, x) => a + x, 0) / NB;
const bVar = blockEst.reduce((a, x) => a + (x - bMean) * (x - bMean), 0) / (NB - 1);
const bSd = Math.sqrt(bVar);
const bSe = bSd / Math.sqrt(NB);

// C3: circular-shift seam diagnostic (persistence-preserving rotation of r1)
function mulberry32(seed: number) {
  let a = seed >>> 0;
  return function () {
    a |= 0; a = (a + 0x6D2B79F5) | 0;
    let t = Math.imul(a ^ (a >>> 15), 1 | a);
    t = (t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), 61 | t)) ^ t;
    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
  };
}
const B3 = 200;
const rnd = mulberry32(42);
const rotCum: number[] = [];
for (let b = 0; b < B3; b++) {
  const k = 26 + Math.floor(rnd() * (T - 52)); // shift in [26, T-26]
  const rRot = new Array(T).fill(NaN);
  for (let t = 1; t < T; t++) rRot[t] = r1[1 + ((t - 1 + k) % (T - 1))];
  const sRot = buildSig(rRot);
  const eRot = buildEv(rRot, sRot);
  // pooled climatology cum12 on the rotated series
  const valid: number[] = [];
  for (let t = 0; t < T; t++) if (!isNaN(sRot[t])) valid.push(t);
  const rows: { ep: number; h: number; e: number }[] = [];
  valid.forEach((t0, ep) => {
    for (let h = 1; h <= H; h++) {
      if (t0 + h > T - 2) break;
      const e = eRot[t0 + h - 1];
      if (isNaN(e)) break;
      rows.push({ ep, h, e });
      if (e > 0) break;
    }
  });
  rotCum.push(kmGreenwood(rows).cum);
}
const rotMean = rotCum.reduce((a, x) => a + x, 0) / B3;
const rotVar = rotCum.reduce((a, x) => a + (x - rotMean) * (x - rotMean), 0) / (B3 - 1);
const rotSd = Math.sqrt(rotVar);

function main() {
  const anchorChecks: Record<string, boolean> = {
    clim_pool_1050: CLIM.length === 1050,
    clim_cum12_0441: Math.abs(c0.cum - 0.4409) < 0.0005,
    clim_band_frozen: Math.abs((c0.cum - Z95 * c0.se) - 0.411) < 0.002 && Math.abs((c0.cum + Z95 * c0.se) - 0.471) < 0.002,
    entries_41: ENTRIES.length === 41,
    entry_cum12_0634: Math.abs(entry.cum - 0.634) < 0.0005,
    entry_lb_0487: Math.abs((entry.cum - Z95 * entry.se) - 0.487) < 0.002,
    disjoint_frozen: entry.cum - Z95 * entry.se > c0.cum + Z95 * c0.se,
  };
  const failed = Object.entries(anchorChecks).filter(([, v]) => !v).map(([k]) => k);
  if (failed.length) throw new Error("ANCHOR FAILED: " + failed.join(", "));
  const q = (xs: number[], p: number) => {
    const s = [...xs].sort((a, b) => a - b);
    return s[Math.min(s.length - 1, Math.floor(p * s.length))];
  };
  const rep = {
    session: 62,
    date: "2026-11-12",
    kind: "smh_climato_grid (climatology-counterfactual dependence audit)",
    window: "unsealed pre-2021 design bookkeeping only (date < 2021-01-01)",
    weeks: T,
    span: [DATES[0], DATES[T - 1]],
    anchor: "frozen pool reproduced (1050 windows, clim cum12 0.4409, Greenwood band [0.411, 0.471]; entry 41/0.634, lb 0.487, disjoint true)",
    entry_constants_unchanged_BY_FIAT: true,
    C0_overlapping_pool: {
      n_windows: c0.nWindows, person_weeks: c0.personWeeks, cum12: +c0.cum.toFixed(4),
      se_indep: +c0.se.toFixed(4),
      band_indep: [+(c0.cum - Z95 * c0.se).toFixed(3), +(c0.cum + Z95 * c0.se).toFixed(3)],
    },
    C1a_tiles_offset0: {
      n_windows: c1a.nWindows, cum12: +c1a.cum.toFixed(4), se: +c1a.se.toFixed(4),
      band: [+(c1a.cum - Z95 * c1a.se).toFixed(3), +(c1a.cum + Z95 * c1a.se).toFixed(3)],
      disjoint_vs_entry_lb: ENTRY_LB < c1a.cum + Z95 * c1a.se,
    },
    C1b_tiles_offset6: {
      n_windows: c1b.nWindows, cum12: +c1b.cum.toFixed(4), se: +c1b.se.toFixed(4),
      band: [+(c1b.cum - Z95 * c1b.se).toFixed(3), +(c1b.cum + Z95 * c1b.se).toFixed(3)],
      disjoint_vs_entry_lb: ENTRY_LB < c1b.cum + Z95 * c1b.se,
    },
    C2_era_blocks: {
      block_weeks: BLOCK, n_blocks: NB,
      block_estimates: blockEst.map(x => +x.toFixed(3)),
      block_min: +Math.min(...blockEst).toFixed(3),
      block_max: +Math.max(...blockEst).toFixed(3),
      mean: +bMean.toFixed(4), sd_across_blocks: +bSd.toFixed(4),
      se_of_mean: +bSe.toFixed(4),
      band_level: [+(bMean - Z95 * bSd).toFixed(3), +(bMean + Z95 * bSd).toFixed(3)],
      band_mean: [+(bMean - Z95 * bSe).toFixed(3), +(bMean + Z95 * bSe).toFixed(3)],
      era_share_above_entry_lb: +(blockEst.filter(x => x >= ENTRY_LB).length / NB).toFixed(3),
      n_eff_from_block_sd: +(0.4409 * (1 - 0.4409) / (bSd * bSd)).toFixed(0),
    },
    C3_circshift_diagnostic: {
      B: B3, seed: 42, shifts_in: [26, T - 26],
      mean: +rotMean.toFixed(4), sd: +rotSd.toFixed(5),
      pct2_5: +q(rotCum, 0.025).toFixed(4), pct97_5: +q(rotCum, 0.975).toFixed(4),
      verdict_hint: "near-zero sd expected: the pooled statistic is a path functional, rotation nearly invariant; the surrogate family cannot size its uncertainty - recorded as a null-tool limit",
    },
  };
  console.log(JSON.stringify(rep, null, 2));
  return rep;
}

// research/smh_entrydep_ts_mirror.ts - the SMH ENTRY-SIDE DEPENDENCE AUDIT
// Authored and EXECUTED 13 Nov 2026 (session 67, PROGRESS block 75).
// Doc: docs/smh_robustness_v0.md section 11. Unsealed pre-2021 design
// bookkeeping ONLY: every number filters date < 2021-01-01; the spent NQ
// holdout 2021+ is never touched; nothing feeds the forward protocol, the
// kill lines, the frozen probabilities or any rule set; the frozen
// definition (8-week sig_w ddof=1, 2.5-sigma bar, LOW cut 0.01739, 12-week
// horizon, the 41-entry cross set) is unchanged BY FIAT; ALL cells
// reported, none selected.
//
// The session-62 grid audited the RIVAL's band construction; this mirror
// audits the CLAIM's own: the entry KM's Greenwood band [0.4867, 0.7816]
// treats the 41 episodes as independent. Cells (one knob, the entry-side
// dependence/variance construction):
//   C0 the frozen anchor (asserted identical to the frozen constants);
//   C1 era-block dispersion (10 two-year blocks; per-era KM cum12;
//      across-era mean/sd ddof=1 over the K eras with entries;
//      dependence-honest band mean +/- 1.96*sd/sqrt(K));
//   C2 leave-one-era-out pooled cum12, all 10 reported;
//   C3 declustered KM (pre-committed rule: an entry joins the previous
//      entry's cluster iff within 8 weeks of it; anchors = first entries).
//
// PAYLOAD PROVENANCE: the 41-entry outcome table (entry date, era, terminal
// person-week h, outcome), derived from data/nq_weekly_canonical.csv under
// the frozen constructions. The derivation instrument (a shell/awk scratch,
// /tmp only, never a programme artifact) reproduced the frozen anchor
// EXACTLY before this table was frozen: 41 entries, 327 person-weeks,
// 26 first events, cum12 0.6341, band [0.4867, 0.7816], cum4 0.2683,
// median lag 5. Every person-week row is exactly reconstructible from
// (term, outc): rows h = 1..term, event = 1 only at h == term iff
// outc == 1. The pandas counterpart (smh_robust.py entrydep_cells) instead
// recomputes the table from the canonical data and asserts equality with
// the constants frozen from THIS execution (fix-before-freeze, first run
// pending the next Python-enabled reset; lives inside the existing
// smh_robust verify_all check; the chain stays at 24 checks).
//
// HONEST COMPUTATION LOG (recorded in the doc): the first scratch
// instrument (awk) hit two interpreter defects before any number was
// frozen: (1) a wrong CSV column (low instead of close) - caught
// immediately by the C0 anchor mismatch (47 entries instead of 41); (2)
// the sandbox awk mishandles `continue` inside nested for-loops (the outer
// loop terminates), confirmed by a minimal repro - all `continue` uses were
// replaced with if-block nesting before the anchor pass. The final anchor
// pass, and this mirror's C0 (including the cell-for-cell KM hazard table
// [41/2, 39/3, 36/3, 33/3, 30/3, 27/4, 23/2, 21/0, 21/1, 20/1, 19/2, 17/2]),
// are the frozen numbers.

type Ent = { date: string; era: number; term: number; outc: number };
const ENTRIES: Ent[] = [
["2004-01-16",1,11,1],["2004-03-05",1,4,1],["2004-11-26",1,6,1],
["2005-03-04",2,6,1],["2005-06-10",2,12,0],["2005-08-19",2,12,0],["2005-12-02",2,5,1],
["2005-12-23",2,2,1],["2006-03-24",2,7,1],["2006-11-10",2,12,0],["2006-12-15",2,11,1],
["2007-04-27",3,12,0],["2007-10-12",3,4,1],
["2009-05-08",4,3,1],["2009-09-11",4,12,0],["2010-03-26",4,6,1],["2010-10-29",4,12,0],["2010-12-10",4,12,0],
["2011-05-13",5,7,1],["2011-06-17",5,2,1],["2012-02-10",5,5,1],["2012-07-27",5,9,1],["2012-11-02",5,3,1],
["2013-03-01",6,1,1],["2013-06-14",6,4,1],["2013-06-28",6,2,1],["2013-08-09",6,10,1],["2013-09-06",6,6,1],
["2014-06-06",6,12,0],["2014-10-17",6,1,1],["2014-12-26",6,12,0],
["2015-06-12",7,5,1],["2016-04-01",7,12,0],["2016-08-19",7,3,1],["2016-11-11",7,12,0],
["2017-01-27",8,12,0],["2017-08-25",8,12,0],["2018-01-19",8,12,0],["2018-06-01",8,12,0],
["2019-02-15",9,12,1],["2019-09-27",9,12,1],
].map(r => ({ date: r[0] as string, era: r[1] as number, term: r[2] as number, outc: r[3] as number }));

const Z = 1.959963985;

function personWeeks(ents: Ent[]): { h: number; ev: number }[] {
  const rows: { h: number; ev: number }[] = [];
  for (const e of ents) for (let h = 1; h <= e.term; h++) rows.push({ h, ev: h === e.term && e.outc === 1 ? 1 : 0 });
  return rows;
}
function kmGreen(rows: { h: number; ev: number }[]) {
  let S = 1, acc = 0; const table: [number, number][] = [];
  for (let h = 1; h <= 12; h++) {
    const at = rows.filter(r => r.h === h);
    const n = at.length, d = at.reduce((s, r) => s + r.ev, 0);
    table.push([n, d]);
    if (n > 0) { S *= 1 - d / n; if (d > 0 && n - d > 0) acc += d / (n * (n - d)); }
  }
  let S4 = 1; for (let h = 1; h <= 4; h++) { const [n, d] = table[h - 1]; if (n > 0) S4 *= 1 - d / n; }
  return { cum12: 1 - S, cum4: 1 - S4, se: S * Math.sqrt(acc), table };
}

function main() {
  const out: any = {};
  const rows0 = personWeeks(ENTRIES);
  const c0 = kmGreen(rows0);
  const events = ENTRIES.filter(e => e.outc === 1);
  const lags = events.map(e => e.term).sort((a, b) => a - b);
  const med = lags.length % 2 ? lags[(lags.length - 1) / 2] : (lags[lags.length / 2 - 1] + lags[lags.length / 2]) / 2;
  const anchor_ok = ENTRIES.length === 41 && rows0.length === 327 && events.length === 26
    && Math.abs(c0.cum12 - 0.6341) < 5e-4 && Math.abs((c0.cum12 - Z * c0.se) - 0.4867) < 5e-4
    && Math.abs((c0.cum12 + Z * c0.se) - 0.7816) < 5e-4 && Math.abs(c0.cum4 - 0.2683) < 5e-4 && med === 5;
  out.C0 = { entries: ENTRIES.length, pw: rows0.length, events: events.length,
    cum12: +c0.cum12.toFixed(4), band: [+(c0.cum12 - Z * c0.se).toFixed(4), +(c0.cum12 + Z * c0.se).toFixed(4)],
    cum4: +c0.cum4.toFixed(4), median_lag: med, anchor_ok: anchor_ok, km_table: c0.table };

  const eras: any[] = []; const eraCum: number[] = [];
  for (let E = 0; E <= 9; E++) {
    const sub = ENTRIES.filter(e => e.era === E);
    if (sub.length === 0) { eras.push({ era: E, entries: 0, pw: 0, events: 0, cum12: null, band: [null, null] }); continue; }
    const km = kmGreen(personWeeks(sub));
    eraCum.push(km.cum12);
    eras.push({ era: E, entries: sub.length, pw: personWeeks(sub).length,
      events: sub.filter(e => e.outc === 1).length, cum12: +km.cum12.toFixed(4),
      band: [+(km.cum12 - Z * km.se).toFixed(4), +(km.cum12 + Z * km.se).toFixed(4)] });
  }
  const K = eraCum.length;
  const mean = eraCum.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / K;
  const sd = Math.sqrt(eraCum.reduce((s, v) => s + (v - mean) * (v - mean), 0) / (K - 1));
  out.C1 = { eras, K, mean: +mean.toFixed(4), sd: +sd.toFixed(4),
    dep_band: [+(mean - Z * sd / Math.sqrt(K)).toFixed(4), +(mean + Z * sd / Math.sqrt(K)).toFixed(4)] };

  out.C2 = [];
  for (let E = 0; E <= 9; E++) {
    const sub = ENTRIES.filter(e => e.era !== E);
    const km = kmGreen(personWeeks(sub));
    out.C2.push({ drop_era: E, entries: sub.length, events: sub.filter(e => e.outc === 1).length, cum12: +km.cum12.toFixed(4) });
  }
  const looVals = out.C2.filter((c: any) => c.entries < 41).map((c: any) => c.cum12);
  out.C2_range = [+Math.min(...looVals).toFixed(4), +Math.max(...looVals).toFixed(4)];

  const dsecs = ENTRIES.map(e => Date.parse(e.date + "T00:00:00Z") / (7 * 86400 * 1000));
  const cluster: number[] = [0];
  for (let i = 1; i < ENTRIES.length; i++) cluster.push(dsecs[i] - dsecs[i - 1] <= 8 ? cluster[i - 1] : cluster[i - 1] + 1);
  const ncl = cluster[cluster.length - 1] + 1;
  const anchors: number[] = [];
  ENTRIES.forEach((_, i) => { if (i === 0 || cluster[i] !== cluster[i - 1]) anchors.push(i); });
  const sub = anchors.map(i => ENTRIES[i]);
  const km = kmGreen(personWeeks(sub));
  const sizes: number[] = []; cluster.forEach(c => sizes[c] = (sizes[c] || 0) + 1);
  out.C3 = { clusters: ncl, anchors: anchors.length, sizes, pw: personWeeks(sub).length,
    events: sub.filter(e => e.outc === 1).length, cum12: +km.cum12.toFixed(4),
    band: [+(km.cum12 - Z * km.se).toFixed(4), +(km.cum12 + Z * km.se).toFixed(4)] };

  out.rival = { era_band_upper: 0.499, era_estimates_range: [0.286, 0.638],
    entry_dep_band_clears_rival_era_upper: out.C1.dep_band[0] > 0.499 };
  return out;
}
console.log(JSON.stringify(main(), null, 1));
