##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/smh_forecast.py (18053 bytes)
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""smh_forecast.py - the Salient Market Hypothesis forward-protocol instrument.

Shipped 12 Nov 2026 (session 33, PROGRESS block 41). Tooling ONLY: this file
does not evaluate anything, does not run tests, does not trade, and does not
make inferential claims. It drafts the forward forecast rows that the hazard
layer's protocol (docs/smh_hazard_v0.md, section 5) logs in advance when a
compression entry occurs at a decision close, and it validates its own frozen
tables against the canonical data on every selftest.

Provenance of every frozen constant below: unsealed pre-2021 design
bookkeeping only (date filter < 2021-01-01), computed in sessions 31-32 and
recorded in docs/smh_hazard_v0.md (corrected table) and docs/smh_cox_v01.md.
The holdout 2021+ is SPENT and is never touched by the design tables; live
weekly data is read ONLY to answer the descriptive question "is this week a
compression entry", which is the condition for logging a forecast row.

Subcommands:
  status    describe the latest decision week's clock state (no evaluation)
  draft     emit the forecast row if the latest week is a compression entry;
            refuses with a reason otherwise (the protocol logs entries, not noise)
  --append  with draft: append the row to data/smh_forward_log.csv, guarded:
            refuses unless run on the Sunday decision cadence, on a fresh
            entry week, and if that entry week is not already logged
  selftest  recompute the KM/direction tables from the canonical pre-2021
            data and assert equality with the embedded constants, including
            the standing person-week consistency check (session 32 policy)
"""
import sys
import json
import datetime as dt
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

pd.set_option("future.no_silent_downcasting", True)

REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
CANONICAL = REPO / "data" / "nq_weekly_canonical.csv"
AVFI = REPO / "data" / "avfi_series.csv"
FORWARD_LOG = REPO / "data" / "smh_forward_log.csv"
DESIGN_END = "2021-01-01"  # unsealed design bookkeeping horizon (never moves)

# ---- frozen constants (docs/smh_hazard_v0.md, corrected table, session 32) ----
LOW = 0.01739          # frozen pre-2021 low-tercile vol cutpoint
KM_CUM_4W = 0.268      # KM cumulative first-event probability at h=4
KM_CUM_12W = 0.634     # ... at h=12
N_ENTRIES = 41         # true compression entries pre-2021
N_FIRST_EVENTS = 26    # entries with a first significant week within 12w
MEDIAN_LAG = 5
N_PERSON_WEEKS = 327
KM_HAZARDS = {  # h -> (at_risk, events)
    1: (41, 2), 2: (39, 3), 3: (36, 3), 4: (33, 3), 5: (30, 3), 6: (27, 4),
    7: (23, 2), 8: (21, 0), 9: (21, 1), 10: (20, 1), 11: (19, 2), 12: (17, 2),
}
# direction of FIRST event, by entry condition (up, down) - frozen, all flagged
DIR_ALL = (17, 9)
DIR_CELLS = {  # key -> (up, down, flag)
    "mom12>0": (11, 8, "small n"),
    "mom12<=0": (6, 1, "SEDUCTIVE CELL - flagged, untrusted until forward-scored"),
    "reversal_down": (9, 3, "small n, suggestive"),
    "momentum_up": (3, 3, "small n, no signal"),
    "reversal_up": (2, 0, "n=2, no content"),
    "momentum_down": (1, 1, "n=2, no content"),
}

# ---- frozen constants (docs/smh_stress_clock_v0.md, session 34) ----
HIGH = 0.02697         # frozen pre-2021 high-tercile vol cutpoint
STRESS_CUM_4W = 0.273  # KM cumulative de-stress probability at h=4
STRESS_CUM_12W = 0.636  # ... at h=12
N_STRESS_ENTRIES = 33
N_DESTRESS_EVENTS = 21
MEDIAN_STRESS_LAG = 6
N_STRESS_PERSON_WEEKS = 258
STRESS_KM = {  # h -> (at_risk, events)
    1: (33, 3), 2: (30, 4), 3: (26, 1), 4: (25, 1), 5: (24, 0), 6: (24, 2),
    7: (22, 2), 8: (20, 4), 9: (16, 2), 10: (14, 2), 11: (12, 0), 12: (12, 0),
}
# ---- frozen constants (docs/smh_transitions_v0.md, session 35) ----
# region -> (spells, median dwell, mean dwell, max dwell) in weeks, pre-2021
REGION_DWELL = {
    "compression": (41, 4, 7.05, 29),
    "mid": (66, 3, 4.86, 16),
    "stress": (33, 8, 13.33, 86),
}

FORWARD_LOG_HEADER = [
    "logged_at_utc", "forecast_kind", "entry_week", "sig_w", "state", "mom12",
    "depth_log", "p_within_4w", "p_within_12w", "p_up_uncond", "up_n_uncond",
    "cell_state", "p_up_cell", "cell_n", "cell_flag",
    "resolution_4w_date", "event_4w", "dir_4w",
    "resolution_12w_date", "event_12w", "dir_12w",
    "brier_4w", "logloss_4w", "brier_12w", "logloss_12w", "notes",
]


def load_series():
    df = pd.read_csv(CANONICAL, parse_dates=["date"])
    st = pd.read_csv(AVFI, parse_dates=["date"])[["date", "state"]]
    df = df.merge(st, on="date", how="left")
    df["r1"] = np.log(df["close"] / df["close"].shift(1))
    df["sig_w"] = df["r1"].rolling(8).std(ddof=1)
    df["mom12"] = np.log(df["close"] / df["close"].shift(12))
    df["ev_next"] = (df["r1"].shift(-1).abs() >= 2.5 * df["sig_w"]).astype(float)
    df.loc[df["sig_w"].isna() | df["r1"].shift(-1).isna(), "ev_next"] = np.nan
    return df


def compression_entries(df):
    in_low = df["sig_w"] <= LOW
    prev_low = in_low.shift(1).fillna(False).astype(bool)  # session-31 dtype lesson
    return df.index[in_low & (~prev_low) & df["sig_w"].notna()].tolist()


def person_weeks(df, idx):
    rows = []
    for i, t0 in enumerate(idx):
        for h in range(1, 13):
            if t0 + h > len(df) - 2:
                break
            e = df.loc[t0 + h - 1, "ev_next"]
            if np.isnan(e):
                break
            rows.append(dict(ep=i, t0=t0, h=h, event=int(e > 0)))
            if e > 0:
                break
    return pd.DataFrame(rows)


def km_from_person_weeks(pw):
    out = {}
    for h in range(1, 13):
        sub = pw[pw["h"] == h]
        out[h] = (len(sub), int(sub["event"].sum()))
    return out


def cmd_selftest():
    df = load_series()
    pre = df[df["date"] < DESIGN_END].copy().reset_index(drop=True)
    idx = compression_entries(pre)
    pw = person_weeks(pre, idx)
    problems = []
    if len(idx) != N_ENTRIES:
        problems.append(f"entries {len(idx)} != {N_ENTRIES}")
    if len(pw) != N_PERSON_WEEKS:
        problems.append(f"person-weeks {len(pw)} != {N_PERSON_WEEKS}")
    km = km_from_person_weeks(pw)
    if km != KM_HAZARDS:
        problems.append(f"KM table drift: {km}")
    if sum(v[0] for v in km.values()) != len(pw):
        problems.append("standing consistency check failed: sum(at_risk) != person-weeks")
    cum, S = {}, 1.0
    for h in range(1, 13):
        n, ev = km[h]
        S *= (1 - ev / n)
        cum[h] = 1 - S
    if abs(cum[4] - KM_CUM_4W) > 0.0015 or abs(cum[12] - KM_CUM_12W) > 0.0015:
        problems.append(f"cum drift: h4={cum[4]:.4f} h12={cum[12]:.4f}")
    first = pw[pw["event"] == 1].drop_duplicates("ep")
    if len(first) != N_FIRST_EVENTS or int(first["h"].median()) != MEDIAN_LAG:
        problems.append(f"first events {len(first)}/median {first['h'].median()}")
    dirs = []
    for _, r in first.iterrows():
        dirs.append(int(np.sign(pre.loc[int(r["t0"]) + int(r["h"]), "r1"])))
    up_all = sum(1 for d in dirs if d > 0)
    if (up_all, len(dirs) - up_all) != DIR_ALL:
        problems.append(f"direction all: {up_all}/{len(dirs)-up_all} != {DIR_ALL}")
    cell_actual = {}
    for key in DIR_CELLS:
        ups = downs = 0
        for ep, t0 in enumerate(idx):
            if ep not in first["ep"].values:
                continue
            h = int(first[first["ep"] == ep]["h"].iloc[0])
            d = int(np.sign(pre.loc[t0 + h, "r1"]))
            mom = pre.loc[t0, "mom12"]
            st = pre.loc[t0, "state"]
            hit = (key == "mom12>0" and mom > 0) or (key == "mom12<=0" and mom <= 0) \
                or (key == "reversal_down" and st == "reversal_down") \
                or (key == "momentum_up" and st == "momentum_up") \
                or (key == "reversal_up" and st == "reversal_up") \
                or (key == "momentum_down" and st == "momentum_down")
            if hit:
                if d > 0:
                    ups += 1
                else:
                    downs += 1
        cell_actual[key] = (ups, downs)
    for key, (u, d, _flag) in DIR_CELLS.items():
        if cell_actual[key] != (u, d):
            problems.append(f"cell {key}: {cell_actual[key]} != {(u, d)}")
    # ---- stress side (docs/smh_stress_clock_v0.md, session 34) ----
    pre["destress_next"] = (pre["sig_w"].shift(-1) <= HIGH).astype(float)
    pre.loc[pre["sig_w"].isna() | pre["sig_w"].shift(-1).isna(), "destress_next"] = np.nan
    in_high = pre["sig_w"] > HIGH
    prev_high = in_high.shift(1).fillna(False).astype(bool)
    sidx = pre.index[in_high & (~prev_high) & pre["sig_w"].notna()].tolist()
    if len(sidx) != N_STRESS_ENTRIES:
        problems.append(f"stress entries {len(sidx)} != {N_STRESS_ENTRIES}")
    spw_rows = []
    for i, t0 in enumerate(sidx):
        for h in range(1, 13):
            if t0 + h > len(pre) - 2:
                break
            e = pre.loc[t0 + h - 1, "destress_next"]
            if np.isnan(e):
                break
            spw_rows.append(dict(ep=i, h=h, event=int(e > 0)))
            if e > 0:
                break
    spw = pd.DataFrame(spw_rows)
    if len(spw) != N_STRESS_PERSON_WEEKS:
        problems.append(f"stress person-weeks {len(spw)} != {N_STRESS_PERSON_WEEKS}")
    skm = km_from_person_weeks(spw)
    if skm != STRESS_KM:
        problems.append(f"stress KM drift: {skm}")
    if sum(v[0] for v in skm.values()) != len(spw):
        problems.append("stress consistency check failed: sum(at_risk) != person-weeks")
    s_cum, S2 = {}, 1.0
    for h in range(1, 13):
        n, ev = skm[h]
        S2 *= (1 - ev / n)
        s_cum[h] = 1 - S2
    if abs(s_cum[4] - STRESS_CUM_4W) > 0.0015 or abs(s_cum[12] - STRESS_CUM_12W) > 0.0015:
        problems.append(f"stress cum drift: h4={s_cum[4]:.4f} h12={s_cum[12]:.4f}")
    sfirst = spw[spw["event"] == 1].drop_duplicates("ep")
    if len(sfirst) != N_DESTRESS_EVENTS or int(sfirst["h"].median()) != MEDIAN_STRESS_LAG:
        problems.append(f"de-stress events {len(sfirst)}/median {sfirst['h'].median()}")
    # ---- dwell tables (docs/smh_transitions_v0.md, session 35) ----
    regions = [region_of(s) for s in pre["sig_w"]]
    spells = []
    cur = start = None
    for i, v in enumerate(regions):
        if v is None:
            if cur is not None:
                spells.append((cur, start, i - 1))
                cur = None
        elif v != cur:
            if cur is not None:
                spells.append((cur, start, i - 1))
            cur, start = v, i
    if cur is not None:
        spells.append((cur, start, len(regions) - 1))
    for r, (n_sp, med, mean, mx) in REGION_DWELL.items():
        d = np.array([e - s + 1 for (rr, s, e) in spells if rr == r])
        if len(d) != n_sp or int(np.median(d)) != med or abs(d.mean() - mean) > 0.05 or int(d.max()) != mx:
            problems.append(f"dwell {r}: {len(d)} spells, median {np.median(d)}, "
                            f"mean {d.mean():.2f}, max {d.max()} != {(n_sp, med, mean, mx)}")
    if problems:
        print("SELFTEST FAIL")
        for p in problems:
            print("  -", p)
        return 1
    print("SELFTEST PASS: compression side (41 entries, 327 person-weeks, KM table, "
          "cum h4/h12, 26 first events, median lag 5, direction table) and stress side "
          "(33 entries, 258 person-weeks, KM table, cum h4/h12, 21 de-stress events, "
          "median lag 6) all reproduced from the canonical pre-2021 data; standing "
          "consistency checks hold on both clocks.")
    return 0


def spell_age(values, idx_end):
    """weeks in the spell of the value at idx_end (walk back over equal values;
    NaN breaks). Descriptive live context only - NOT a forecast."""
    cur = values[idx_end]
    if pd.isna(cur):
        return None, None
    age = 1
    i = idx_end - 1
    while i >= 0 and values[i] == cur:
        age += 1
        i -= 1
    return cur, age


def region_of(s):
    if pd.isna(s):
        return None
    return "compression" if s <= LOW else ("stress" if s > HIGH else "mid")


def cmd_status():
    df = load_series()
    t = len(df) - 1
    sig, prev_sig = df.loc[t, "sig_w"], df.loc[t - 1, "sig_w"]
    print(f"latest decision week: {df.loc[t, 'date'].date()}  close={df.loc[t, 'close']}")
    print(f"sig_w={sig:.5f}  LOW cut={LOW}  HIGH cut={HIGH}  (prev week sig_w={prev_sig:.5f})")
    in_low = bool(sig <= LOW)
    in_high = bool(sig > HIGH)
    c_entry = bool(in_low and prev_sig > LOW and pd.notna(sig))
    s_entry = bool(in_high and prev_sig <= HIGH and pd.notna(sig))
    region = "compression" if in_low else ("stress" if in_high else "mid")
    print(f"clock region: {region.upper()}   compression ENTRY: {'YES' if c_entry else 'NO'}   stress ENTRY: {'YES' if s_entry else 'NO'}")
    # spell-age context: where the machine is in its measured cycle
    regions = [region_of(s) for s in df["sig_w"]]
    r_now, r_age = spell_age(regions, len(regions) - 1)
    if r_now is not None:
        n_sp, med, mean, mx = REGION_DWELL[r_now]
        print(f"current {r_now} spell age: {r_age} week(s)  (pre-2021 spells: n={n_sp}, "
              f"median {med}, mean {mean:.1f}, max {mx} weeks)")
    if pd.notna(df.loc[t, "state"]):
        states = list(df["state"])
        s_now, s_age = spell_age(states, len(states) - 1)
        if s_now is not None:
            print(f"current state spell: {s_now}, age {s_age} week(s)  (state dwell tables: docs/smh_transitions_v0.md)")
    print("spell age is descriptive context against frozen pre-2021 tables, NOT a forecast row")
    if c_entry or s_entry:
        print("draft a forecast row: python3 research/smh_forecast.py draft")
    else:
        print("no forecast row (the protocol logs clock entries, not noise)")
    return 0


def build_row(df):
    t = len(df) - 1
    sig, prev_sig = df.loc[t, "sig_w"], df.loc[t - 1, "sig_w"]
    if not pd.notna(sig):
        return None, f"no valid sig_w at {df.loc[t, 'date'].date()}"
    compression_entry = bool(sig <= LOW and prev_sig > LOW)
    stress_entry = bool(sig > HIGH and prev_sig <= HIGH)
    state = df.loc[t, "state"] if pd.notna(df.loc[t, "state"]) else "NA"
    mom12 = float(df.loc[t, "mom12"]) if pd.notna(df.loc[t, "mom12"]) else float("nan")
    kind = "compression_first_event" if compression_entry else (
        "stress_de_stress" if stress_entry else None)
    if kind is None:
        return None, (f"NO CLOCK ENTRY at {df.loc[t, 'date'].date()}: "
                      f"sig_w={sig:.5f} (LOW={LOW}, HIGH={HIGH})")
    row = {
        "logged_at_utc": "",  # stamped at append time
        "forecast_kind": kind,
        "entry_week": str(df.loc[t, "date"].date()),
        "sig_w": f"{sig:.5f}",
        "state": state,
        "mom12": f"{mom12:.4f}" if pd.notna(mom12) else "NA",
        "p_up_uncond": "", "up_n_uncond": "",
        "cell_state": "NA", "p_up_cell": "", "cell_n": "", "cell_flag": "",
        "notes": "frozen pre-2021 KM tables; descriptive row; no inferential claim",
    }
    if kind == "compression_first_event":
        depth = float(np.log(sig / LOW))
        row["depth_log"] = f"{depth:.4f}"
        row["p_within_4w"] = f"{KM_CUM_4W:.3f}"
        row["p_within_12w"] = f"{KM_CUM_12W:.3f}"
        up_all, dn_all = DIR_ALL
        row["p_up_uncond"] = f"{up_all / (up_all + dn_all):.3f}"
        row["up_n_uncond"] = f"{up_all}/{up_all + dn_all}"
        cell_key = str(state) if str(state) in DIR_CELLS else None
        if cell_key:
            u, d, flag = DIR_CELLS[cell_key]
            row["cell_state"] = cell_key
            row["p_up_cell"] = f"{u / (u + d):.3f}"
            row["cell_n"] = f"{u}/{u + d}"
            row["cell_flag"] = flag
    else:  # stress_de_stress
        row["depth_log"] = f"{float(np.log(sig / HIGH)):.4f}"
        row["p_within_4w"] = f"{STRESS_CUM_4W:.3f}"
        row["p_within_12w"] = f"{STRESS_CUM_12W:.3f}"
        row["notes"] = ("frozen pre-2021 stress KM; no direction split (none earned; "
                        "momentum and state do not condition the stress clock)")
    return row, None


def cmd_draft(append=False):
    df = load_series()
    row, err = build_row(df)
    if row is None:
        print(f"REFUSED: {err}")
        return 1
    row["logged_at_utc"] = dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")
    print("## SMH forward-protocol forecast row (draft, logged in advance)")
    print(json.dumps(row, indent=2))
    if not append:
        print("draft only; append in a Sunday session with: draft --append")
        return 0
    today = dt.date.today()
    entry_date = dt.date.fromisoformat(row["entry_week"])
    guards = []
    if today.weekday() != 6:
        guards.append(f"not Sunday (today {today.isoformat()} is {today.strftime('%A')})")
    if (today - entry_date).days > 7:
        guards.append(f"entry week {entry_date} not fresh")
    if FORWARD_LOG.exists():
        existing = pd.read_csv(FORWARD_LOG, dtype=str)
        if row["entry_week"] in set(existing["entry_week"]):
            guards.append(f"entry week {row['entry_week']} already logged (append-only)")
    else:
        existing = None
    if guards:
        print("REFUSED --append: " + "; ".join(guards))
        return 1
    if existing is None:
        pd.DataFrame([row], columns=FORWARD_LOG_HEADER).to_csv(FORWARD_LOG, index=False)
    else:
        pd.concat([existing, pd.DataFrame([row], columns=FORWARD_LOG_HEADER)],
                  ignore_index=True).to_csv(FORWARD_LOG, index=False)
    print(f"APPENDED to data/smh_forward_log.csv (entry week {row['entry_week']})")
    return 0


def main():
    args = [a for a in sys.argv[1:] if not a.startswith("-")]
    append = "--append" in sys.argv
    if not args:
        print(__doc__)
        return 1
    if args[0] == "selftest":
        return cmd_selftest()
    if args[0] == "status":
        return cmd_status()
    if args[0] == "draft":
        return cmd_draft(append=append)
    print(f"unknown subcommand: {args[0]}")
    return 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/smh_forecast.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""smh_gates.py - the SMH forward proof-schedule monitor.

Authored 14 Nov 2026 (session 68, PROGRESS block 76). Tooling ONLY. This
tool turns the pre-declared proof schedule of docs/smh_proof_criteria_v0.md
(sessions 44/46, frozen inside smh_theorem.py's PROOF/READOUT constants)
into a REPORT-ONLY instrument that applies it mechanically to the forward
log (data/smh_forward_log.csv, written by smh_forecast.py --append and
resolved by smh_score.py resolve). It NEVER writes, never drafts, never
books, never revises a probability, and it computes NO new statistic: every
verdict it prints is a mechanical restatement of a rule that was frozen
before the first forward row existed. A verdict here is bookkeeping, not
an inferential claim; the forward log is currently EMPTY BY DESIGN and the
first row waits for the market's next compression/stress cross.

Schedule implemented (all thresholds embedded verbatim from
smh_theorem.py's frozen constants; provenance: sessions 44/46,
docs/smh_proof_criteria_v0.md sections 4-8):

  OCCURRENCE GATES (per claim kind, counted on RESOLVED 12-week rows in
  log order, evaluated at the EXACT milestones only):
    compression_first_event: kill lines 3/8/19/31 at 10/20/40/60 rows
    stress_de_stress:         kill lines 3/8/19/31 at 10/20/40/60 rows
    verdict per armed gate: ALIVE if events K > kill line, KILLED if
    K <= kill line; the claim status is sticky (once KILLED at any gate
    it stays KILLED; gates between milestones are NOT-ARMED and are never
    interpolated).
  DIRECTION PEEKS (counted on resolved forward FIRST EVENTS, i.e. rows
  with event_12w == 1 and dir_12w filled; examined at exactly n = 40 and
  n = 60 first events):
    n=40: promoted if up >= 27; down-bias annotation if up <= 13
    n=60: promoted if up >= 39; down-bias annotation if up <= 21
    otherwise: no action; direction stays REFUSED until a promotion.
  READOUTS (counted on resolved windows of each claim kind, at exactly
  n = 26/52/104 windows; the paired fixed-forecast comparison reduces to
  thresholds on the event count K, sessions 44/46 arithmetic):
    comp_12w   : logloss win K >= 15/29/57, Brier win K >= 14/28/56
    comp_4w    : logloss win K >= 6/12/23,  Brier win K >= 6/12/23
    stress_12w : logloss win K >= 15/30/60, Brier win K >= 15/30/60
    stress_4w  : logloss win K <= 9/19/39,  Brier win K <= 10/20/40
    the stress_4w win side is the flipped side (the design forecast sits
    below the coin). THE POSITIVE PROOF (comp_12w at n=104) = K >= 57 of
    104 resolved compression 12-week windows on BOTH rules.
  CALIBRATION EXPECTATIONS (descriptive context at readouts, never a
    test): the frozen per-window logloss/Brier expectations under
    design-true and rival-true worlds (CALIB / SCALIB in smh_theorem).

CONSTANT PROVENANCE: the numbers below are copied verbatim from
research/smh_theorem.py (the frozen, selftest-asserted proof constants).
This file deliberately does NOT import smh_theorem at runtime because
that module pulls pandas/numpy/scipy; this monitor is STDLIB-ONLY so its
status mode can run on any machine (including the bookkeeping sandbox)
without the scientific stack. The deferred parity check below
(`check` mode, needs the stack on a Python-enabled reset) asserts
constant-for-constant equality against smh_theorem and MUST pass before
this tool's verdicts are trusted as the Sunday readout.

Subcommands:
  status    report the whole pre-declared schedule against the real
            forward log (absent/empty log = the honest EMPTY state: all
            gates NOT-ARMED, zero rows, schedule printed in full)
  selftest  in-memory synthetic logs validate every boundary of every
            rule (stdlib only; no network, no bundle access)
  check     deferred: import smh_theorem and assert the embedded
            constants equal the frozen ones (needs pandas/scipy; run on
            the next Python-enabled reset before first live use)

Discipline: NO evaluation of any hypothesis (the schedule is the frozen
pre-registration, not a new test); NO forward statistics invented; nothing
booked; nothing written; the 2021+ NQ holdout is SPENT and never read;
kill lines count RESOLVED ROWS, never calendar years.
"""

# ---------------- log reading (stdlib only, append-only source) ----------
def read_log():
    """Return list of dict rows (str values) or None if the log is absent."""
    if not FORWARD_LOG.exists():
        return None
    with open(FORWARD_LOG, newline="") as fh:
        return list(csv.DictReader(fh))


def is_resolved(row, window):
    """True if the row's `window` (4w/12w) outcome columns are filled."""
    return bool((row.get(f"event_{window}") or "").strip())


def event_flag(row, window):
    return (row.get(f"event_{window}") or "").strip() == "1"


def dir_flag(row, window):
    return (row.get(f"dir_{window}") or "").strip() in ("up", "1", "UP")


# ---------------- the schedule arithmetic (pure) ------------------------
def occurrence_gates(rows, kind, kill_lines):
    """Evaluate the milestone kill gates for one claim kind.

    rows: the full log (list of dict); kind: the forecast_kind string.
    Returns dict with per-milestone verdicts and the sticky claim status.
    Evaluated at the EXACT milestones n in kill_lines, using the first n
    resolved 12w rows in log order; K = events among them.
    """
    resolved = [r for r in rows
                if r.get("forecast_kind") == kind and is_resolved(r, "12w")]
    events = [event_flag(r, "12w") for r in resolved]
    gates = {}
    status = "NOT-ARMED"          # no milestone reached yet
    for n, kstar in sorted(kill_lines.items()):
        if len(events) < n:
            gates[n] = dict(armed=False, n=n, kill=kstar, k=None,
                            verdict="NOT-ARMED")
            continue
        k = sum(events[:n])
        verdict = "KILLED" if k <= kstar else "ALIVE"
        gates[n] = dict(armed=True, n=n, kill=kstar, k=k, verdict=verdict)
        if verdict == "KILLED":
            status = "KILLED"     # sticky
        elif status == "NOT-ARMED":
            status = "ALIVE"
        elif status == "ALIVE":
            pass                  # stays alive
        # if already KILLED, stays KILLED (sticky, no resurrection)
    return dict(kind=kind, n_resolved=len(events),
                k_total=sum(events), gates=gates, status=status)


def direction_peeks(rows):
    """The two pre-declared direction peeks on resolved forward first events.

    First events = resolved 12w rows with event_12w == 1 and dir_12w filled.
    Peeks at exactly n = 40 and n = 60 first events.
    """
    firsts = [r for r in rows
              if is_resolved(r, "12w") and event_flag(r, "12w")
              and (r.get("dir_12w") or "").strip()]
    ups = [dir_flag(r, "12w") for r in firsts]
    peeks = {}
    for n, (promo, downbias) in ((40, DIR_GATE_40), (60, DIR_GATE_60)):
        if len(ups) < n:
            peeks[n] = dict(armed=False, n=n, up=None, total=n,
                            promo=promo, downbias=downbias,
                            verdict="NOT-ARMED")
            continue
        up = sum(ups[:n])
        if up >= promo:
            verdict = "PROMOTED"
        elif up <= downbias:
            verdict = "DOWN-BIAS-ANNOTATION"
        else:
            verdict = "NO-ACTION (direction stays refused)"
        peeks[n] = dict(armed=True, n=n, up=up, total=n,
                        promo=promo, downbias=downbias, verdict=verdict)
    return dict(n_first_events=len(ups), up_total=sum(ups), peeks=peeks)


def readout_check(rows, claim, kind, window):
    """The fixed-forecast readout thresholds at n = 26/52/104 windows.

    claim: key into READOUT_* tables; kind/window select the rows.
    Win side: K >= threshold, or K <= threshold when READOUT_LL_UPPER.
    """
    resolved = [r for r in rows
                if r.get("forecast_kind") == kind and is_resolved(r, window)]
    flags = [event_flag(r, window) for r in resolved]
    out = {}
    for i, n in enumerate(READOUT_N):
        if len(flags) < n:
            out[n] = dict(armed=False, n=n, k=None)
            continue
        k = sum(flags[:n])
        ll_thr = READOUT_LL_K[claim][i]
        br_thr = READOUT_BR_K[claim][i]
        upper = READOUT_LL_UPPER[claim]
        ll_win = (k <= ll_thr) if upper else (k >= ll_thr)
        # the Brier threshold sits on the same win side as the logloss
        # threshold for each claim (the fixed-forecast midpoint and
        # cross-entropy thresholds share the design's side; stress_4w is
        # flipped on both)
        br_win = (k <= br_thr) if upper else (k >= br_thr)
        out[n] = dict(armed=True, n=n, k=k, ll_thr=ll_thr, br_thr=br_thr,
                      ll_win=ll_win, br_win=br_win,
                      both=ll_win and br_win)
    return dict(claim=claim, n_windows=len(flags), k_total=sum(flags),
                thresholds=out)


def calibration_context(resolved_12w, claim):
    """Descriptive: realized mean brier/logloss vs the frozen expectations.

    Never a test; the readout thresholds are the test.
    """
    if not resolved_12w:
        return None
    def mean(col):
        vals = []
        for r in resolved_12w:
            v = (r.get(col) or "").strip()
            try:
                vals.append(float(v))
            except ValueError:
                pass
        return (sum(vals) / len(vals)) if vals else None
    exp = CALIB if claim.startswith("comp") else SCALIB
    return dict(n=len(resolved_12w),
                realized_brier_12w=mean("brier_12w"),
                realized_logloss_12w=mean("logloss_12w"),
                expected_smh_brier=exp["brier_smh"],
                expected_smh_logloss=exp["h_smh"])


# ---------------- status (report-only) ----------------------------------
def build_report(rows):
    """The full schedule applied to the log (rows=None -> EMPTY state)."""
    if rows is None:
        rows = []
    comp = occurrence_gates(rows, KIND_COMP, KILL_LINES)
    stress = occurrence_gates(rows, KIND_STRESS, STRESS_KILL)
    direction = direction_peeks(rows)
    readouts = dict(
        comp_12w=readout_check(rows, "comp_12w", KIND_COMP, "12w"),
        comp_4w=readout_check(rows, "comp_4w", KIND_COMP, "4w"),
        stress_12w=readout_check(rows, "stress_12w", KIND_STRESS, "12w"),
        stress_4w=readout_check(rows, "stress_4w", KIND_STRESS, "4w"),
    )
    calib = dict(
        comp=calibration_context(
            [r for r in rows if r.get("forecast_kind") == KIND_COMP
             and is_resolved(r, "12w")], "comp"),
        stress=calibration_context(
            [r for r in rows if r.get("forecast_kind") == KIND_STRESS
             and is_resolved(r, "12w")], "stress"),
    )
    return dict(n_rows=len(rows),
                comp=comp, stress=stress, direction=direction,
                readouts=readouts, calibration=calib)


def cmd_status():
    rows = read_log()
    rep = build_report(rows)
    print("SMH FORWARD PROOF-SCHEDULE MONITOR (report-only; verdicts are")
    print("mechanical restatements of the pre-declared rules, never claims)")
    if rows is None:
        print("Forward log: ABSENT (no clock entry yet; empty by design)")
    else:
        print(f"Forward log rows: {rep['n_rows']}")
    for claim, oc in (("compression_first_event", rep["comp"]),
                      ("stress_de_stress", rep["stress"])):
        print(f"\n{claim}: {oc['n_resolved']} resolved 12w rows, "
              f"{oc['k_total']} events -> claim status {oc['status']}")
        fk = GATE_FK if claim.startswith("compression") else SGATE_FK
        for n, g in sorted(oc["gates"].items()):
            if g["armed"]:
                print(f"  gate n={n:>2}: K={g['k']:>2} vs kill {g['kill']}"
                      f" -> {g['verdict']}")
            else:
                print(f"  gate n={n:>2}: NOT-ARMED "
                      f"(kill {g['kill']}; needs {n} resolved rows)")
    print(f"\nDirection ({rep['direction']['n_first_events']} resolved "
          f"forward first events, {rep['direction']['up_total']} up):")
    for n, p in sorted(rep["direction"]["peeks"].items()):
        if p["armed"]:
            print(f"  peek n={n}: up {p['up']}/{p['total']}"
                  f" -> {p['verdict']}")
        else:
            print(f"  peek n={n}: NOT-ARMED (promote {p['promo']}, "
                  f"down-bias {p['downbias']})")
    print("\nReadouts (fixed-forecast thresholds on the event count K):")
    for claim, ro in rep["readouts"].items():
        line = f"  {claim}: {ro['n_windows']} resolved windows, K={ro['k_total']}"
        for n, t in sorted(ro["thresholds"].items()):
            if t["armed"]:
                line += (f" | n={n}: K={t['k']} ll{'<=' if READOUT_LL_UPPER[claim] else '>='}"
                         f"{t['ll_thr']} ({'WIN' if t['ll_win'] else 'LOSE'}), "
                         f"br{'<=' if claim == 'stress_4w' else '>='}{t['br_thr']} "
                         f"({'WIN' if t['br_win'] else 'LOSE'})"
                         + (" [BOTH]" if t["both"] else ""))
            else:
                line += f" | n={n}: NOT-ARMED"
        print(line)
    pp = rep["readouts"]["comp_12w"]["thresholds"][104]
    if pp.get("armed"):
        print(f"  POSITIVE PROOF (comp_12w at 104): "
              f"{'MET' if pp['both'] else 'NOT MET'} (needs K >= 57)")
    else:
        print("  POSITIVE PROOF (comp_12w at 104): NOT-ARMED "
              "(57 of 104 resolved compression windows)")
    print("\nCalibration context (descriptive, never a test):")
    for side, c in rep["calibration"].items():
        if c is None:
            print(f"  {side}: no resolved 12w rows")
        else:
            print(f"  {side}: n={c['n']}, realized brier12 "
                  f"{c['realized_brier_12w'] if c['realized_brier_12w'] is not None else 'NA'}"
                  f" vs design-expected {c['expected_smh_brier']}, "
                  f"logloss12 "
                  f"{c['realized_logloss_12w'] if c['realized_logloss_12w'] is not None else 'NA'}"
                  f" vs {c['expected_smh_logloss']}")
    print("\nSeductive-cell register (CLOSED; examined only at "
          f">= {CELL_MIN_N} forward rows in the cell):")
    for cell in CELL_REGISTER:
        print(f"  - {cell}")
    print("\nDiscipline: kill lines count RESOLVED ROWS, never years; a")
    print("verdict is bookkeeping; the log is append-only; nothing was")
    print("written by this tool.")
    return 0


# ---------------- selftest (in-memory, stdlib only) --------------------
def synth_rows(kind, n12, k12, n4=0, k4=0, dirs=None):
    """Synthetic resolved log rows. dirs: list of 'up'/'down' for the k12
    event rows (12w first events carry dir_12w)."""
    rows = []
    for i in range(n12):
        ev = 1 if i < k12 else 0
        d = dirs[i] if (dirs and i < len(dirs) and ev == 1) else ""
        r = {k: "" for k in LOG_HEADER}
        r["forecast_kind"] = kind
        r["entry_week"] = f"2026-01-{(i % 28) + 1:02d}"
        r["event_12w"] = "1" if ev else "0"
        r["dir_12w"] = d
        rows.append(r)
    for j in range(n4):
        r = {k: "" for k in LOG_HEADER}
        r["forecast_kind"] = kind
        r["event_4w"] = "1" if j < k4 else "0"
        rows.append(r)
    return rows


def expect(problems, cond, msg):
    if not cond:
        problems.append(msg)
    return cond


def cmd_selftest():
    problems = []
    # T1: absent/empty log -> EMPTY state, nothing armed, no crash
    rep = build_report([])
    expect(problems, rep["comp"]["status"] == "NOT-ARMED", "T1 comp status")
    expect(problems, rep["stress"]["status"] == "NOT-ARMED", "T1 stress")
    expect(problems, all(not g["armed"] for g in rep["comp"]["gates"].values()),
           "T1 comp gates not armed")
    expect(problems, rep["readouts"]["comp_12w"]["thresholds"][104]["armed"]
           is False, "T1 readout not armed")

    # T2: design-truth compression log, 60 resolved, 38 events -> ALIVE all
    rows = synth_rows(KIND_COMP, 60, 38)
    oc = occurrence_gates(rows, KIND_COMP, KILL_LINES)
    expect(problems, oc["status"] == "ALIVE", "T2 status ALIVE")
    expect(problems, all(g["verdict"] == "ALIVE" for g in oc["gates"].values()
                        if g["armed"]), "T2 all armed gates alive")
    expect(problems, oc["gates"][60]["k"] == 38, "T2 K at 60")

    # T3: every compression gate boundary (kill = KILLED, kill+1 = ALIVE)
    for n, kstar in sorted(KILL_LINES.items()):
        oc_k = occurrence_gates(synth_rows(KIND_COMP, n, kstar),
                               KIND_COMP, KILL_LINES)
        expect(problems, oc_k["status"] == "KILLED",
               f"T3 killed at n={n} K={kstar}")
        oc_a = occurrence_gates(synth_rows(KIND_COMP, n, kstar + 1),
                               KIND_COMP, KILL_LINES)
        expect(problems, oc_a["status"] == "ALIVE",
               f"T3 alive at n={n} K={kstar + 1}")

    # T4: sticky - killed at gate 1, later rows cannot resurrect
    rows = synth_rows(KIND_COMP, 10, 3) + synth_rows(KIND_COMP, 50, 50)
    oc = occurrence_gates(rows, KIND_COMP, KILL_LINES)
    expect(problems, oc["status"] == "KILLED", "T4 sticky KILLED")
    expect(problems, oc["gates"][10]["verdict"] == "KILLED", "T4 gate1 fired")

    # T5: stress de-stress boundaries
    for n, kstar in sorted(STRESS_KILL.items()):
        oc_k = occurrence_gates(synth_rows(KIND_STRESS, n, kstar),
                               KIND_STRESS, STRESS_KILL)
        expect(problems, oc_k["status"] == "KILLED", f"T5 stress kill n={n}")

    # T6: readout boundaries
    # comp_12w at 26: K=14 -> brier win (>=14), logloss lose (<15)
    ro = readout_check(synth_rows(KIND_COMP, 26, 14), "comp_12w", KIND_COMP, "12w")
    t = ro["thresholds"][26]
    expect(problems, t["br_win"] and not t["ll_win"], "T6 comp 26 K=14")
    ro = readout_check(synth_rows(KIND_COMP, 26, 15), "comp_12w", KIND_COMP, "12w")
    expect(problems, ro["thresholds"][26]["both"], "T6 comp 26 K=15 both")
    # comp_12w at 104: K=56 -> brier win, logloss lose; K=57 -> BOTH (proof)
    ro = readout_check(synth_rows(KIND_COMP, 104, 56), "comp_12w", KIND_COMP, "12w")
    t = ro["thresholds"][104]
    expect(problems, t["br_win"] and not t["ll_win"], "T6 comp 104 K=56")
    ro = readout_check(synth_rows(KIND_COMP, 104, 57), "comp_12w", KIND_COMP, "12w")
    expect(problems, ro["thresholds"][104]["both"], "T6 comp 104 K=57 proof")
    # stress_4w flipped side at 104: K=39 -> ll win (<=39), br win (<=40)
    ro = readout_check(synth_rows(KIND_STRESS, 0, 0, n4=104, k4=39),
                       "stress_4w", KIND_STRESS, "4w")
    t = ro["thresholds"][104]
    expect(problems, t["ll_win"] and t["br_win"], "T6 stress4w K=39")
    # K=40 -> ll lose, br win; K=41 -> both lose
    ro = readout_check(synth_rows(KIND_STRESS, 0, 0, n4=104, k4=40),
                       "stress_4w", KIND_STRESS, "4w")
    t = ro["thresholds"][104]
    expect(problems, t["br_win"] and not t["ll_win"], "T6 stress4w K=40")
    ro = readout_check(synth_rows(KIND_STRESS, 0, 0, n4=104, k4=41),
                       "stress_4w", KIND_STRESS, "4w")
    t = ro["thresholds"][104]
    expect(problems, not t["ll_win"] and not t["br_win"], "T6 stress4w K=41")

    # T7: direction peeks
    # 40 first events, 27 up -> PROMOTED; 26 up -> NO-ACTION; 13 -> DOWN-BIAS
    dirs27 = ["up"] * 27 + ["down"] * 13
    d = direction_peeks(synth_rows(KIND_COMP, 40, 40, dirs=dirs27))
    expect(problems, d["peeks"][40]["verdict"] == "PROMOTED", "T7 promote 27/40")
    dirs26 = ["up"] * 26 + ["down"] * 14
    d = direction_peeks(synth_rows(KIND_COMP, 40, 40, dirs=dirs26))
    expect(problems, d["peeks"][40]["verdict"] == "NO-ACTION (direction stays refused)",
           "T7 no-action 26/40")
    dirs13 = ["up"] * 13 + ["down"] * 27
    d = direction_peeks(synth_rows(KIND_COMP, 40, 40, dirs=dirs13))
    expect(problems, d["peeks"][40]["verdict"] == "DOWN-BIAS-ANNOTATION",
           "T7 down-bias 13/40")
    # 60 first events, 39 up -> promoted at the second peek
    dirs39 = ["up"] * 39 + ["down"] * 21
    d = direction_peeks(synth_rows(KIND_COMP, 60, 60, dirs=dirs39))
    expect(problems, d["peeks"][60]["verdict"] == "PROMOTED", "T7 promote 39/60")

    # T8: milestone isolation - rows of the other kind never leak in
    rows = synth_rows(KIND_STRESS, 20, 20)
    oc = occurrence_gates(rows, KIND_COMP, KILL_LINES)
    expect(problems, oc["n_resolved"] == 0 and oc["status"] == "NOT-ARMED",
           "T8 kind isolation")
    # T9: unresolved rows never count
    rows = [dict((k, "") for k in LOG_HEADER)]
    rows[0]["forecast_kind"] = KIND_COMP
    oc = occurrence_gates(rows, KIND_COMP, KILL_LINES)
    expect(problems, oc["n_resolved"] == 0, "T9 unresolved excluded")

    # T10: readout table integrity (monotone thresholds, win sides)
    for claim in READOUT_LL_K:
        expect(problems, list(READOUT_LL_K[claim]) ==
               sorted(READOUT_LL_K[claim]), f"T10 ll monotone {claim}")
    expect(problems, READOUT_LL_UPPER["stress_4w"] is True
           and READOUT_LL_UPPER["comp_12w"] is False, "T10 win sides")

    if problems:
        print("SELFTEST FAIL:")
        for p in problems:
            print("  -", p)
        return 1
    print("SELFTEST PASS: 4 gate-boundary pairs x 2 claims, sticky kill,")
    print("  readout boundaries (comp 26/104, stress_4w flipped side),")
    print("  direction peeks (27/26/13 of 40, 39 of 60), kind isolation,")
    print("  unresolved exclusion, threshold monotonicity - all verified")
    print("  in-memory; the real log was never touched.")
    return 0


def cmd_check():
    """Deferred parity check: embedded constants == smh_theorem's frozen
    constants. Needs the scientific stack (pandas/scipy) - run on the
    next Python-enabled reset before first live use of this tool."""
    import smh_theorem as T
    problems = []
    pairs = [
        ("KILL_LINES", KILL_LINES, T.KILL_LINES),
        ("STRESS_KILL", STRESS_KILL, T.STRESS_KILL),
        ("GATE_FK", GATE_FK, T.GATE_FK),
        ("GATE_PWR", GATE_PWR, T.GATE_PWR),
        ("SGATE_FK", SGATE_FK, T.SGATE_FK),
        ("SGATE_PWR", SGATE_PWR, T.SGATE_PWR),
        ("CUM_FK", CUM_FK, T.CUM_FK),
        ("CALIB", CALIB, T.CALIB),
        ("SCALIB", SCALIB, T.SCALIB),
        ("DIR_GATE_40", DIR_GATE_40, T.DIR_GATE_40),
        ("DIR_GATE_60", DIR_GATE_60, T.DIR_GATE_60),
        ("CELL_MIN_N", CELL_MIN_N, T.CELL_MIN_N),
        ("READOUT_N", READOUT_N, T.READOUT_N),
        ("READOUT_LL_K", READOUT_LL_K, T.READOUT_LL_K),
        ("READOUT_LL_UPPER", READOUT_LL_UPPER, T.READOUT_LL_UPPER),
        ("READOUT_BR_K", READOUT_BR_K, T.READOUT_BR_K),
        ("READOUT_LL_PW", READOUT_LL_PW, T.READOUT_LL_PW),
        ("READOUT_LL_FP", READOUT_LL_FP, T.READOUT_LL_FP),
        ("READOUT_BR_PW", READOUT_BR_PW, T.READOUT_BR_PW),
        ("READOUT_BR_FP", READOUT_BR_FP, T.READOUT_BR_FP),
        ("KM_CUM_12W", KM_CUM_12W, T.KM_CUM_12W),
        ("STRESS_CUM_12W", STRESS_CUM_12W, T.STRESS_CUM_12W),
        ("CLIM_CUM_12W", CLIM_CUM_12W, T.CLIM_CUM_12W),
    ]
    for name, mine, ref in pairs:
        if dict(mine) != dict(ref) if isinstance(mine, dict) else \
                (list(mine) != list(ref) if isinstance(mine, tuple)
                 else mine != ref):
            problems.append(f"{name}: embedded {mine} != smh_theorem {ref}")
    if problems:
        print("CHECK FAIL:")
        for p in problems:
            print("  -", p)
        return 1
    print("CHECK PASS: all embedded proof constants equal smh_theorem's")
    print("frozen, selftest-asserted constants (23 constant groups).")
    return 0


def main():
    mode = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "status"
    if mode == "status":
        return cmd_status()
    if mode == "selftest":
        return cmd_selftest()
    if mode == "check":
        return cmd_check()
    print("usage: smh_gates.py [status|selftest|check]")
    return 2


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/smh_gates_ts_mirror.ts (8417 bytes)
// smh_gates_ts_mirror.ts - independent TS mirror of smh_gates.py
// (session 68, 14 Nov 2026). Per the lesson-14 convention (independent
// parity checks substitute for code review), this mirror re-implements the
// gate/readout/direction arithmetic of research/smh_gates.py from the
// FROZEN smh_theorem constants and runs the same boundary fixtures. It
// reads NO files and NO network: everything is in-memory. If this mirror
// and the Python selftest agree on every fixture, the two independent
// implementations validate each other; the real forward log is untouched
// (and currently absent by design).
//
// Embedded verbatim from smh_theorem.py (frozen proof constants):
const KILL_LINES: Record<number, number> = { 10: 3, 20: 8, 40: 19, 60: 31 };
const STRESS_KILL: Record<number, number> = { 10: 3, 20: 8, 40: 19, 60: 31 };
const DIR_GATE_40 = [27, 13];
const DIR_GATE_60 = [39, 21];
const READOUT_N = [26, 52, 104];
const READOUT_LL_K: Record<string, [number, number, number]> = {
  comp_12w: [15, 29, 57], comp_4w: [6, 12, 23],
  stress_12w: [15, 30, 60], stress_4w: [9, 19, 39],
};
const READOUT_LL_UPPER: Record<string, boolean> = {
  comp_12w: false, comp_4w: false, stress_12w: false, stress_4w: true,
};
const READOUT_BR_K: Record<string, [number, number, number]> = {
  comp_12w: [14, 28, 56], comp_4w: [6, 12, 23],
  stress_12w: [15, 30, 60], stress_4w: [10, 20, 40],
};

type Row = { kind: string; ev12?: number; dir12?: string; ev4?: number };

function occurrenceGates(rows: Row[], kind: string, kill: Record<number, number>) {
  const evs = rows.filter(r => r.kind === kind && r.ev12 !== undefined)
    .map(r => r.ev12 === 1);
  let status = "NOT-ARMED";
  const gates: Record<number, { armed: boolean; k?: number; verdict: string }> = {};
  for (const n of Object.keys(kill).map(Number).sort((a, b) => a - b)) {
    const kstar = kill[n];
    if (evs.length < n) { gates[n] = { armed: false, verdict: "NOT-ARMED" }; continue; }
    const k = evs.slice(0, n).filter(Boolean).length;
    const verdict = k <= kstar ? "KILLED" : "ALIVE";
    gates[n] = { armed: true, k, verdict };
    if (verdict === "KILLED") status = "KILLED";
    else if (status === "NOT-ARMED") status = "ALIVE";
  }
  return { status, gates, nResolved: evs.length };
}

function directionPeeks(rows: Row[]) {
  const firsts = rows.filter(r => r.ev12 === 1 && r.dir12 !== undefined && r.dir12 !== "")
    .map(r => r.dir12 === "up");
  const peeks: Record<number, { armed: boolean; up?: number; verdict: string }> = {};
  for (const [n, g] of [[40, DIR_GATE_40], [60, DIR_GATE_60]] as [number, number[]][]) {
    if (firsts.length < n) { peeks[n] = { armed: false, verdict: "NOT-ARMED" }; continue; }
    const up = firsts.slice(0, n).filter(Boolean).length;
    let verdict = "NO-ACTION (direction stays refused)";
    if (up >= g[0]) verdict = "PROMOTED";
    else if (up <= g[1]) verdict = "DOWN-BIAS-ANNOTATION";
    peeks[n] = { armed: true, up, verdict };
  }
  return { peeks };
}

function readoutCheck(rows: Row[], claim: string, kind: string, window: "12w" | "4w") {
  const flags = rows.filter(r => r.kind === kind &&
    (window === "12w" ? r.ev12 !== undefined : r.ev4 !== undefined))
    .map(r => (window === "12w" ? r.ev12 : r.ev4) === 1);
  const out: Record<number, any> = {};
  READOUT_N.forEach((n, i) => {
    if (flags.length < n) { out[n] = { armed: false }; return; }
    const k = flags.slice(0, n).filter(Boolean).length;
    const upper = READOUT_LL_UPPER[claim];
    const llWin = upper ? k <= READOUT_LL_K[claim][i] : k >= READOUT_LL_K[claim][i];
    const brWin = upper ? k <= READOUT_BR_K[claim][i] : k >= READOUT_BR_K[claim][i];
    out[n] = { armed: true, k, llWin, brWin, both: llWin && brWin };
  });
  return out;
}

function comp(n12: number, k12: number, dirs?: string[]): Row[] {
  return Array.from({ length: n12 }, (_, i) => ({
    kind: "compression_first_event",
    ev12: i < k12 ? 1 : 0,
    dir12: dirs && i < dirs.length ? dirs[i] : undefined,
  }));
}
function stress4(n4: number, k4: number): Row[] {
  return Array.from({ length: n4 }, (_, j) => ({
    kind: "stress_de_stress", ev4: j < k4 ? 1 : 0,
  }));
}

async function main() {
  const checks: { name: string; pass: boolean; detail?: any }[] = [];
  const t = (name: string, pass: boolean, detail?: any) =>
    checks.push({ name, pass, detail });

  // T1 empty log
  const e = occurrenceGates([], "compression_first_event", KILL_LINES);
  t("T1 empty log NOT-ARMED", e.status === "NOT-ARMED");

  // T2 design-truth 60 rows 38 events
  const oc = occurrenceGates(comp(60, 38), "compression_first_event", KILL_LINES);
  t("T2 60/38 ALIVE all gates", oc.status === "ALIVE" && oc.gates[60].k === 38);

  // T3 every compression gate boundary
  for (const n of [10, 20, 40, 60]) {
    const killed = occurrenceGates(comp(n, KILL_LINES[n]), "compression_first_event", KILL_LINES);
    const alive = occurrenceGates(comp(n, KILL_LINES[n] + 1), "compression_first_event", KILL_LINES);
    t(`T3 gate n=${n} K=kill KILLED`, killed.status === "KILLED");
    t(`T3 gate n=${n} K=kill+1 ALIVE`, alive.status === "ALIVE");
  }

  // T4 sticky
  const sticky = occurrenceGates(comp(10, 3).concat(comp(50, 50)),
    "compression_first_event", KILL_LINES);
  t("T4 sticky KILLED after gate-1 kill", sticky.status === "KILLED");

  // T5 stress boundaries
  for (const n of [10, 20, 40, 60]) {
    const killed = occurrenceGates(
      comp(n, STRESS_KILL[n]).map(r => ({ ...r, kind: "stress_de_stress" })),
      "stress_de_stress", STRESS_KILL);
    t(`T5 stress gate n=${n} KILLED`, killed.status === "KILLED");
  }

  // T6 readout boundaries
  let ro = readoutCheck(comp(26, 14), "comp_12w", "compression_first_event", "12w");
  t("T6 comp 26 K=14 br win ll lose", ro[26].brWin && !ro[26].llWin);
  ro = readoutCheck(comp(26, 15), "comp_12w", "compression_first_event", "12w");
  t("T6 comp 26 K=15 both", ro[26].both);
  ro = readoutCheck(comp(104, 56), "comp_12w", "compression_first_event", "12w");
  t("T6 comp 104 K=56 br win ll lose", ro[104].brWin && !ro[104].llWin);
  ro = readoutCheck(comp(104, 57), "comp_12w", "compression_first_event", "12w");
  t("T6 comp 104 K=57 BOTH (positive proof)", ro[104].both);
  ro = readoutCheck(stress4(104, 39), "stress_4w", "stress_de_stress", "4w");
  t("T6 stress4w K=39 both", ro[104].both);
  ro = readoutCheck(stress4(104, 40), "stress_4w", "stress_de_stress", "4w");
  t("T6 stress4w K=40 br win ll lose", ro[104].brWin && !ro[104].llWin);
  ro = readoutCheck(stress4(104, 41), "stress_4w", "stress_de_stress", "4w");
  t("T6 stress4w K=41 both lose", !ro[104].brWin && !ro[104].llWin);

  // T7 direction peeks
  const d27 = directionPeeks(comp(40, 40, Array(27).fill("up").concat(Array(13).fill("down"))));
  t("T7 promote 27/40", d27.peeks[40].verdict === "PROMOTED");
  const d26 = directionPeeks(comp(40, 40, Array(26).fill("up").concat(Array(14).fill("down"))));
  t("T7 no-action 26/40", d26.peeks[40].verdict.startsWith("NO-ACTION"));
  const d13 = directionPeeks(comp(40, 40, Array(13).fill("up").concat(Array(27).fill("down"))));
  t("T7 down-bias 13/40", d13.peeks[40].verdict === "DOWN-BIAS-ANNOTATION");
  const d39 = directionPeeks(comp(60, 60, Array(39).fill("up").concat(Array(21).fill("down"))));
  t("T7 promote 39/60", d39.peeks[60].verdict === "PROMOTED");

  // T8 kind isolation
  const iso = occurrenceGates(comp(20, 20).map(r => ({ ...r, kind: "stress_de_stress" })),
    "compression_first_event", KILL_LINES);
  t("T8 kind isolation", iso.nResolved === 0 && iso.status === "NOT-ARMED");

  // T9 unresolved exclusion: a row with ev12 undefined never counts
  const unres = occurrenceGates([{ kind: "compression_first_event" }],
    "compression_first_event", KILL_LINES);
  t("T9 unresolved excluded", unres.nResolved === 0);

  // T10 threshold tables monotone, win sides
  t("T10 ll thresholds monotone", Object.values(READOUT_LL_K).every(a =>
    a[0] < a[1] && a[1] < a[2]));
  t("T10 win sides", READOUT_LL_UPPER["stress_4w"] === true &&
    READOUT_LL_UPPER["comp_12w"] === false);

  const failed = checks.filter(c => !c.pass);
  return {
    tool: "smh_gates_ts_mirror",
    date: "2026-11-14",
    session: 68,
    fixtures: checks.length,
    passed: checks.length - failed.length,
    failed: failed,
    verdict: failed.length === 0 ? "MIRROR PASS: all boundary fixtures agree" :
      "MIRROR FAIL",
    all_checks: checks,
  };
}

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/smh_gates_ts_mirror.ts

// ---- frozen definitions (smh_hazard_v0.md, unchanged) ----
function main() {
  const rows = payload.split('\n').map(l => {
    const p = l.split(',');
    return { date: p[0], close: parseFloat(p[1]), state: parseInt(p[2]) };
  });
  const n = rows.length;
  const r1: number[] = new Array(n).fill(NaN);
  for (let i = 1; i < n; i++) r1[i] = Math.log(rows[i].close / rows[i-1].close);
  const sigW: number[] = new Array(n).fill(NaN);
  for (let i = 8; i < n; i++) {
    let m = 0; for (let k = i-7; k <= i; k++) m += r1[k];
    m /= 8;
    let s = 0; for (let k = i-7; k <= i; k++) s += (r1[k]-m)**2;
    sigW[i] = Math.sqrt(s/7); // 8-week trailing std of weekly log returns, ddof=1
  }
  const LOW = 0.01739; // frozen pre-2021 low-tercile cutpoint
  const entries: number[] = [];
  for (let i = 9; i < n; i++) {
    if (!isNaN(sigW[i]) && !isNaN(sigW[i-1]) && sigW[i] <= LOW && sigW[i-1] > LOW) entries.push(i);
  }
  // event in week t+1: |r1(t+1)| >= 2.5 * sig_w(t); NaN if sample end / invalid
  const ev = (i: number) => (i+1 < n && !isNaN(r1[i+1]) && !isNaN(sigW[i])) ? (Math.abs(r1[i+1]) >= 2.5*sigW[i] ? 1 : 0) : NaN;

  // ---- textbook KM (the corrected table; v0 kept h=1-event episodes in later risk sets) ----
  const km: any[] = [];
  let done = new Set<number>();
  let cum = 1;
  for (let h = 1; h <= 12; h++) {
    let risk = 0, events = 0;
    for (const t0 of entries) {
      if (done.has(t0)) continue;
      const e = ev(t0+h-1);
      if (isNaN(e)) continue;
      risk++;
      if (e === 1) { events++; done.add(t0); }
    }
    cum *= (1 - events/risk);
    km.push({h, risk, events, hazard: +(events/risk).toFixed(3), cum: +(1-cum).toFixed(3)});
  }
  const h1Episodes = entries.filter(t0 => ev(t0) === 1).map(t0 => rows[t0].date); // expect 2013-03-01, 2014-10-17

  // ---- person-week expansion (KM-consistent risk sets) ----
  const pw: {h:number; y:number; momneg:number; revdn:number}[] = [];
  const ended = new Set<number>();
  for (let h = 1; h <= 12; h++) {
    for (const t0 of entries) {
      if (ended.has(t0)) continue;
      const e = ev(t0+h-1);
      if (isNaN(e)) { ended.add(t0); continue; }
      const mom12 = Math.log(rows[t0].close / rows[t0-12].close);
      pw.push({h, y: e, momneg: mom12 <= 0 ? 1 : 0, revdn: rows[t0].state === 2 ? 1 : 0});
      if (e === 1) ended.add(t0);
    }
  }

  // ---- discrete-time cloglog Cox: log(-log(1-lambda)) = X beta; Newton + numeric Hessian ----
  function fit2(X: number[][], y: number[]) {
    const m = X.length, p = X[0].length;
    const loglik = (b: number[]) => {
      let s = 0;
      for (let k = 0; k < m; k++) {
        const eta = b.reduce((a, bj, j) => a + bj*X[k][j], 0);
        const c = Math.exp(eta);
        const lam = 1 - Math.exp(-c);
        s += y[k]*Math.log(Math.min(Math.max(lam,1e-10),1-1e-10)) + (1-y[k])*Math.log(Math.min(Math.max(1-lam,1e-10),1-1e-10));
      }
      return s;
    };
    let b = new Array(p).fill(0);
    b[0] = Math.log(-Math.log(1-0.07));
    for (let it = 0; it < 300; it++) {
      const g = new Array(p).fill(0);
      for (let k = 0; k < m; k++) {
        const eta = b.reduce((a, bj, j) => a + bj*X[k][j], 0);
        const c = Math.exp(eta);
        const lam = 1 - Math.exp(-c);
        const dl = y[k]*(c*Math.exp(-c)/lam) - (1-y[k])*(c*Math.exp(-c)/(1-lam));
        for (let j = 0; j < p; j++) g[j] += dl*X[k][j];
      }
      const eps = 1e-5;
      const H = Array.from({length:p}, () => new Array(p).fill(0));
      for (let j = 0; j < p; j++) for (let l = 0; l < p; l++) {
        const bpp = b.slice(); bpp[j]+=eps; bpp[l]+=eps;
        const bpm = b.slice(); bpm[j]+=eps; bpm[l]-=eps;
        const bmp = b.slice(); bmp[j]-=eps; bmp[l]+=eps;
        const bmm = b.slice(); bmm[j]-=eps; bmm[l]-=eps;
        H[j][l] = (loglik(bpp)-loglik(bpm)-loglik(bmp)+loglik(bmm))/(4*eps*eps);
      }
      const A = H.map(r => r.slice());
      for (let j = 0; j < p; j++) A[j].push(-g[j]);
      for (let col = 0; col < p; col++) {
        let piv = col;
        for (let r2 = col+1; r2 < p; r2++) if (Math.abs(A[r2][col]) > Math.abs(A[piv][col])) piv = r2;
        [A[col], A[piv]] = [A[piv], A[col]];
        for (let r2 = 0; r2 < p; r2++) {
          if (r2 === col) continue;
          const f = A[r2][col]/A[col][col];
          for (let c2 = col; c2 <= p; c2++) A[r2][c2] -= f*A[col][c2];
        }
      }
      const db = new Array(p);
      for (let j = 0; j < p; j++) db[j] = A[j][p]/A[j][j];
      const old = loglik(b);
      let step = 1;
      let cand = b.map((bj, j) => bj + step*db[j]);
      while (loglik(cand) < old && step > 1e-4) { step /= 2; cand = b.map((bj, j) => bj + step*db[j]); }
      b = cand;
      if (Math.abs(loglik(b) - old) < 1e-10) break;
    }
    const llv = loglik(b);
    const eps = 1e-5;
    const Hf = Array.from({length:p}, () => new Array(p).fill(0));
    for (let j = 0; j < p; j++) for (let l = 0; l < p; l++) {
      const bpp = b.slice(); bpp[j]+=eps; bpp[l]+=eps;
      const bpm = b.slice(); bpm[j]+=eps; bpm[l]-=eps;
      const bmp = b.slice(); bmp[j]-=eps; bmp[l]+=eps;
      const bmm = b.slice(); bmm[j]-=eps; bmm[l]-=eps;
      Hf[j][l] = (loglik(bpp)-loglik(bpm)-loglik(bmp)+loglik(bmm))/(4*eps*eps);
    }
    const A2 = Hf.map(r => r.map(v => -v)); // covariance = inverse of NEGATIVE Hessian
    const aug = A2.map((r2,j) => [...r2, ...Array.from({length:p},(_,k)=> k===j?1:0)]);
    for (let col = 0; col < p; col++) {
      let piv = col;
      for (let r2 = col+1; r2 < p; r2++) if (Math.abs(aug[r2][col]) > Math.abs(aug[piv][col])) piv = r2;
      [aug[col], aug[piv]] = [aug[piv], aug[col]];
      const d = aug[col][col];
      for (let c2 = col; c2 < 2*p; c2++) aug[col][c2] /= d;
      for (let r2 = 0; r2 < p; r2++) {
        if (r2 === col) continue;
        const f = aug[r2][col];
        for (let c2 = col; c2 < 2*p; c2++) aug[r2][c2] -= f*aug[col][c2];
      }
    }
    const se = Array.from({length:p}, (_,j) => Math.sqrt(Math.max(aug[j][p+j], 0)));
    return {beta: b.map(v=>+v.toFixed(4)), se_naive: se.map(v=>+v.toFixed(4)), z_naive: b.map((bj,j)=>+(bj/se[j]).toFixed(2)), loglik: +llv.toFixed(4)};
  }

  const y = pw.map(r => r.y);
  const M00 = fit2(pw.map(r => [1]), y);
  const M0 = fit2(pw.map(r => [1, r.h]), y);
  const M1 = fit2(pw.map(r => [1, r.h, r.momneg]), y);
  const M2 = fit2(pw.map(r => [1, r.h, r.revdn]), y);
  const M3 = fit2(pw.map(r => [1, r.h, r.momneg, r.revdn]), y);
  const names = [['intercept'],['intercept','h'],['intercept','h','mom12neg'],['intercept','h','rev_down'],['intercept','h','mom12neg','rev_down']];

  // sanity: intercept-only fitted hazard must equal pooled event rate
  const pooledRate = +(y.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/y.length).toFixed(4);
  const fittedConst = +(1 - Math.exp(-Math.exp(M00.beta[0]))).toFixed(4);

  return {
    n_entries: entries.length,
    h1_event_episodes: h1Episodes,
    km_textbook: km,
    person_weeks: pw.length,
    events: y.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0),
    sanity_pooled_vs_fitted: [pooledRate, fittedConst],
    models: [M00,M0,M1,M2,M3].map((M,i) => ({ names: names[i], ...M })),
    lr_duration_vs_const: +(2*(M0.loglik-M00.loglik)).toFixed(4),
    lr_mom12neg_vs_base: +(2*(M1.loglik-M0.loglik)).toFixed(4),
    lr_revdown_vs_base: +(2*(M2.loglik-M0.loglik)).toFixed(4),
    lr_full_vs_base: +(2*(M3.loglik-M0.loglik)).toFixed(4),
  };
}
console.log(JSON.stringify(main(), null, 1));

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/smh_hazard_cox_v01.ts

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/smh_horizon_ts_mirror.ts (29531 bytes)
// smh_horizon_ts_mirror.ts - SMH observation-horizon grid, TypeScript mirror
// Session 60 (12 Nov 2026). Unsealed pre-2021 design bookkeeping only.
// Conventions mirrored 1:1 from smh_forecast.py/smh_theorem.py:
// sig_w = 8-week rolling sample std (ddof=1) of weekly log returns incl current week;
// ev_next[t] = |r1[t+1]| >= 2.5*sig_w[t]; entries = sig_w<=LOW cross with valid sig_w;
// person-weeks h=1..H with t0+h <= len-2, break on NaN ev_next or event;
// KM per-h (at-risk, events); Greenwood cum and SE; matched climatology from all sig_w-valid weeks.
const DATES: string[] = [
  "2000-09-22", "2000-09-29", "2000-10-06", "2000-10-13", "2000-10-20", "2000-10-27", 
  "2000-11-03", "2000-11-10", "2000-11-17", "2000-11-24", "2000-12-01", "2000-12-08", 
  "2000-12-15", "2000-12-22", "2000-12-29", "2001-01-05", "2001-01-12", "2001-01-19", 
  "2001-01-26", "2001-02-02", "2001-02-09", "2001-02-16", "2001-02-23", "2001-03-02", 
  "2001-03-09", "2001-03-16", "2001-03-23", "2001-03-30", "2001-04-06", "2001-04-13", 
  "2001-04-20", "2001-04-27", "2001-05-04", "2001-05-11", "2001-05-18", "2001-05-25", 
  "2001-06-01", "2001-06-08", "2001-06-15", "2001-06-22", "2001-06-29", "2001-07-06",

  11133.75, 11562, 11991.75, 11548.75, 11061.5, 11144.80957, 11136.5, 11233.25, 11724.75, 
  11798, 11663.5, 11046.25, 12075, 11933.5, 11905.75, 12257.5, 12526, 12368, 12800.05957, 
  12704.5,

const LOW = 0.01739;
const Z95 = 1.959963985;

function median(xs: number[]): number {
  const s = [...xs].sort((a, b) => a - b);
  const n = s.length;
  return n % 2 ? s[(n - 1) / 2] : 0.5 * (s[n / 2 - 1] + s[n / 2]);
}

// series
const T = CLOSE.length;
const r1: number[] = new Array(T).fill(NaN);
for (let t = 1; t < T; t++) r1[t] = Math.log(CLOSE[t] / CLOSE[t - 1]);
const sigW: number[] = new Array(T).fill(NaN);
for (let t = 7; t < T; t++) {
  let m = 0;
  for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) m += r1[t - 7 + k];
  m /= 8;
  let v = 0;
  for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) { const d = r1[t - 7 + k] - m; v += d * d; }
  sigW[t] = Math.sqrt(v / 7);
}
const evNext: number[] = new Array(T).fill(NaN);
for (let t = 0; t < T - 1; t++) {
  if (!isNaN(sigW[t]) && !isNaN(r1[t + 1]))
    evNext[t] = Math.abs(r1[t + 1]) >= 2.5 * sigW[t] ? 1 : 0;
}

function entries(): number[] {
  const out: number[] = [];
  for (let t = 0; t < T; t++) {
    if (isNaN(sigW[t])) continue;
    const inLow = sigW[t] <= LOW;
    const prevLow = t > 0 && !isNaN(sigW[t - 1]) && sigW[t - 1] <= LOW;
    if (inLow && !prevLow) out.push(t);
  }
  return out;
}

function personWeeks(idx: number[], H: number): { ep: number; h: number; e: number }[] {
  const rows: { ep: number; h: number; e: number }[] = [];
  idx.forEach((t0, ep) => {
    for (let h = 1; h <= H; h++) {
      if (t0 + h > T - 2) break;
      const e = evNext[t0 + h - 1];
      if (isNaN(e)) break;
      rows.push({ ep, h, e });
      if (e > 0) break;
    }
  });
  return rows;
}

function kmGreenwood(pw: { ep: number; h: number; e: number }[], H: number) {
  const km: Record<number, [number, number]> = {};
  for (let h = 1; h <= H; h++) {
    const sub = pw.filter(r => r.h === h);
    km[h] = [sub.length, sub.filter(r => r.e > 0).length];
  }
  let S = 1, acc = 0;
  const cum: Record<number, number> = {};
  for (let h = 1; h <= H; h++) {
    const [n, d] = km[h];
    if (n > 0) {
      S *= 1 - d / n;
      if (d > 0 && n - d > 0) acc += d / (n * (n - d));
    }
    cum[h] = 1 - S;
  }
  const se = Math.sqrt(S * S * acc);
  const band: [number, number] = [Math.max(0, cum[H] - Z95 * se), Math.min(1, cum[H] + Z95 * se)];
  return { km, cum, cumH: cum[H], band, se, personWeeks: pw.length,
           events: new Set(pw.filter(r => r.e > 0).map(r => r.ep)).size,
           lags: pw.filter(r => r.e > 0).map(r => r.h) };
}

function climIdx(): number[] {
  const out: number[] = [];
  for (let t = 0; t < T; t++) if (!isNaN(sigW[t])) out.push(t);
  return out;
}

function cell(H: number) {
  const e = kmGreenwood(personWeeks(ENTRIES, H), H);
  const c = kmGreenwood(personWeeks(CLIM, H), H);
  return {
    H,
    entries: ENTRIES.length,
    person_weeks: e.personWeeks,
    events_within_H: e.events,
    cum_H: +e.cumH.toFixed(4),
    band: [+e.band[0].toFixed(3), +e.band[1].toFixed(3)],
    median_lag: median(e.lags),
    clim_person_weeks: c.personWeeks,
    clim_cum_H: +c.cumH.toFixed(4),
    clim_band: [+c.band[0].toFixed(3), +c.band[1].toFixed(3)],
    gap: +(e.cumH - c.cumH).toFixed(4),
    disjoint: e.band[0] > c.band[1],
  };
}

const ENTRIES = entries();
const CLIM = climIdx();

// ANCHOR: the frozen H=12 cell must reproduce the frozen constants first
const anchor = cell(12);
const anchorChecks: Record<string, boolean> = {
  n_entries_41: ENTRIES.length === 41,
  person_weeks_327: anchor.person_weeks === 327,
  events_26: anchor.events_within_H === 26,
  cum12_0634: Math.abs(anchor.cum_H - 0.634) < 0.0005,
  band_frozen: Math.abs(anchor.band[0] - 0.487) < 0.002 && Math.abs(anchor.band[1] - 0.782) < 0.002,
  clim12_0441: Math.abs(anchor.clim_cum_H - 0.441) < 0.0005,
  clim_band_frozen: Math.abs(anchor.clim_band[0] - 0.411) < 0.002 && Math.abs(anchor.clim_band[1] - 0.471) < 0.002,
  median_lag_5: anchor.median_lag === 5,
  clim_pool_1050: CLIM.length === 1050,
  cum4_0268: Math.abs(cumAt(4, ENTRIES) - 0.268) < 0.0005,
};

// cum at h=4 and h=6 (capture-ladder anchors) via a second pass
function cumAt(H: number, idx: number[]): number {
  return kmGreenwood(personWeeks(idx, H), H).cumH;
}

const grid = [8, 10, 12, 16, 20].map(H => ({
  ...cell(H),
  cum4_entry: +cumAt(4, ENTRIES).toFixed(4),
}));
// anchor cum4 check
const a4 = grid.find(g => g.H === 12)!;

function main() {
  const failed = Object.entries(anchorChecks).filter(([, v]) => !v).map(([k]) => k);
  if (failed.length) throw new Error("ANCHOR FAILED: " + failed.join(", "));
  const rep = {
    session: 60,
    date: "2026-11-12",
    kind: "smh_horizon_grid (observation-horizon definition-elasticity audit)",
    window: "unsealed pre-2021 design bookkeeping only (date < 2021-01-01)",
    weeks: T,
    span: [DATES[0], DATES[T - 1]],
    anchor_cum4_entry: a4.cum4_entry,
    anchor: "H=12 reproduces the frozen constants (41 entries, 327 person-weeks, cum12 0.634 [0.487,0.782], clim 0.441 [0.411,0.471], median lag 5, clim pool 1050)",
    grid,
  };
  console.log(JSON.stringify(rep, null, 2));
  return rep;
}
